## Part 1 - Surface maps (6)

### explorer_sa3: Latent lab (bend_tab.py), Viewer (viewer_tab.py), Dataset (dataset_tab.py), Analysis (analysis_tab.py + analysis.py), their data layer (sidecar_index.py, latents.py, scalar_cache.py, audio_panel.py, player_client.py), plus UI_BRIEF.md pain points/constraints and README.md two-process architecture. Callbacks for Viewer/Dataset/Analysis live in callbacks.py (read for the ID contract); bend owns its own callbacks.

These four tabs are the "data" half of the Dash explorer: three read-only analysis surfaces over the latent sidecar corpus plus one experimental write surface (Latent lab) that POSTs to the render server. Viewer is a track -> crop browser showing a 256xT latent heatmap, a per-frame timeseries chart, and a two-player audio panel (server /decode reconstruction next to /source original). Dataset is a corpus-wide scatter whose axis options are three CropMeta scalars plus one lazily-computed per-crop mean per 1-D TIMESERIES field, materialised by a background thread into latent_dir/_ts_scalar_cache.npz and polled by a dcc.Interval. Analysis holds three unrelated views sharing one Recompute button: a 256x256 latent-channel cross-correlation heatmap over a sampled subset, a per-dim Pearson-r bar chart against one 1-D feature, and Kim's co-activation view (standardized dim series x standardized feature series, pointwise product, rows sorted by |global r| from a precomputed CSV, feature curve on a zoom-linked shared x-axis). Latent lab picks a crop (or a free .npy path that wins) and stacks up to four ordered bend ops driven by ONE seed, sends {ops, seed, latent_dir, crop_id|latent_path} to POST /bend, and A/Bs the returned decode against the crop's clean decode + source; a browser-side history Store keeps the last 50 recipes with their players. Important for a rewrite: UI_BRIEF.md's one-paragraph descriptions of Viewer, Dataset and Analysis are materially wrong about what the code actually does (details in hazards).

**API calls**

- POST /bend (render_client.bend, timeout 600 s) — payload keys: ops [{op, amount, ...op-specific keys merged from the extra-JSON field}], seed (int, -1 = random), latent_dir (str, client-side path), and exactly one of crop_id or latent_path. Response keys read by the UI: urls [rel paths], files [names], meta, seed (echoed, authoritative), timings.total_sec.
- GET /status (render_client.status, timeout 1 s, polled every 2 s by bend-interval) — response keys read: busy, job_id. None on any exception = server down.
- GET /audio/<job>/<file>.wav — via render_client.audio_url(rel) = BASE + rel, used as html.Audio src for both live and history bend results.
- GET /decode?crop=<crop_id> (player_client.decode_url) — SAME-L reconstruction, html.Audio src in audio_panel.panel.
- GET /source?crop=<crop_id> (player_client.source_url) — original audio slice, second html.Audio in audio_panel.panel. README: needs the removable Mantu drive mounted, else a clear 500 while other endpoints keep working.
- GET /status (player_client.status, urlopen, timeout 0.5 s) — boolean reachability gate for the audio panel; called synchronously on every crop change in both Viewer and Latent lab.
- Defined in player_client but UNUSED by these four tabs: GET /mix?crop_a=&crop_b=&t=&interp=slerp (mix_url) and GET /steer?crop=&head=&gain= (steer_url, default gain 48.0; README notes SA3-medium LatCH wants gain 48-96, not the Small default of 8, and that the ONNX player returns 501 for /steer).
- Defined in render_client and used by the other tabs, listed here because a rewrite shares the client: GET /info (memoized), GET /ckpts, GET /roots, GET /models, GET /slots, POST /slots, POST /ab, GET /presets, GET /presets/<name>, POST /presets, POST /schedule, POST /generate, POST /a2a_track, POST /a2a_mix, POST /decode, POST /longform. render_client._OPS = {generate, a2a_track, a2a_mix, decode, longform, bend} gates the render() helper.
- Base URL resolution: SA3_RENDER_BASE env > [render_server] base_url in latent_player_sa3.ini > http://localhost:8056. Player side: SA3_PLAYER_BASE env > http://localhost:${SA3_PLAYER_PORT:-8056}. latent_dir comes from [server] latent_dir in the same ini.

**Behaviors to preserve**

- Bend seed grammar: one seeded RNG for the whole stack, consumed in row order. Reproducibility is (ops list, seed) as a pair — the same ops in a different row order give a different result at the same seed. -1 is documented as 'random' and the server echoes the seed it actually used back in resp['seed'], which the UI writes into both the status line and the history label, so a random roll stays reproducible afterwards.
- Bend op grammar is deliberately half-structured: `op` + `amount` are first-class controls, every other parameter is free-form JSON merged into the spec dict. The UI validates only that it parses and is an object; unknown keys go straight to the server. Row order is the apply order.
- Empty op rows are skipped silently (op in None/''/'none'). If no row has an op the render aborts client-side with 'error: no op rows active' — the server is never called.
- Source selection precedence in Latent lab: non-empty bend-latent-path wins over bend-crop-dd; if both are empty the render aborts with 'error: pick a crop or enter a latent path'. Exactly one of latent_path / crop_id is ever sent.
- Track -> crop dropdowns (both Viewer and Latent lab) auto-select the first crop by rel_pos so plots/players populate on a single interaction. Crops within a track are always ordered by relative_position_start, not by id.
- Viewer's timeseries dropdown preserves the user's chosen feature across crop changes when that feature exists in the new crop, otherwise it silently falls back to the first field.
- Viewer's latent heatmap draws a dashed vline at the padding boundary derived from sum(padding_mask); if the sidecar has no padding_mask, content_frames returns the full 4096 and no line is drawn.
- Every graph in the Viewer starts as a labelled placeholder_figure with axes hidden and grey annotation text, rather than an empty plane — a small but deliberate empty-state convention worth carrying over.
- Dataset feature list grows underneath the user: the interval callback rewrites the x/y option lists every second while the background cache computes, then latches the interval off. Selections made before the cache is ready survive as values but the label set changes.
- The scalar cache is persistent across runs (latent_dir/_ts_scalar_cache.npz) and is only reused when its stored _ids list matches the freshly scanned index EXACTLY — one added or removed crop discards the whole cache and re-walks every sidecar.
- Analysis is entirely click-to-compute: nothing renders until Recompute is pressed, which simultaneously fills sa3-feat-dd (whose default value then triggers a second heavy computation) and the co-activation dropdowns.
- Co-activation row ordering comes from a precomputed global table (stats/latent_dim_feature_xcorr.csv, 999 crops), not from the displayed crop — so the dim ordering is stable across crops, which is the point of the view. Without the CSV it falls back to arange(0..top_n), losing the sort.
- Both the Viewer audio panel and the Latent lab source panel do a blocking player reachability check (urlopen /status, 0.5 s) inside the callback on every crop change, and render a launch-command block instead of players when it fails.
- audio_panel intentionally shows decode-vs-source side by side — the encoder-quality A/B is a first-class workflow, and Latent lab reuses it as the 'clean' arm against the bent render below.
- render_client.info() memoizes /info for the process lifetime; render_client.schedule() memoizes per (steps, duration, dist_shift, sigma_max) with a 256-entry cache flush. Restarting the server does not invalidate either.

**Rewrite hazards**

- UI_BRIEF.md misdescribes three of the four tabs. Viewer is NOT a '2D scatter of the track's latent embedding from precomputed projections' — it is a 256xT latent heatmap plus a per-frame timeseries chart. Dataset's 'point-click -> play that crop' does NOT exist (no clickData callback anywhere). Analysis's 'latent-dim x feature cross-correlation heatmap (256 dims x ~20 features)' does not exist either — the heatmap is 256x256 dim-vs-dim, and the dim-vs-feature view is a 1-D bar chart. Building to the brief would ship features that were never there and drop features that were.
- The Analysis scope selector (sa3-analysis-n) only feeds the xcorr heatmap. sa3-featcorr-graph hardcodes sample_ids(index, 400) and the co-activation view is per-crop. A rewrite that wires one global scope control to all three changes behaviour and cost.
- 'ALL' scope = 0 -> len(index) and then loads every latent fully into RAM as float32: [256, 4096] float32 is 4 MB per crop, so ~5400 crops is ~21 GB before np.concatenate doubles it in _stack_frames (which also upcasts to float64). This will OOM. There is no chunking, no memmap, no progress feedback beyond dcc.Loading.
- sample_ids uses a FIXED seed 0, so 'random sample' is the same sample every time; and _featcorr re-derives the same sample and reloads all 400 latents from disk on every feature-dropdown change. Nothing is cached between callbacks.
- analysis_tab's co-activation callbacks are registered by a second entry point (analysis_tab.register_coact, called from the top of callbacks.register) rather than living with the rest. Easy to lose when splitting files.
- stats/latent_dim_feature_xcorr.csv is resolved as Path(__file__).parents[2]/'stats'/... — a repo-relative path outside the package. If it moves or is absent, dim_corr_table() stays empty, sa3-coact-feat has zero options, and the co-activation view is silently dead with no error message.
- The in-file comment at analysis_tab.py:117-118 records a real past outage: the callback used c.stem (Path API) on CropMeta objects and 500'd. The index element type is a load-bearing, easily-broken assumption.
- bend_tab duplicates group_by_track(index) and the entire track/crop option-building logic from callbacks.py. Two copies of the same list, two callbacks with identical bodies — refactoring one without the other diverges the two tabs.
- The bend payload sends latent_dir (the VIEWER's local path, from latent_player_sa3.ini) to the server. This only works because both processes share a filesystem. Any client/server split across machines silently breaks bend but not the GET endpoints.
- build_ops consumes a flat positional tuple, slicing rows[i*3:(i+1)*3] and unpacking as (op, amount, extra). That order is defined only by the nested State list-comprehension order in the callback decorator ('op','amount','extra'). Reordering those State entries, or adding a per-row control, corrupts every op silently rather than erroring.
- bend-src-panel returns no_update on a falsy crop, so clearing the crop leaves the previous crop's A/B players in place — the reference audio can silently belong to a different latent than the one being bent, especially once bend-latent-path overrides the crop.
- bend history lives only in a dcc.Store (browser memory, cap 50) and its urls point at server-side /audio/<job>/*.wav. A page reload loses the recipes; a server restart or job cleanup leaves dead players with live-looking labels.
- sa3-ds-interval sets disabled=True permanently once the cache is ready. There is no way to force a recompute if the sidecars change; the only invalidation is the _ids mismatch check at load time.
- The ScalarCache background thread swallows every per-crop exception (bare `except Exception: pass`) — corrupt sidecars become NaN and those crops just vanish from the scatter with no count or warning. The npz save is likewise silently best-effort.
- Viewer's _show does json.loads + np.load with no try/except; a missing .json, missing .TIMESERIES.npz, or a latent whose shape[0] != 256 raises straight out of the callback into a Dash 500. Same for the co-activation _draw (np.load on a constructed f-string path).
- Viewer's sa3-ts-dd offers ALL npz fields including 2-D ones, and timeseries_figure quietly switches to a heatmap of ts.T with a frame-index x-axis (no seconds) — the same dropdown produces two incompatible chart semantics. Analysis, by contrast, filters to 1-D via oned_feature_names.
- Port and endpoint drift between the docs and the code: README documents the player on :7892 with GET /decode /source /mix /steer /crops /meta, but player_client.BASE now defaults to :8056 (those GETs were folded onto the render server on 2026-08-25) and app.run uses port 8051 while UI_BRIEF says 8050. README's 'Running it' section and endpoint table are stale.
- player_client and audio_panel hardcode Linux paths (/home/kim/Projects/...) in the offline launch hints, and README assumes AMD/MIGraphX. Cross-platform packaging needs these externalised.
- UI_BRIEF hard constraint 3 (shared-playhead result players) is NOT implemented on this side — bend's _players are plain html.Audio. If that behaviour is meant to be global in the rewrite, Latent lab is a new implementation, not a port.
- render_client.status() and .bend() are blocking `requests` calls inside Dash callbacks (1 s and 600 s timeouts). bend-interval fires a status poll every 2 s per open tab. Any rewrite with a real event loop should make these async; a 600 s synchronous render is the single biggest UX liability on this tab.
- There is no assets/ directory: the app runs on Dash default light styling, and UI_BRIEF's dark-theme requirement is unimplemented ground-up work, not a restyle.

**Open questions**

- Does the /bend server actually treat seed=-1 as 'draw a random seed and echo it', or is that only a UI label? The UI reads resp['seed'] unconditionally, so the round-trip is assumed but not verifiable without SAO/eval/latent_bend.py.
- What is the full BEND_OPS set server-side? The UI offers six and explicitly drops `splice` (needs a second latent). Should the rewrite expose splice with a second latent picker, which is a natural fit for a two-clip timeline?
- Are the per-op extra keys stable enough to become real typed controls (bits, seg, k, shift/max_shift, channels[])? The free-JSON field with a tooltip is the single clearest candidate for structured per-op forms in a rewrite, but the authoritative key list lives in latent_bend.py, which is not visible here.
- band_scale's tooltip points at latent_dim_feature_xcorr.csv for picking target channels, and analysis_tab already parses that CSV. Should the rewrite let the user pick a FEATURE and auto-fill the top-|r| channel list — i.e. wire Analysis to Latent lab directly?
- Was Dataset click-to-play removed or never built? The brief asserts it and the scatter already carries per-point hover text but no customdata crop id — adding it needs the crop id threaded into the trace.
- Does the brief's '2D scatter of the track's latent embedding (coordinates from precomputed projections)' refer to a projection artifact that exists somewhere and was never wired up, or to a different/older tool? analysis.pca_frames() computes PCA components but no callback calls it.
- Where does the render server write /audio/<job>/ files and what is the retention policy? History entries become dead links at some point and the UI has no way to tell.
- Does bend re-encode or operate purely on the stored latent? Given the measured non-translation-invariance of SAME latents, ops like channel_roll and segment_shuffle move content in ways the decoder was never trained on — worth confirming whether that is the intent (databending as an effect) or a latent bug the user would want flagged.
- Is the shared-playhead result-player behaviour meant to become global in SA3 Studio (including bend and the viewer A/B pair), or does it stay specific to the Inference/A2A result lists?
- How large is the real index? app.py title says len(index) crops; the brief says ~2,676 tracks; analysis_tab caps the co-activation crop dropdown at index[:4000] and the scope dropdown says 'ALL 5400'. The three numbers should be reconciled before sizing any list virtualization.

### Shared steering panel — `C:\Users\kim.ake\OneDrive - Bluefors\Documents\py\mir-feature-extraction\plots\explorer_sa3\controls.py` (478 lines), instantiated twice: `steering_panel("inf", dora_default="none")` on the Inference tab (inference_tab.py:224) and `steering_panel("a2a", dora_default="evr1x")` on the A2A tab (a2a_tab.py:213).

controls.py is a prefix-parameterized Dash sub-layout that renders the LatCH / FiLM / DoRA steering block once per tab under a namespace `ns` ("inf" or "a2a"), so every id is `{ns}-ctl-*`. It exposes three functions that form a positional contract: `steering_panel(ns, dora_default, collapsed)` builds the layout, `steering_states(ns)` returns a fixed-order list of Dash `State`s, and `steering_payload(vals)` slices that list by index into the `{latch: [...], film: {...}, dora: {...}}` JSON blocks plus top-level `rho/mu/gamma/n_iter` accepted by /generate, /a2a_track, /a2a_mix and /longform. THE "23 STATES" CLAIM IS STALE — the module docstring (line 5) still says 23, but `steering_states` builds 35 and `steering_payload` hard-raises `ValueError` on anything but 35 (lines 178-180). The panel is not three independently collapsible groups: it is ONE `html.Details` (line 147, summary "Steering (LatCH / FiLM / DoRA)", `open=not collapsed`, default collapsed) containing four flat rows — 3 LatCH slot rows, a FiLM row, a DoRA row, and an undocumented fourth "LatCH adv" row (rho / mu / gamma / n_iter). Beyond layout it also owns three server-fed pickers driven by a shared 5-second `dcc.Interval`, and a per-head autofill callback that rewrites 17 properties of a slot when its head dropdown changes.

**Controls (26)**

| id | label | kind | payload field | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-head` | LatCH {i} | dcc.Dropdown (clearable=True, placeholder "head (off)", width 260px) | latch[].head | i in 1..3 (controls.LATCH_SLOTS=3). options=[] at build time; filled by _fill_options from GET /info `latch_heads` as {label: "{name} [{family}]", value: name}. When /info is unreachable options stay [] and placeholder becomes "render server :8056 unreachable -- retrying every 5 s" (retries because the short-circuit is `if current`). Value in (None,"none","") => the WHOLE slot is dropped from the payload (controls.py:184). State index 0/8/16. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-kind` | (no text label; second dropdown in the slot row) | dcc.Dropdown (clearable=False, width 130px) | latch[].kind | Static options _KIND_OPTIONS = ["constant","ramp_up","ramp_down","beat_grid"], default "constant". Both options and value are OVERWRITTEN by _autofill_defaults from the head's `supports_kinds` and `target_kind_default`; `disabled` is set to `not supports_scalar_target`. Payload falls back to "constant" if falsy. State index 1/9/17. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-vlabel` | value | html.Span (display-only) |  | Its `title` (hover tooltip) is an Output of _autofill_defaults and carries the head-specific guidance: dataset mean/sigma, "type RAW units, the server standardises", and the out-of-distribution warning. MUST NOT be added to steering_states (explicit comment at controls.py:77). |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-value` | value | dcc.Input type=number, width 90px | latch[].value | Build-time default -30.0, step 0.1, no min/max. _autofill_defaults overwrites value (head `value_default`), min (`slider_min`), max (`slider_max`), step (round(max((hi-lo)/200, 1e-4), 6), else 0.1) and disabled (`not supports_scalar_target` — vector-output heads cannot take a scalar target). Payload fallback -30.0 when None. State index 2/10/18. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-gain` | gain | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, step=1, width 90px | latch[].gain | Build-time default 512.0; overwritten by the head's `default_gain`. SEMANTICS ARE RELATIVE, NOT ABSOLUTE: the server (resolve_latch:514-517) sets rho = mu = slot-1 gain and rescales every slot to slot_gain / slot1_gain, so a weight copied from another tool will not reproduce. Typical auto values: 512 for medium heads, 2048 for chroma_other. Payload fallback 512.0. State index 3/11/19. On the inf tab this id is also an allow_duplicate Output of bracket recall. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-start` | start | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, max=1, step=0.01, width 90px | latch[].start_pct | Default 0.0. Tooltip (_WINDOW_HELP) is load-bearing measured knowledge: start/end are FRACTIONS OF SAMPLING STEPS, and delivered guidance is NOT proportional to window width — per-step strength is weighted by (1-sigma)/sum(1-sigma) (latch_guided.py:117,138). Measured on a 24-step schedule: first 20% of steps carry 0.2% of the budget, first 60% carry 9.3%, last 20% carry ~62%. Payload fallback 0.0. State index 4/12/20. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-end` | end | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, max=1, step=0.01, width 90px | latch[].end_pct | Default 0.6 — which per the tooltip delivers under a tenth of the available guidance; widening 0.6 -> 0.8 roughly quadruples it. Advice encoded in the UI: if a head seems dead, widen the window toward 1.0 BEFORE raising gain. Payload fallback 0.6. State index 5/13/21. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-llabel` | loss | html.Span (display-only) |  | `title` is an Output of _autofill_defaults; text differs depending on whether the head supports loss selection ("trained with X, empty uses it" vs "disabled: that is the objective, not a preference"). |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-loss` | loss | dcc.Dropdown (clearable=False, placeholder "default", width 130px) | latch[].loss_type | _LOSS_OPTIONS = ["", "mse", "smooth_l1", "l1", "huber", "bce_logits", "cosine", "scalar_pooled", "chroma_rung1", "chroma_rung2"]. "" means head/server default and the key is OMITTED from the payload entirely (only emitted `if loss:`). _autofill_defaults sets value to the head's own `loss_type` and disabled = `not supports_loss_select`. scalar_pooled is the 2026-07-10 buzz fix for constant targets on scalar heads; chroma_rung1/2 are T2. State index 6/14/22. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-wsec` | w_sec | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, step=0.1, placeholder "def", width 90px | latch[].w_sec | Default None; key omitted from payload unless not None. This is the ONE slot field _autofill_defaults never writes. State index 7/15/23. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-health` | (badge at end of slot row) | html.Span (display-only) |  | children/style/title all Outputs of _autofill_defaults. Empty when health=="ok", else "WARN {health}"; appends "readout: unrecorded" when `readout_source == "not-recorded"`. Colour map _HEALTH_COLOR: ok #3a3, undertrained #c60, unstable #c00, unknown #888. Tooltip carries `health_reason` plus a note that train_latch.py only began saving chroma_key/target_source on 2026-08-24. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-meter` | (full-width caption under the slot row) | html.Div (display-only, 11px, #888) |  | Three variants: dataset mean +/- sigma with the slider range; an N-channel-head refusal message; or 'no dataset statistics in this checkpoint'. LOAD-BEARING BEYOND DISPLAY: inference_tab._preset_apply_slots is triggered by Input("inf-ctl-latch{i}-meter","children") specifically to sequence itself AFTER _autofill_defaults. |
| `{ns}-ctl-film-enable` | FiLM density adapter | dcc.Checklist, single option value "on" | film (gate) | Default [] (off). The whole `film` block is None unless "on" in the value. Note the real label is "FiLM density adapter", not "control adapter". State index 24. |
| `{ns}-ctl-film-value` | onsets/s | dcc.Input type=number, step=0.1, width 90px | film.value | Default 4.0, no min/max clamp. Payload fallback 4.0. State index 25. |
| `{ns}-ctl-film-gain` | gain | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, max=5, step=0.05, width 90px | film.gain | Default 1.75. Payload fallback 1.75. State index 26. |
| `(no control) film.ckpt` | — | implicit, resolved at payload-build time | film.ckpt | NOT user-selectable. steering_payload calls render_client.info() and reads `film_default.ckpt`; if /info is unreachable it falls back to _FILM_CKPT_FALLBACK = <Mantu>/sa3_control_runs/onset_Fusion_lr1e-4_randomcrop/riffer_final.pt, where <Mantu> is resolved at IMPORT time by _resolve_mantu() probing /run/media/kim/Mantu then /run/media/kim/Mantu1. A rewrite must expose this as a real control or replicate the resolution. |
| `{ns}-ctl-dora-dd` | DoRA | dcc.Dropdown (clearable=False, width 160px) | dora.name OR dora.ckpt_path | Default differs per tab: "none" on inf, "evr1x" on a2a (the `dora_default` arg). Base options _DORA_OPTIONS = ["none","hof","newstack","evr1x"]; _fill_dora_options appends every loadable adapter from GET /models?family=adapter&loadable=true, with option value = the adapter's PATH and label "{label} · r{rank}  [{root_id}]". Adapters on unmounted removable drives are kept but labelled "— drive offline", disabled=True, and sorted LAST (692 such entries measured 2026-08-26 with Mantu unplugged). Dispatch in steering_payload: value "none" -> {"name":"none", strength} sent EXPLICITLY (an absent dora block makes the server use the op default, evr1x for /a2a_mix, silently overriding the user); value starting with "/" -> {"name": None, "ckpt_path": value}; any other -> {"name": value, "ckpt_path": None}. State index 27. |
| `{ns}-ctl-dora-strength` | strength | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, max=10, step=0.01, width 90px | dora.strength | Default 1.0, fallback 1.0. Sent even when name=="none" because a top-level ckpt_path folds into the same dict server-side (resolve_dora_req) and honours dora.strength. On the inf tab this is an allow_duplicate Output of bracket._recall (bracket.py:323). State index 28. |
| `{ns}-ctl-dora-imin` | sigma-interval (first box) | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, max=1, step=0.05, width 90px | dora.interval_min | Default 0.0. Sigma-native interval gating (dit.py:466) — e.g. (0.25, 1.0) disables the adapter for the final low-noise detail steps. Only attached when a dora dict exists at all. State index 29. |
| `{ns}-ctl-dora-imax` | sigma-interval (second box) | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, max=1, step=0.05, width 90px | dora.interval_max | Default 1.0. State index 30. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch-rho` | rho (LatCH adv) | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, step=1, placeholder "auto", width 90px | rho (TOP-LEVEL, not inside latch) | Default None = auto = slot-1 gain. Key omitted from payload when None. Tooltip says LEAVE EMPTY; override only to decouple step size from slot balance, and move by factors of 2. State index 31. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch-mu` | mu (LatCH adv) | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, step=1, placeholder "auto", width 90px | mu (top-level) | Default None, auto-tied to rho (both default to slot-1 gain). Raising mu above rho pulls toward the current latent (guidance bites less); lowering it lets guidance dominate and is where artefacts start. Key omitted when None. State index 32. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch-gamma` | gamma (LatCH adv) | dcc.Input type=number, min=0, max=1, step=0.05, width 90px | gamma (top-level) | Default 0.3 — non-None, so this key is ALWAYS emitted. Noise added to the latent before each head evaluation (z_in = z0 + gamma*eps, latch_guided.py:179). Higher gamma = deliberately less precise = safer (Pons et al. 2603.04366). Measured caveat 2026-08-28: raising 0.3 -> 0.6 on an overdriven narrow-window render changed nothing (crest 2.21 -> 2.22, bass 0.293 -> 0.292) because gamma blurs the head INPUT and does not shrink the STEP. REPRODUCIBILITY: gamma=0 makes the render bit-reproducible; any gamma>0 makes head evaluation stochastic so a fixed seed no longer yields an identical file. State index 33. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch-niter` | n_iter (LatCH adv) | dcc.Input type=number, min=1, max=16, step=1, width 90px | n_iter (top-level, cast to int) | Default 4 — non-None, so ALWAYS emitted. Not purely a cost knob: each iteration takes a full mu_t gradient step on the re-evaluated z0 (latch_guided.py:176-186), so it raises adherence too. 2 for a quick look, 4 to work, 8 when a target is ignored. The paper's TFG also has an N_recur; only N_iter is implemented. State index 34. |
| `{ns}-ctl-info-poll` | (invisible) | dcc.Interval, interval=5000ms, max_intervals=-1 |  | Not a user control but a required part of the surface. Every picker in the panel fills off this rather than the once-per-load Input(<id>,"id") pattern, because with :8056 down at page load the old pattern returned [] and never retried. On the inf tab this same interval is ALSO the trigger for inf-root-dd, inf-ckpt-dd and other non-panel pickers (inference_tab.py:507, 528, 602, 699, 720) — deleting or renaming it breaks unrelated tab machinery. |
| `(container) html.Details` | Steering (LatCH / FiLM / DoRA) | html.Details / html.Summary |  | ONE collapsible wrapper around all four rows, `open=not collapsed`, collapsed=True by default on both tabs. There are NOT three separate collapsible groups. |

**API calls**

- GET /info (render_client.info, memoized) — read for `latch_heads` (name, family, supports_scalar_target, supports_loss_select, supports_kinds, slider_min, slider_max, units, std_mean, std_std, sigma_k, out_channels, default_gain, value_default, target_kind_default, loss_type, health, health_reason, readout_source) and for `film_default.ckpt`
- GET /roots (render_client.roots) — {ok, roots:[{id,label,path,available,count}], stale_root_ids}; used as a change signature so the DoRA picker refreshes when a removable drive is mounted
- GET /models?family=adapter&loadable=true (render_client.models) — {models:[{path,label,rank,root_id}]} -> DoRA dropdown options
- POST /generate — payload keys contributed by this panel: latch[], film{ckpt,value,gain}, dora{name|ckpt_path,strength,interval_min,interval_max}, rho, mu, gamma, n_iter (via inference_tab.build_payload, no init path)
- POST /a2a_track — same panel keys (via inference_tab.build_payload when inf-init-path is non-empty)
- POST /a2a_mix — same panel keys, merged last over the a2a form payload (a2a_tab.py:527)
- POST /longform — same panel keys, merged last over the arc payload (a2a_tab.py:589)

**Behaviors to preserve**

- THE 23-STATE FIGURE IS OBSOLETE. Module docstring line 5 says "EXACTLY 23 States"; steering_states actually returns 35 and steering_payload raises ValueError on any other length (lines 178-180). Reconstructing the v1 contract from the append-only history comment: 3 slots x 6 fields (head/kind/value/gain/start/end) = 18, + film_enable/value/gain = 21, + dora_dd/dora_strength = 23. The 2026-07-12 v2 extension added +loss and +wsec per slot (+6), +dora imin/imax (+2), and the whole rho/mu/gamma/n_iter advanced row (+4) = 35. Anyone porting the '23-state contract' will be porting a year-old shape.
- The contract is POSITIONAL, not keyed. steering_payload slices vals[i*8:(i+1)*8] for slots, vals[24:27] for FiLM, vals[27:31] for DoRA, vals[31:35] for the advanced row. Insert one State anywhere but the end and every downstream field silently shifts. controls.py:77 carries an explicit comment forbidding the display-only ids (vlabel, llabel, health, meter) from entering the list.
- steering_payload is TOTAL, not a diff: it always returns keys latch/film/dora (film and dora may be None) plus gamma and n_iter (never None, since their defaults are 0.3 and 4). Callers do payload.update(steering), so gamma/n_iter always reach the server.
- A slot with no head selected is dropped entirely — head in (None,"none","") -> continue. Slots are not positionally significant in the payload; `latch` is a compacted list. But slot 1 IS special server-side: rho and mu auto-derive from SLOT 1's gain and every other slot's weight becomes slot_gain/slot1_gain. Which head sits in slot 1 therefore changes the meaning of every other slot's gain.
- Selecting a head fires _autofill_defaults, which rewrites 17 properties of that slot from /info: gain value, kind value+options+disabled, value value+min+max+step+disabled, vlabel title, loss value+disabled, llabel title, meter children, health children+style+title. _slot_view is factored out as a pure function precisely so it is unit-testable; a rewrite should keep that split.
- DoRA "none" must be transmitted by name. An omitted dora block means 'use the op default' server-side, which is evr1x for /a2a_mix — so silently dropping it would override the user's explicit base-model choice.
- The DoRA dropdown is dual-typed: legacy registry names (hof/newstack/evr1x) and absolute checkpoint PATHS coexist in the same value space, distinguished only by a leading "/". _resolve_dora on the server takes either; a bare name must be in DORA_REGISTRY, a ckpt_path bypasses it. Until 2026-08-24 the picker was hardcoded to the four names, which is why no trained adapter was selectable.
- Offline adapters are shown-but-disabled rather than hidden, on the stated principle that silently offering 692 unloadable adapters is 'the same class of lie as the one-size-fits-all LatCH slider'. _fill_dora_options rebuilds only when the options list is empty or when the root-availability signature from GET /roots changes, so plugging in a drive refreshes the picker without a page reload.
- INFERENCE TAB ONLY — preset load is deliberately split in two callbacks to defeat a race. Setting a head fires _autofill_defaults, which would overwrite anything a preset wrote in the same response, so _preset_apply_slots is triggered by Input(latch{i}-meter,'children') (an Output of the autofill) and applies the seven late suffixes kind/value/gain/start/end/loss/wsec afterwards, from a dcc.Store 'inf-preset-pending' that is cleared on use. `head` is an 'early' id; the other seven are 'late'.
- INFERENCE TAB ONLY — the sigma schedule chart (inf-sigma-graph) takes Inputs on latch{i}-head/start/end for all three slots and shades the active guidance windows over the sigma curve. This is the only place the window semantics are made visible.
- INFERENCE TAB ONLY — bracket sweep recall writes back into the panel: inf-ctl-dora-strength and inf-ctl-latch1-gain are allow_duplicate Outputs of bracket._recall (bracket.py:319-345), selected by the 'brk-ax-weight-target' radio (values 'dora_strength' | 'latch1_gain'). So the bracket's weight axis sweeps exactly ONE of two panel fields, and only slot 1's gain.
- A2A TAB ONLY — steering_states("a2a") is consumed by TWO different render callbacks: the a2a_mix render (a2a_tab.py:475/527) and the /longform prompt-ARC render (a2a_tab.py:564/589). One panel instance, two ops. The inf tab's single panel likewise feeds two ops via build_payload's routing (non-empty inf-init-path -> a2a_track, else generate), so all four render ops share this one control surface.
- Tab-specific default: dora_default is "none" on inf and "evr1x" on a2a. Nothing else in the panel differs by namespace.
- render_client.info() is memoized in a module global after the first success (_INFO_CACHE), so head lists, per-head defaults and the FiLM ckpt are frozen for the life of the process. Restarting the render server with new heads requires restarting the Dash app.
- The head dropdown fill short-circuits on `if current` — once filled it never refreshes. Only the DoRA picker watches for change (via the root-availability signature).

**Rewrite hazards**

- Trusting the docstrings. The module docstring says 23 states; steering_states' own docstring says 35; the code enforces 35. UI_BRIEF.md line 122 additionally records controls.py as 241 lines when it is 478. Any rewrite driven by the prose rather than the code will be a year out of date.
- Reordering or inserting States. steering_payload indexes by absolute position (vals[24:27], vals[27:31], vals[31:35]) with a hard length check. A rewrite that switches to a dict-keyed payload must delete both the slicing and the length assertion together, or it will look like it works and mis-map fields.
- Adding a control to the slot row without adding it to steering_states — or adding a display-only widget TO steering_states — both shift the slicing. The four display-only ids per slot (vlabel, llabel, health, meter) are already load-bearing as callback Outputs, and meter is additionally a callback TRIGGER for preset restore on the inf tab.
- _slot_view will raise TypeError when a head reports std_mean but no slider_min/slider_max: the meter f-string formats lo and hi with :.4g unguarded (controls.py:373), while the step computation above it does guard for None. The callback would fail silently in the browser, leaving the slot half-configured.
- _fill_dora_options updates the module-global _ROOT_SIG[ns] BEFORE deciding whether to return options (lines 431-433). If the rebuild produces no more than the four legacy names while `current` is already truthy, it returns no_update but the signature is recorded as seen — so it will not retry until the root availability changes again. The picker can get stuck at four entries.
- _ROOT_SIG is a module-level dict shared by both namespaces, keyed by ns. Two panel instances mutate the same global. A rewrite that instantiates the panel more than twice, or in more than one process/window, needs this state per-instance.
- The FiLM checkpoint is invisible state. It comes from /info film_default.ckpt at payload-build time, with an import-time filesystem probe (_resolve_mantu) as fallback. Both paths are Linux-specific (/run/media/kim/Mantu). On Windows or macOS the fallback resolves to a nonexistent path and the degraded /info-unreachable case will send a bogus ckpt rather than erroring. The removable-drive label collision (Mantu vs Mantu1) is a real observed condition, not defensive coding.
- Gain values are RELATIVE and normalized server-side (rho = mu = slot-1 gain; every slot weight = slot_gain / slot1_gain). The tooltip explicitly warns not to paraphrase this as a raw-weight claim. A new UI that presents gain as an absolute weight, or that reorders slots for cosmetic reasons, changes the render.
- gamma defaults to 0.3, which means the default configuration is NOT bit-reproducible even at a fixed seed. Any 'reproduce this render' feature in the new app must surface gamma=0 as a precondition.
- The default LatCH window 0.0-0.6 delivers under 10% of the available guidance budget because per-step strength is weighted toward the clean end. A redesigned UI that draws start/end as a symmetric range slider will visually imply a linear budget it does not have.
- The 5-second poller is the app's only recovery path from a render server that was down at page load, and on the inf tab it is shared by non-panel pickers. Dropping it in favour of a one-shot fetch reintroduces the permanently-empty-panel bug that is documented twice in comments.
- A2A-tab knobs OUTSIDE this panel overlap it conceptually: a2a-chroma / a2a-chroma-gain (default 2048.0) and a2a-guid-end (default 0.6) duplicate what a chroma LatCH slot plus its end_pct would express, and both are sent in the same a2a_mix payload that steering_payload updates. payload.update(steering) runs LAST, so on any key collision the panel wins — worth checking against the server's actual field names before merging the two surfaces.
- Three callbacks in register() are defined inside a `for i in range(...)` loop and all share the function names _fill_options / _autofill_defaults. Dash keys them by Output id so it works, but the closure over `head_id` is only safe because the body does not read loop variables. Any edit that starts reading `i` inside a body will capture the last value.

**Open questions**

- What are the current /info `latch_heads` in practice? The server is not running and /info is not readable here, so the actual head names, families, units, slider bounds and health values are unknown — the panel is entirely data-driven from that endpoint and a rewrite cannot enumerate the real head list from this repo.
- Does the server accept rho/mu/gamma/n_iter at top level for ALL four ops, or only for generate/a2a_track? controls.py places them top-level unconditionally and a2a_tab merges them into a2a_mix and longform payloads; only explorer_render_server.py (not available locally) can confirm resolve_latch reads them on every path.
- Does a2a_mix's own `chroma_gain` / `guidance_end_pct` collide with, or operate independently of, a chroma LatCH slot's gain and end_pct? Both are in the same payload and the panel's keys are applied last.
- What is `w_sec` in the server's units, and why is it the only slot field with no autofill? No comment or tooltip explains it.
- Are `chroma_rung1` / `chroma_rung2` still valid loss options, and against which heads? They are labelled only as 'T2' in a comment.
- Is `beat_grid` as a target kind actually supported by any head, or is it a legacy entry in _KIND_OPTIONS that supports_kinds always filters out?
- Which heads have `supports_scalar_target=False`? Those slots disable the value box entirely and the meter tells the user to supply a measured curve via `target_raw` — but there is NO control anywhere in this panel for supplying target_raw, so that path appears reachable only from a CLI. A new app probably needs to add it.
- The bracket weight-target radio offers only dora_strength and latch1_gain. Was that a deliberate scope limit or just what was wired first?

---

### Dash A2A transition tab (plots/explorer_sa3/a2a_tab.py, 637 lines) + the bracket param fan-out panel (plots/explorer_sa3/bracket.py, 345 lines), with the shared steering panel (controls.py) and payload contract (render_client.py, inference_tab.build_payload) read for context.

The A2A tab is a two-track DJ-style transition builder: you load an A source and a B source, place them against each other in time (A's out-point, B's in-point) on a shared "composite time" axis, mark a transition window inside that composite, choose how the seam is regenerated (sinesweep / refine / inpaint noising, or a pure crossfade), and POST the whole thing to the render server as /a2a_mix. The overlaid waveform canvas is a Plotly figure only — A drawn positive, B drawn mirrored negative, with a shaded vrect for the transition window; Dash has no native drag, so placement is done with paired numeric-input + slider controls that write each other, optionally quantized to a bar grid derived from A's librosa-detected BPM. Nothing is decoded or auditioned locally: the client reads the source files with soundfile purely to build a 40 Hz block-max envelope and a BPM estimate, and every actual sound comes back as a URL from the render server. The tab also hosts a second, unrelated renderer — a prompt-ARC longform panel (POST /longform) that reuses the same steps/cfg/seed/dist-shift fields — and instantiates the frozen 35-value LatCH/FiLM/DoRA steering contract under the a2a-ctl-* namespace, with harmonic chroma steering (chroma_morph) defaulted ON and DoRA defaulted to evr1x. bracket.py is NOT part of this tab at all: it is a Cartesian param fan-out panel wired to the Inference tab, which snapshots that form, enumerates combos over steps/cfg/nl/weight and fires one render per 400 ms tick.

**Controls (98)**

| id | label | kind | payload field | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `a2a-server-badge` | render server status badge | output-div |  | Filled every 2 s from GET /status: 'render server BUSY\|idle' + last 5 log_tail lines; on unreachable shows red 'render server DOWN' plus render_client.LAUNCH_HINT. |
| `a2a-interval` | status poll timer | dcc.Interval |  | interval=2000 ms, always running while the tab is mounted. |
| `a2a-wave-store` | loaded-waveform store | dcc.Store | a_path / b_path (source of) | data={} initially; on load becomes {'a'\|'b': {path, dur, bpm, env:[float], env_hz}}. env is a JSON list of 4-decimal floats at ~40 Hz — a 5-min track is ~12k numbers per side pushed into the browser. |
| `a2a-a-crop-dd` | A: pick source track | dcc.Dropdown | a_path (indirect) | Options built ONCE at register() time from the clip index: unique c.source_track, label 'artist — title', value = absolute source path, sorted case-insensitively. Filled via the self-trigger idiom Input('a2a-a-crop-dd','id'), which Dash fires exactly once per page load. clearable=True, minWidth 22em. |
| `a2a-a-path` | A: free audio path | dcc.Input (text) | a_path | Placeholder '…or free audio path (wins when non-empty)'. Stripped; if non-empty it overrides the dropdown. |
| `a2a-a-load-btn` | A: Load | html.Button |  | Triggers the shared _load callback; side chosen by ctx.triggered_id. |
| `a2a-a-meta` | A: metadata readout | html.Span (output) |  | 'dur 214.3s · 128.0 BPM' or 'BPM ?'. Also the ONLY place load errors surface ('load failed: <exc>', 'pick a track or enter a path'). |
| `a2a-b-crop-dd` | B: pick source track | dcc.Dropdown | b_path (indirect) | Same option list as A. |
| `a2a-b-path` | B: free audio path | dcc.Input (text) | b_path | Wins over the B dropdown when non-empty. |
| `a2a-b-load-btn` | B: Load | html.Button |  |  |
| `a2a-b-meta` | B: metadata readout | html.Span (output) |  |  |
| `a2a-a-anchor` | A ends @ (s) | dcc.Input (number) | a_end_sec | min=0, step=0.1, width 6.5em. On A load: value = round(0.62*dur,2), max = dur. Two-way synced with the slider; bar-snapped when a2a-snap is on. |
| `a2a-a-anchor-slider` | A out-point slider | dcc.Slider | a_end_sec (mirror) | min=0 max=1 step=0.1 initially; max set to dur on load. marks=None, tooltip bottom. Not read by the render callback — only a2a-a-anchor is. |
| `a2a-b-anchor` | B enters @ (s) | dcc.Input (number) | b_start_sec | On B load: max = max(0, dur - seg); value = round(min(0.40*dur, that max), 2). NOT re-clamped when a2a-seg-sec later changes. |
| `a2a-b-anchor-slider` | B in-point slider | dcc.Slider | b_start_sec (mirror) | Same max as the numeric input. |
| `a2a-seg-sec` | segment (s) | dcc.Input (number) | seg_sec | value=75.0, min=20, max=180, step=1. Defines the composite length: A is drawn from a_end-seg to a_end, B from b_start to b_start+seg. Drives the x-range of the overlay and the max of a2a-trans-range. |
| `a2a-snap` | snap to bar grid (BPM from A) | dcc.Checklist | snap_to_downbeat | value=['on'] by default. GUI effect: in the anchor sync callback, bar = 240.0/bpm_A (i.e. 4 beats), val = round(val/bar)*bar, rounded to 3 dp. Applied to BOTH A and B anchors using A's BPM. Silently no-ops if A has no BPM. Also sent to the server as snap_to_downbeat. |
| `a2a-overlay-graph` | overlaid A/B waveform canvas | dcc.Graph (display only) |  | config displayModeBar False; NO clickData/relayoutData callbacks — you cannot drag anything on it. Built by overlay_figure(store, a_end, b_start, seg, trans_range): A = go.Scatter fill tozeroy, line width 0.7, colour #1f77b4, positive y; B = same but y negated, colour #d62728. add_vrect(x0=t0,x1=t1,opacity=0.15,#888) for the transition window plus dotted vlines at both edges. Title: 'A ends @ Xs / B enters @ Ys — window t0–t1s of SEGs composite', else 'load both tracks'. height 280, xaxis range [0,seg] titled 'composite time (s)', yaxis ticklabels off, showlegend False. |
| `a2a-trans-range` | transition window (composite s) | dcc.RangeSlider | trans_start_sec / trans_end_sec | min=0, max=75 (rescaled to seg), step=0.5, value=[26,49], allowCross=False, marks=None, tooltip bottom. A separate callback (no prevent_initial_call) sets max=seg and clamps value into [0,seg] whenever a2a-seg-sec changes. |
| `a2a-quantize-bars` | quantize whole bars | dcc.Checklist | quantize_bars | default ['on']. Purely server-side — no GUI quantization of the range slider. |
| `a2a-mode` | mode | dcc.RadioItems (inline) | mode | Options 'sinesweep (sine noise schedule)' \| 'refine' \| 'inpaint'; default 'sinesweep'. Gates a2a-seam-size (enabled only in sinesweep), a2a-seam-nl (enabled only in inpaint) and the pure-basis option (disabled unless sinesweep). |
| `a2a-noise` | noise level | dcc.Input (number) | noise_level | value=0.42, min=0.05, max=0.95, step=0.01. Single value — there is NO noise ladder on this tab (the comma-list ladder lives on the Inference tab as inf-noise-ladder). |
| `a2a-seam-size` | seam-inpaint (frames) | dcc.Dropdown | seam_inpaint | Fixed options 0 / 128 / 256 / 512, value=0, clearable=False, width 6em. Sent as int. Disabled unless mode=='sinesweep' — but its State is still read and still sent when disabled. |
| `a2a-seam-nl` | seam nl (inpaint mode, 0=off) | dcc.Input (number) | seam_nl | value=0.35, min=0, max=0.95, step=0.01. Disabled unless mode=='inpaint'; still sent regardless. |
| `a2a-pure-basis` | pure-basis splice | dcc.Checklist | pure_basis | default ['on']. The callback rewrites its options to carry disabled=(mode!='sinesweep'), so the label greys out but the checked value persists into the payload. |
| `a2a-interp` | interp | dcc.Dropdown | interp | 'slerp' \| 'lerp', default slerp, clearable=False, width 7em. Comment says server defaults slerp/model/4242/2424 reproduce pre-crossfade-lab behaviour. |
| `a2a-construction` | construction | dcc.Dropdown | construction | 'model' \| 'latent_xfade (no model pass)' \| 'audio_xfade (v2 baseline)', default 'model', clearable=False, width 16em. The two xfade modes bypass the diffusion pass entirely, yet all noising/steering keys are still assembled and sent. |
| `a2a-eps-seed` | eps seed (sinesweep clamp) | dcc.Input (number) | eps_seed | value=4242, step=1, sent as int. |
| `a2a-seam-eps-seed` | seam eps seed | dcc.Input (number) | seam_eps_seed | value=2424, step=1, sent as int. |
| `a2a-tempo-match` | tempo match | dcc.Checklist | tempo_match | default ['on']. |
| `a2a-tempo-mode` | tempo mode | dcc.RadioItems (inline) | tempo_mode | 'ramp' \| 'follow', default 'ramp'. Always sent even when tempo_match is off. |
| `a2a-fine-align` | fine align (onset concurrence) | dcc.Checklist | fine_align | default ['on']. Server-side onset alignment — this is the same alignment concern the new app's audio timeline preview is meant to let you eyeball first. |
| `a2a-chroma` | chroma morph (harmonic steering) | dcc.Checklist | chroma_morph | default ['on'] — harmonic steering is ON out of the box on this tab. |
| `a2a-chroma-gain` | chroma gain | dcc.Input (number) | chroma_gain | value=2048.0, min=0, step=1. Same magnitude as the chroma_other LatCH head's default gain noted in controls.py's rho help text; this is a separate server-side chroma morph path, distinct from a chroma LatCH head in a slot. |
| `a2a-guid-end` | guidance end pct | dcc.Input (number) | guidance_end_pct | value=0.6, min=0, max=1, step=0.05. Mirrors the 0.0–0.6 default LatCH window; per controls.py's _WINDOW_HELP, that window delivers under a tenth of the guidance budget because per-step strength is weighted by (1-sigma)/sum(1-sigma). |
| `a2a-prompt-region` | region prompt (required) | dcc.Textarea | prompt_region | Placeholder 'TrackType: Music, VocalType: Instrumental, …'. Render aborts with 'ERROR: region prompt is required' if blank after strip. |
| `a2a-whole-enable` | whole-track a2a pass | dcc.Checklist | whole_track (presence) | default [] (OFF). When on, payload['whole_track'] = {'noise_level':…, 'prompt':…}; when off the key is present with value None. |
| `a2a-whole-prompt` | whole-track prompt | dcc.Textarea | whole_track.prompt | Second, independent prompt — the dual-prompt design: one prompt for the regenerated transition region, one for an optional whole-track a2a pass over the finished composite. |
| `a2a-whole-noise` | whole-track nl | dcc.Input (number) | whole_track.noise_level | value=0.4, min=0.05, max=0.95, step=0.01. |
| `a2a-steps` | steps | dcc.Input (number) | steps | value=24, min=1, max=500. SHARED by the transition render and the longform render. |
| `a2a-cfg` | cfg | dcc.Input (number) | cfg_scale | value=6.0, min=0, max=25, step=0.1. Shared with longform. |
| `a2a-seed` | seed | dcc.Input (number) | seed | value=-1, step=1. Shared with longform. -1 = server-resolved random. |
| `a2a-dist-mode` | dist shift | dcc.Dropdown | dist_shift (conditional) | 'default (ckpt)' \| 'flux', value='default', clearable=False, width 10em. The key is OMITTED entirely unless 'flux' is chosen — absence means the ckpt's own sampling_dist_shift (resolve_dist_shift). Unlike the Inference tab there is no numeric dist-shift override here. |
| `a2a-arc-schedule` | arc schedule '0:promptA\|45:promptB\|…' | dcc.Textarea | schedule (longform) | steered_longform grammar; a bare string is a single prompt. Required for the longform button. |
| `a2a-arc-init-path` | init audio path (blank = t2a longform) | dcc.Input (text) | audio_path (longform) | Non-empty routes /longform to the windowed a2a arc loop over that source; empty routes to the t2a LongFormRenderer path. |
| `a2a-arc-noise` | arc nl | dcc.Input (number) | noise_level (longform, only with audio_path) | value=0.4, min=0.05, max=0.95. |
| `a2a-arc-duration` | duration (s, t2a) | dcc.Input (number) | duration (longform, only without audio_path) | value=120.0, min=10, max=600. |
| `a2a-arc-window` | window (s) | dcc.Input (number) | window_sec | value=30.0, min=10, max=120. |
| `a2a-arc-overlap` | overlap (s) | dcc.Input (number) | overlap_sec | value=5.0, min=1, max=30, step=0.5. |
| `a2a-arc-xfade` | xfade (s, t2a prompt joins) | dcc.Input (number) | xfade_sec | value=4.0, min=0, max=20, step=0.5. |
| `a2a-longform-btn` | Longform render | html.Button |  | POST /longform via render_client.longform (timeout 7200 s). |
| `a2a-longform-status` | longform status JSON | html.Pre (output) |  | json.dumps of seed / timings / warnings / meta minus params_echo. |
| `a2a-longform-result` | longform players | html.Div (output) |  | _players(resp): one html.Audio per resp['urls'] zipped with resp['files']. |
| `a2a-render-btn` | Render transition | html.Button |  | POST /a2a_mix via render_client.render (timeout 3600 s), blocking the Dash worker thread. |
| `a2a-status` | transition status JSON | html.Pre (output) |  | Same shape as longform status; RenderError text is shown verbatim including the server traceback. |
| `a2a-result` | transition players | html.Div (output) |  |  |
| `a2a-history` | render history store | dcc.Store |  | List of {ts, label, urls, files, meta, params} capped at HISTORY_CAP=50, newest first. Written by BOTH the transition and the longform callbacks (allow_duplicate). Label for a mix: '<A stem> → <B stem> [mode nl=…]'; for longform: 'longform[<meta.mode>] <first 50 chars of schedule>'. The full payload is stored under 'params' but there is no recall-to-form UI on this tab. |
| `a2a-history-div` | history list | html.Div (output) |  | One bordered row per entry with its own audio players. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-head` | LatCH 1 head | dcc.Dropdown | latch[0].head | Options filled from GET /info latch_heads as '<name> [<family>]'; placeholder 'head (off)' or 'render server :8056 unreachable -- retrying every 5 s'. Choosing a head autofills 17 outputs on that slot from the checkpoint's own defaults. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-kind` | LatCH 1 kind | dcc.Dropdown | latch[0].kind | constant \| ramp_up \| ramp_down \| beat_grid (or the head's supports_kinds); default 'constant'. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-value` | LatCH 1 value | dcc.Input (number) | latch[0].value | default -30.0; min/max/step rewritten per head (mean ± k·sigma). Disabled for non-scalar (multi-channel) heads. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-gain` | LatCH 1 gain | dcc.Input (number) | latch[0].gain | default 512.0; overwritten by the head's default_gain. Server ties rho=mu=slot-1 gain and scales other slots relative to it. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-start` | LatCH 1 start | dcc.Input (number) | latch[0].start_pct | 0.0 default, 0–1 step 0.01. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-end` | LatCH 1 end | dcc.Input (number) | latch[0].end_pct | 0.6 default. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-loss` | LatCH 1 loss | dcc.Dropdown | latch[0].loss_type (omitted when blank) | '' \| mse \| smooth_l1 \| l1 \| huber \| bce_logits \| cosine \| scalar_pooled \| chroma_rung1 \| chroma_rung2. Disabled when the head does not support loss selection. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-wsec` | LatCH 1 w_sec | dcc.Input (number) | latch[0].w_sec (omitted when None) | placeholder 'def'. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-vlabel` | LatCH 1 value label | html.Span (tooltip only) |  | DISPLAY ONLY — must never enter steering_states(). |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-llabel` | LatCH 1 loss label | html.Span (tooltip only) |  | Display only. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-health` | LatCH 1 health badge | html.Span (output) |  | Display only: ok/undertrained/unstable/unknown colouring plus 'readout: unrecorded'. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch1-meter` | LatCH 1 dataset meter | html.Div (output) |  | Display only: 'dataset <mean> ± <std> · slider = mean ± kσ · [lo, hi]'. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch2-head` | LatCH 2 head | dcc.Dropdown | latch[1].head | Slot 2 repeats the exact same eight value ids plus the four display ids: a2a-ctl-latch2-{head,kind,value,gain,start,end,loss,wsec,vlabel,llabel,health,meter}. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch3-head` | LatCH 3 head | dcc.Dropdown | latch[2].head | Slot 3 likewise: a2a-ctl-latch3-{head,kind,value,gain,start,end,loss,wsec,vlabel,llabel,health,meter}. LATCH_SLOTS = 3. |
| `a2a-ctl-film-enable` | FiLM density adapter | dcc.Checklist | film (presence) | default off. When on, film.ckpt comes from GET /info film_default.ckpt, falling back to a hardcoded /run/media/kim/Mantu… path. |
| `a2a-ctl-film-value` | FiLM onsets/s | dcc.Input (number) | film.value | default 4.0. |
| `a2a-ctl-film-gain` | FiLM gain | dcc.Input (number) | film.gain | default 1.75, 0–5 step 0.05. |
| `a2a-ctl-dora-dd` | DoRA adapter | dcc.Dropdown | dora.name / dora.ckpt_path | IMPORTANT: on this tab dora_default='evr1x', so an adapter is applied by default. Options = the four registry names (none/hof/newstack/evr1x) plus every loadable adapter from GET /models?family=adapter, with unmounted-drive entries kept but disabled and sorted last. A value starting with '/' is sent as ckpt_path with name=None. |
| `a2a-ctl-dora-strength` | DoRA strength | dcc.Input (number) | dora.strength | default 1.0, 0–10 step 0.01. Sent even for name='none' because a top-level ckpt_path folds into the same dict server-side. |
| `a2a-ctl-dora-imin` | DoRA σ-interval min | dcc.Input (number) | dora.interval_min | default 0.0. |
| `a2a-ctl-dora-imax` | DoRA σ-interval max | dcc.Input (number) | dora.interval_max | default 1.0. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch-rho` | LatCH ρ | dcc.Input (number) | rho (top-level, omitted when blank) | placeholder 'auto'; blank keeps the server's rho=mu=slot-1-gain tie. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch-mu` | LatCH μ | dcc.Input (number) | mu (top-level, omitted when blank) | placeholder 'auto'. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch-gamma` | LatCH γ | dcc.Input (number) | gamma | default 0.3, 0–1 step 0.05. γ>0 makes head evaluation stochastic, so a fixed seed no longer reproduces bit-identical audio. |
| `a2a-ctl-latch-niter` | LatCH n_iter | dcc.Input (number) | n_iter | default 4, 1–16. |
| `a2a-ctl-info-poll` | steering /info poller | dcc.Interval |  | 5000 ms, max_intervals=-1. Exists because the old Input(id,'id') fill fired once and left the panel permanently empty when :8056 was down at page load. |
| `brk-ax-steps` | bracket axis: steps | dcc.Input (text, comma list) | steps | INFERENCE TAB, not A2A. Empty = fall back to the form's single value. Cast int. |
| `brk-ax-cfg` | bracket axis: cfg | dcc.Input (text, comma list) | cfg_scale | Cast float. |
| `brk-ax-nl` | bracket axis: nl (a2a only) | dcc.Input (text, comma list) | noise_level | Cast float. Rejected unless op resolves to a2a_track (non-empty inf-init-path) AND the form's noise ladder (inf-noise-ladder) is empty — two ladder mechanisms would multiply. |
| `brk-ax-weight` | bracket axis: weight | dcc.Input (text, comma list) | _weight → dora.strength or latch[0].gain | Cast float. Internal key '_weight' is special-cased in _apply_weight; it never reaches the wire under that name. |
| `brk-ax-weight-target` | bracket weight target | dcc.Dropdown | (selects which nested field _weight writes) | 'DoRA strength' (dora_strength, default) \| 'LatCH-1 gain' (latch1_gain). latch1_gain requires a head in slot 1; dora_strength requires a ckpt_path or a non-'none' adapter. |
| `brk-count` | live combo count | html.Span (output) |  | 'N combos (a×b×c×d)'; appends ' · op a2a_track × batch N' when inf-init-path is set, '— ⚠ nl axis needs an init audio path' when it is not, and '— that's a long night' when N>32. Shows '⚠ axis <name>: bad token …' on a parse error. |
| `brk-start` | Start bracket | html.Button |  | Snapshots inference_tab.form_states() (30 form ids + 35 steering states), calls build_payload, builds the combo list, arms brk-tick. |
| `brk-stop` | Stop | html.Button |  | Sets brk-queue to None and disables the ticker; the in-flight POST still finishes and lands as the last grid entry. |
| `brk-status` | bracket status line | html.Pre (output) |  | '[done/total] last Xs · ETA Ys' from the mean of observed timings.total_sec, then 'done — N combos in Ts'. |
| `brk-queue` | bracket queue store | dcc.Store |  | {op, base, combos, total, weight_target, t0}; each tick pops combos[0]. |
| `brk-results` | bracket results store | dcc.Store |  | [{index, label, combo, op, seed, error, urls, files, total_sec, params_echo}]. |
| `brk-tick` | bracket ticker | dcc.Interval |  | interval=400 ms, disabled=True until Start. ONE render per tick — sequential fan-out with per-item UI updates; the server serializes on GPU_LOCK anyway. |
| `brk-grid` | bracket result grid | html.Div (output) |  | Inline-block cards, maxWidth 340px, red border for failed combos with the first 500 chars of the error, an audio player per url, a 'Recall → form' button and a collapsible params_echo dump. |
| `{"type":"brk-recall","index":N}` | Recall → form | pattern-matching html.Button |  | ALL-matched. Writes the combo back into inf-steps, inf-cfg, inf-init-noise, and (depending on brk-ax-weight-target) inf-ctl-dora-strength or inf-ctl-latch1-gain — all allow_duplicate because controls' autofill also owns latch1-gain. Index-based lookup, never string matching. |

**API calls**

- POST /a2a_mix (render_client.render('a2a_mix', payload), timeout 3600 s) — keys assembled in a2a_tab._render, in this exact order: a_path, b_path, a_end_sec, b_start_sec, seg_sec, snap_to_downbeat, tempo_match, tempo_mode, fine_align, trans_start_sec, trans_end_sec, quantize_bars, mode, noise_level, seam_inpaint, seam_nl, pure_basis, interp, construction, eps_seed, seam_eps_seed, chroma_morph, chroma_gain, guidance_end_pct, prompt_region, whole_track, steps, cfg_scale, seed; plus dist_shift ONLY when a2a-dist-mode=='flux'; plus the steering merge (latch, film, dora, and rho/mu/gamma/n_iter only when their inputs are non-blank). whole_track is either null or {noise_level, prompt}. Types: a_end_sec/b_start_sec passed through raw from the inputs, seg_sec/noise_level/seam_nl/chroma_gain/guidance_end_pct/cfg_scale float, seam_inpaint/eps_seed/seam_eps_seed/steps/seed int, the five checklist-driven keys bool.
- POST /longform (render_client.longform, timeout 7200 s) — keys from a2a_tab._longform: schedule, steps, cfg_scale, seed, window_sec, overlap_sec, xfade_sec; then EITHER audio_path + noise_level (when a2a-arc-init-path is non-empty → windowed a2a arc loop) OR duration (t2a LongFormRenderer path); plus dist_shift only for 'flux'; plus the same steering merge.
- POST /a2a_track — NOT assembled in a2a_tab.py. It is built by inference_tab.build_payload when inf-init-path is non-empty, and is the op bracket.py fans out over: audio_path, prompt (base + ', ' + variation), noise_level, steps, cfg_scale, seed, cfg_interval [lo,hi], apg_scale, optional dist_shift, optional ckpt_path, optional mutate {enabled, op, amount, target, seed, decay_rate, decay_direction, quantile}, optional noise_levels (the comma ladder), optional preserve {enabled, head, k, until}, plus latch/film/dora/rho/mu/gamma/n_iter.
- POST /generate — inference_tab.build_payload when inf-init-path is empty: prompt, negative_prompt, duration, batch_size, duration_padding_sec + the same common/steering blocks.
- GET /status — a2a-interval, every 2 s, 1 s timeout; returns {busy, log_tail}.
- GET /info — memoized; supplies latch_heads (name, family, default_gain, target_kind_default, supports_kinds, supports_scalar_target, supports_loss_select, slider_min/max, std_mean/std_std/sigma_k, units, loss_type, out_channels, health, health_reason, readout_source) and film_default.ckpt.
- GET /models?family=adapter&loadable=1 and GET /roots — DoRA picker options and removable-drive availability.
- GET /audio/<job>/<file> — via render_client.audio_url(rel) = BASE + rel, used as the src of every html.Audio.
- Response shape consumed everywhere: {urls: [rel], files: [abs path], seed, timings: {total_sec, ...}, warnings, meta: {mode, params_echo, ...}} and on failure {error, traceback} or non-200 → RenderError.

**Behaviors to preserve**

- OVERLAID WAVEFORM CANVAS: not a widget, a Plotly figure recomputed by a pure function overlay_figure(store, a_end, b_start, seg, trans_range) on every change of a2a-wave-store / a2a-a-anchor / a2a-b-anchor / a2a-seg-sec / a2a-trans-range. A is drawn upward, B is drawn with y negated so the two mirror around zero; both are area fills (fill='tozeroy', line width 0.7). There is no per-clip colour coding beyond blue A / red B, no playhead, no zoom (displayModeBar off), and no interaction callbacks at all.
- COMPOSITE TIME: the x axis is not either source's time. A occupies source-time [a_end-seg, a_end] and B occupies [b_start, b_start+seg], both mapped onto composite time 0..seg. _env_slice clamps negative source times to 0 and then shifts the trace right by the clamped amount (x = arange(len)/hz + (max(0,t0)-t0)), so when A's out-point is closer to the file start than `seg`, A's waveform starts partway into the canvas instead of being stretched.
- ENVELOPE: built entirely client-side in _load_side via soundfile. mono = mean of |channels|; hop = round(sr/40) (=1102 at 44.1 kHz); env = block MAX over each hop (not RMS), with a final partial block appended. env_hz is stored as sr/hop, i.e. ~40.02 Hz, not exactly ENV_HZ_TARGET, and _env_slice reads the stored env_hz.
- BPM DETECTION: librosa.beat.beat_track on a 60 s excerpt starting at max(0, anchor-30) where anchor = 0.62*dur for A and 0.40*dur for B — i.e. tempo is measured around where the transition is expected, not over the whole track. np.atleast_1d(tempo)[0], rounded to 2 dp, None if <=0. The whole librosa block is wrapped in a bare except that silently swallows any failure (including librosa not being installed), leaving bpm=None.
- BAR SNAP: bar length = 240.0/bpm_A seconds, i.e. hardcoded 4 beats per bar; snapping is round-to-nearest, applied in the numeric<->slider sync callback to whichever of the two fired (ctx.triggered_id decides), then written back to BOTH. B's anchor is snapped with A's BPM — B's own detected BPM is never used for snapping. Snapping happens on top of also sending snap_to_downbeat=True to the server.
- ANCHOR SYNC: one callback per side generated by a _make_sync(side) factory; the numeric input and the slider are both Inputs and both Outputs, and the loop is broken by writing the same value to both. Loading writes the anchors with allow_duplicate outputs.
- LOAD DEFAULTS: A lands at 62% of its duration with max=dur; B lands at min(40% of dur, dur-seg) with max=dur-seg. Loading one side never touches the other (no_update everywhere else). The seg used for B's max is the value at load time.
- TRANSITION RANGE RESCALE: when a2a-seg-sec changes, max becomes seg and the current [lo,hi] is clamped (lo into [0,seg], hi into [lo,seg]). This callback has no prevent_initial_call, so it fires once at page load with the default 75 s.
- MODE GATING is cosmetic only: _gate disables a2a-seam-size outside sinesweep, a2a-seam-nl outside inpaint, and greys the pure-basis option outside sinesweep, but the render callback reads all three as States and puts seam_inpaint / seam_nl / pure_basis into every payload regardless of mode.
- DUAL PROMPTS: prompt_region (required, aborts the render if blank) describes the regenerated transition region; whole_track.prompt is a second prompt for an optional whole-composite a2a pass at its own noise level. When the whole-track checkbox is off the key is still present as whole_track: null.
- HARMONIC STEERING is two independent mechanisms on the same tab: (1) chroma_morph / chroma_gain / guidance_end_pct as flat payload keys, defaulted ON with gain 2048; (2) any chroma LatCH head loaded into one of the three a2a-ctl-latch* slots, which travels in the latch[] block with its own gain/start_pct/end_pct/loss_type. Nothing prevents both being active at once.
- STEERING CONTRACT: controls.steering_states('a2a') returns EXACTLY 35 States in a frozen positional order (3 slots × 8 fields = 24, then film_enable/value/gain, dora dd/strength/imin/imax, rho/mu/gamma/n_iter) and steering_payload raises ValueError if it gets any other count. Both the render and the longform callbacks splat *controls.steering_states('a2a') as their trailing States and merge the result with payload.update(controls.steering_payload(steer)).
- LONGFORM SHARES FIELDS: the prompt-ARC panel reuses a2a-steps, a2a-cfg, a2a-seed and a2a-dist-mode from the transition section, plus the same steering panel — so changing steps for a transition silently changes it for the next longform render and vice versa.
- RESULT HANDLING: _players(resp) zips resp['urls'] with resp['files'] and builds html.Audio(src=render_client.audio_url(url)) — i.e. BASE + the relative /audio/<job>/x.wav path. Status panes print seed / timings / warnings / meta with params_echo stripped out.
- HISTORY: one shared 50-entry store fed by both renderers, newest first, each entry keeping the complete outgoing payload under 'params'. There is no way to load an entry back into the form on this tab (unlike bracket's Recall).
- SERVER BADGE: GET /status every 2 s (1 s timeout, None on failure). On failure it prints the literal launch command from render_client.LAUNCH_HINT. render_client.info() is memoized in a module global forever after the first success, so head/FiLM metadata never refreshes without restarting the app.
- BLOCKING CALLS: /a2a_mix uses a 3600 s timeout, /longform 7200 s, and both run synchronously inside the Dash callback, holding a worker thread for the whole render.
- BRACKET PURPOSE: bracket.py is the Gradio_Lab 'param fan-out' port. It is instantiated inside inference_tab.layout() (bracket.layout() at line 272) and wired with bracket.register(app, form_states, build_payload) at line 680 — the two functions are passed in rather than imported to break a circular import. It has NO connection to the A2A tab; its recall targets are inf-* ids.
- BRACKET MECHANICS: axes are declared in _AXES order (steps, cfg, nl, weight) and combos come from itertools.product over the ACTIVE axes only, so weight varies fastest. A form seed of -1 is resolved once per run to a single shared random int so combos differ only along the axes. _apply_weight copies nested dicts (latch list entries, dora dict) rather than mutating the shared base. Failed combos stay in the grid with their error text. meta.params_echo returned by the server is treated as the authoritative recall record.

**Rewrite hazards**

- Docstring drift on the steering contract: a2a_tab.py's header and controls.py line 5 both describe the panel as 'EXACTLY 23 States', but steering_states() actually returns 35 and steering_payload() hard-raises on anything else. Trust the code, not the docstrings.
- The 35-value steering contract is POSITIONAL. controls.py explicitly warns that display-only ids (-vlabel, -llabel, -health, -meter) must never be added to steering_states(). Any rewrite that turns this into a dict/struct must preserve the exact field order per slot: head, kind, value, gain, start, end, loss, wsec.
- Disabled controls still ship values. seam_inpaint, seam_nl and pure_basis are sent in every payload regardless of mode; construction=latent_xfade/audio_xfade still carries the full noising and steering blocks. A rewrite that omits 'disabled' fields will not reproduce current renders.
- dist_shift is present-or-absent, not a value. 'default' means OMIT THE KEY so the checkpoint's own sampling_dist_shift applies. Sending dist_shift: 'default' or null is a different request.
- DoRA defaults to evr1x on this tab (dora_default='evr1x'), and controls.py notes the server ALSO defaults /a2a_mix to evr1x when the dora block is absent — so 'none' must be sent explicitly by name. Dropping the dora block to mean 'no adapter' silently applies evr1x.
- B's anchor max is computed once at load as dur-seg and is never recomputed when a2a-seg-sec changes; the transition range slider is rescaled but the B anchor is not. You can end up with b_start+seg past the end of B.
- Bar snap uses A's BPM for both anchors and hardcodes 4 beats/bar (240/bpm). No time-signature control exists, and B's own BPM is detected but only ever displayed.
- BPM detection failure is silent (bare except around the whole librosa block). snap then silently does nothing while the checkbox still reads 'on'.
- Source loading is soundfile-only. The docstring notes the server decodes anything via ffmpeg, so a path that fails to load in the GUI may still render fine — and the failure appears only in a small meta span, never in the status pane.
- The whole envelope is round-tripped through a dcc.Store as a JSON float list at ~40 Hz per side. Long sources mean tens of thousands of numbers crossing the wire on every callback that takes a2a-wave-store as State or Input.
- The source dropdowns are filled with the Input(component,'id') self-trigger idiom, which Dash fires exactly once per page load; the option list is a closure built at register() time from the clip index and never refreshes. controls.py already had to abandon this idiom for the steering pickers (replaced with a 5 s poller) after it left the panel permanently empty when :8056 was down at load.
- render_client.info() is memoized in a module-level global with no invalidation — restarting the render server with different LatCH heads requires restarting the Dash app.
- _NUM is a shared dict splatted into many dcc.Input constructors, so every numeric input shares one style dict object; a naive port that mutates it per-control will affect all of them.
- a2a-steps / a2a-cfg / a2a-seed / a2a-dist-mode are shared state between two different render ops (a2a_mix and longform) with no visual indication of that coupling.
- bracket's nl axis silently conflicts with the Inference tab's noise ladder; the guard exists only in _start, and the guard for weight targets depends on inspecting the already-built base payload (base['latch'], base['ckpt_path'], base['dora']['name']), not on the widgets. Any change to build_payload's shape breaks those checks quietly.
- bracket's Stop does not cancel the in-flight POST; there is no server-side cancel in the visible contract at all — the same limitation will apply to a Render button in the new app.
- The overlay canvas is display-only. Anything in a rewrite that implies you can drag clips on the current waveform is inventing a feature: today placement is numeric+slider only, exactly as the module docstring says ('Dash has no native drag').
- seam_inpaint is labelled 'frames' with options 0/128/256/512. At the 92.9 ms SAME latent frame rate those would be ~11.9/23.8/47.6 s, which is implausible for a seam — the unit is almost certainly something else (server-side latent frames of a different rate, or samples). Do not carry the label 'frames' into a new UI without confirming against the server.

**Open questions**

- What does the server do with snap_to_downbeat given the GUI has already bar-snapped the anchors — is it a second, independent snap (to a detected downbeat rather than the GUI's round-to-nearest-4-beats grid), and can the two disagree?
- What is the actual unit of seam_inpaint (0/128/256/512 'frames')? At 92.9 ms per SAME latent frame these are 12–48 seconds, which cannot be a seam width.
- Does /a2a_mix RE-ENCODE A and B at their chosen offsets, or does it reuse pre-encoded latents? Given the measured non-translation-invariance of SAME latents, this determines whether a_end_sec/b_start_sec can be freely repositioned in the new app or must trigger a server round-trip.
- How do chroma_morph/chroma_gain relate to a chroma LatCH head in a slot — separate code paths, additive, or mutually exclusive? chroma_gain's default 2048 matches the chroma_other head's default gain mentioned in controls.py's rho help.
- How does the flat guidance_end_pct interact with each LatCH slot's own start_pct/end_pct window?
- What exactly do construction='latent_xfade' and 'audio_xfade' ignore? They are described as 'no model pass' and 'v2 baseline', but the client still sends every noising and steering key.
- pure_basis ('pure-basis splice') is only enabled in sinesweep mode and defaults ON — what basis, and what changes when it is off?
- tempo_mode 'ramp' vs 'follow' semantics, and whether tempo_mode is honoured when tempo_match is false (it is always sent).
- Is there any server-side cancel for an in-flight render? Neither the A2A tab nor bracket has one; bracket's Stop only stops queueing.
- The A2A history keeps the full payload but offers no recall-to-form. Was that a deliberate omission or just unimplemented — should the new app's equivalent restore a past transition setup?

---

### Inference tab — `plots/explorer_sa3/inference_tab.py` (871 lines), plus the two panels it mounts inline: `controls.steering_panel("inf")` (controls.py, 478 lines) and `bracket.layout()` (bracket.py, 345 lines). Contract file `render_client.py` read for endpoint shapes.

This is the primary text-to-audio / audio-to-audio render surface. One form drives two ops: a non-empty `inf-init-path` routes the whole payload to POST /a2a_track, otherwise POST /generate — the routing is a single `if (init_path or "").strip():` inside `build_payload()`, and nothing in the UI announces which op is armed except the label text on that field. `build_payload()` is the single source of truth: it is called by the Render button, the A/B fan-out, the preset save, and the bracket fan-out, so all four cannot drift. The signature control is the sigma-schedule chart (`inf-sigma-graph`) paired with a two-handle RangeSlider in SIGMA space (`inf-cfg-interval`) — CFG is gated in the DiT as `cfg_interval[0] <= sigma <= cfg_interval[1]` (limited-interval guidance, Kynkäänniemi 2024), and the chart shows which STEP indices that sigma window actually lands on for the current dist-shift-warped schedule fetched from the server's POST /schedule. Around it sit a checkpoint journal picker with removable-drive awareness, an A/B resident-adapter slot rig, a 48-component shared steering panel (3 LatCH slots + FiLM + DoRA + advanced rho/mu/gamma/n_iter), a DiT weight-mutation "weight garden", an a2a-only rhythm-preserve ping-pong sampler block, a bracket param fan-out, and a client-side render history capped at 50 entries. Everything is positional: 30 form ids in `_FORM_IDS` order followed by a frozen 35-value steering contract, unpacked by tuple destructuring — reordering any of it silently mis-binds fields rather than erroring.

**Controls (111)**

| id | label | kind | payload field | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `inf-server-badge` | Render server status badge | div (output only) |  | Filled every 2 s from GET /status. Down state prints render_client.LAUNCH_HINT as a <pre>; up state prints 'BUSY — <job_id>' or 'idle' plus the last 5 lines of st['log_tail']. |
| `inf-interval` | (badge poller) | dcc.Interval |  | interval=2000 ms. Exists so the badge stays live while the Render callback blocks its worker thread synchronously for up to 3600 s. |
| `inf-preset-pending` | (preset hand-off store) | dcc.Store |  | Carries the LATE (per-LatCH-slot) half of a loaded preset between the two Load callbacks. Cleared to None on use. |
