| `inf-preset-dd` | Preset | dropdown |  | clearable, placeholder 'saved presets', width 300px. Options from GET /presets, label = '<name>  ·  <prompt[:60]>'. Refilled on the 5 s poll and after every save. |
| `inf-preset-load` | Load | button |  | n_clicks=0. Triggers the FIRST half of the two-phase load (see behaviors). |
| `inf-preset-name` | save as… | text input |  | width 200px. Empty -> status 'type a name in the box first'. |
| `inf-preset-save` | Save | button |  | n_clicks=0. Calls build_payload() first — a preset that would not round-trip is refused with the same ValueError text the Render button shows. |
| `inf-preset-status` | (preset status line) | span (output only) |  | 11px #888. Written by both _preset_save and _preset_load (allow_duplicate). |
| `inf-prompt` | Base prompt (persistent) | textarea | prompt | 100% x 60px. Placeholder shows the prefix vocabulary convention: 'TrackType: Music, VocalType: Instrumental, … (prefix vocabulary, then free text)'. Combined with inf-variation by _full_prompt(). |
| `inf-variation` | Variation (appended with ', ' when both non-empty) | textarea | prompt | 100% x 36px. _full_prompt(base, var) -> 'base, var' when both set, else whichever is non-empty. Empty result raises ValueError('error: prompt is empty'). |
| `inf-negprompt` | Negative prompt | text input | negative_prompt | value='' default. ONLY reaches the payload on the /generate branch — silently dropped for a2a_track. |
| `inf-root-dd` | roots | dropdown (multi) |  | value=[] default, placeholder 'all available roots', flex:1 minWidth 320px. Options from GET /roots: label '<label> (<count>)' plus '  — DRIVE NOT MOUNTED' when unavailable; disabled unless available or count>0. Scopes the ckpt journal (root_ids param), not the render payload. Tooltip names SAO/eval/model_roots.json as the config file. |
| `inf-ckpt-dd` | checkpoint | dropdown | ckpt_path | width 420px, clearable, placeholder 'base model (no ckpt)'. Options from GET /ckpts; label is the path relative to resp['root'] plus '[family · corpus · r<rank> · ep<epoch> · verdict]' and '(<MB>)'. LOSES to inf-ckpt-path when both are set. |
| `inf-ckpt-rescan` | Rescan | button |  | Forces rescan=1 on GET /ckpts AND a forced refresh of inf-root-dd options — one click after plugging in a removable drive, no page reload. |
| `inf-ckpt-path` | or path | text input | ckpt_path | width 30%, placeholder 'free-text ckpt path (overrides dropdown)'. Precedence in build_payload: (ckpt_path or '').strip() or (ckpt_dd or '').strip(). |
| `inf-ckpt-status` | (ckpt journal status) | span (output only) |  | '<n> ckpts · <scope>' plus ' · STALE (unmounted): <ids>' from resp['stale_root_ids']; or 'render server :8056 unreachable — retrying every 5 s'; or 'ckpt root unavailable: <error>' on the 404 body. |
| `inf-model-info` | (model info card) | div (output only) |  | Fires on either ckpt input. GET /models?q=<path>, exact path match. Renders label + 'family · corpus · r<rank>/a<alpha> · adapter_type · ep/step · <GB> resident', then recipe/purpose(why)/verdict/kim_feedback — a dict-valued recipe is expanded one line per key (explicit anti-pattern note: str(dict) puts a 1000-char repr on one line). Also a file:// link to run_meta.json and a provenance line. |
| `inf-slot-dd` | slots | dropdown (multi) | (POST /slots specs) | Inside <details open=False> 'A/B slots'. placeholder 'pick up to 3–4 adapters', flex:1 minWidth 360px. Options = GET /models?family=adapter&loadable=1 only — a full-FT ckpt replaces the backbone so it cannot be a slot. Label '<label> · r<rank>  [<root_id>]'. ~0.40 GB each at rank 128 (measured). |
| `inf-slot-apply` | Make resident | button |  | POST /slots {'slots':[{'ckpt_path':p},…]}. RenderError -> 'refused: <server reason>' (over VRAM floor / over max_slots / fullft path). Server refuses rather than OOMing. |
| `inf-slot-status` | (slot/VRAM meter) | div (output only) |  | '<n> resident · <free_gb> GB free · floor <vram_floor_gb> GB · max <max_slots> · <sum cost_gb> GB in slots'. |
| `inf-ab-arms` | compare | checklist (inline) | ab.slots | Options are rebuilt from GET /slots every 5 s: always 'base (no adapter)' -> value 'base' FIRST (the control arm), then one per resident slot with value=str(s['index']) and label s['label'][:40]. Selection is preserved across refills when still valid, else all options are selected. Values are STRINGS; _ab_go casts 'base'->None and others->int(). |
| `inf-ab-go` | Render A/B | button |  | POST /ab with the full form payload + payload['ab']={'slots':[None\|int,…]}. Refuses <2 arms ('pick at least two arms (base counts as one)') and refuses op != 'generate' ('A/B is the t2a path only — clear the init audio path'). |
| `inf-ab-status` | (A/B status) | span (output only) |  | On success: 'seed <s> — SAME seed on every arm, so they differ only by the model · <total_sec>s'. |
| `inf-ab-results` | (A/B result cells) | div (output only) |  | Flex-wrapped cards, one per arm: bold label, 'slot <n> · strength <x>', a 250px <audio controls>. Per-arm a['error'] renders red in place of the player without failing the batch. |
| `inf-duration` | duration (s) | number input | duration | value=47, min=1, max=378, step=1. Payload key present on /generate ONLY, but it is ALSO an input to POST /schedule for the chart on both paths, because dist_shift is length-dependent. |
| `inf-steps` | steps | number input | steps | value=24, min=1, max=500. Default in build_payload if blank: 24. Also drives the chart x-axis and the LatCH window fractions. |
| `inf-cfg` | cfg | number input | cfg_scale | value=6.0, min=0, max=25, step=0.1. Also printed into the chart title (annotation only — it does not change the curve). |
| `inf-seed` | seed | number input | seed | value=-1 (= random). No min/max. VOLATILE: never restored by a preset load, and stripped server-side on preset save. Bracket resolves -1 ONCE per run so combos share it. |
| `inf-batch` | batch | number input | batch_size | value=1, min=1, max=4. /generate branch ONLY. Also VOLATILE (not preset-restored). |
| `inf-apg` | apg | number input | apg_scale | value=1.0, min=0, max=1, step=0.1, dotted-underline tooltip. Adaptive Projected Guidance (Sadat 2024): 1.0 = orthogonal-only (cleaner at high CFG), 0.0 = vanilla CFG, in between blends. |
| `inf-durpad` | dur pad (s) | number input | duration_padding_sec | value=6.0, min=0, max=30, step=0.5. /generate branch ONLY. Silence appended after the requested duration before generation (paper default 6 s), trimmed on decode. |
| `inf-dist-shift` | dist shift | number input | dist_shift | value=None, placeholder 'ckpt default', width 90px, NO min/max/step. Sigma-SCHEDULE WARP, not a model input. Blank = the checkpoint's trained length-dependent default (paper Eq 3). A number = constant-alpha override: 1.0 linear, >1 concentrates steps at HIGH sigma (structure), <1 at LOW sigma (detail/polish — the under-trained tail). Key is OMITTED from the payload when it resolves to None. |
| `inf-dist-mode` | mode | dropdown | dist_shift | clearable=False, value='default', options ('default','flux'), width 110px. 'default' = the ckpt's trained sampling_dist_shift; 'flux' = the stock length-dependent FluxDistributionShift (breathing_v2_blockbuild convention), sent as the STRING "flux". A non-blank number in inf-dist-shift always wins. Resolution lives in the module-level pure function resolve_dist_shift_value(dist_shift, dist_mode). |
| `inf-init-path` | init audio path (non-empty → a2a_track) | text input | audio_path | width 50%. THE OP SWITCH for the whole tab. Non-empty (after strip) -> POST /a2a_track and duration/batch/negprompt/durpad are all dropped; empty -> POST /generate and init_noise/ladder/preserve are all dropped. Also an Input to the bracket combo counter. |
| `inf-init-noise` | init noise | number input | noise_level | value=0.4, min=0.05, max=0.95, step=0.01. a2a_track only. Overridden by a non-empty noise ladder. |
| `inf-noise-ladder` | noise ladder (e.g. 0.35,0.42,0.5) | text input | noise_levels | Comma-separated floats parsed by _parse_ladder; renders the same seed once per level. Bad token -> ValueError('bad noise ladder: <repr>'). Mutually exclusive with the bracket nl axis (bracket refuses: 'two ladder mechanisms would multiply'). |
| `inf-cfg-interval` | CFG interval (σ) | RangeSlider (2 handles) — THE SIGNATURE CONTROL | cfg_interval | min=0.0 max=1.0 step=0.01 value=[0.0,1.0] allowCross=False, marks {0:'σ=0', 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1:'σ=1'}, tooltip placement bottom always_visible=False (shows only while dragging). Lives in SIGMA space, NOT step space — the DiT gates per step on cfg_interval[0] <= sigma <= cfg_interval[1] (dit.py:479). Outside the interval the model runs uncond-free, which is also faster. [0,1] = CFG everywhere = default. Sent as [float(lo), float(hi)] on BOTH ops. |
| `inf-sigma-graph` | σ schedule chart | dcc.Graph inside dcc.Loading |  | config displayModeBar=False, height 280px (and layout height=280, margin l40 r10 t40 b30, yaxis_range [0,1.05], legend horizontal at y=1.12). READ-ONLY: no clickData/relayoutData/selectedData callback exists — the chart is a mirror of the RangeSlider, never an input. Plotly port of interface/diffusion_cond.py:create_sigma_chart. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-head` | LatCH 1 head | dropdown | latch[0].head | width 260px, clearable, placeholder 'head (off)' (replaced by 'render server :8056 unreachable -- retrying every 5 s' when /info fails). Options from GET /info latch_heads, label '<name> [<family>]'. head in (None,'none','') = slot OFF, skipped entirely in steering_payload. Setting it FIRES the 17-output autofill. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-kind` | LatCH 1 target kind | dropdown | latch[0].kind | clearable=False, default 'constant', width 130px. Base options ['constant','ramp_up','ramp_down','beat_grid'] but the autofill REPLACES options with h['supports_kinds'] and sets disabled=not scalar. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-value` | LatCH 1 value | number input | latch[0].value | default -30.0, step 0.1, width 90px. min/max/step/disabled are all rewritten per head by _slot_view: slider_min/slider_max = dataset mean ± k·σ, step = (hi-lo)/200 rounded. Disabled for non-scalar (multi-channel) heads. RAW units — the server standardises. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-gain` | LatCH 1 gain | number input | latch[0].gain | default 512.0, min 0, step 1, width 90px; overwritten by the head's default_gain on head change. RELATIVE not absolute: server normalises rho=mu=slot-1 gain and every slot's weight becomes slot_gain / slot-1 gain. Also a bracket weight-axis target and a bracket recall output (allow_duplicate). |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-start` | LatCH 1 start | number input | latch[0].start_pct | default 0.0, min 0 max 1 step 0.01. FRACTION OF STEPS. Feeds the gold LatCH band on the sigma chart. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-end` | LatCH 1 end | number input | latch[0].end_pct | default 0.6, min 0 max 1 step 0.01. Guidance is NOT proportional to window width — per-step strength scales by (1-sigma)/sum(1-sigma), so the default 0.0–0.6 window delivers under a tenth of the budget and widening to 0.8 roughly quadruples it (measured on a real 24-step schedule: first 20% of steps carry 0.2%, last 20% carry ~62%). |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-loss` | LatCH 1 loss | dropdown | latch[0].loss_type | clearable=False, value='' (= head default), width 130px. Options ['','mse','smooth_l1','l1','huber','bce_logits','cosine','scalar_pooled','chroma_rung1','chroma_rung2']. Autofill sets value to the head's trained loss_type and disables the control on non-scalar heads. Key omitted from the payload when falsy. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-wsec` | LatCH 1 w_sec | number input | latch[0].w_sec | value=None, min 0, step 0.1, placeholder 'def', width 90px. Key omitted when None. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-vlabel` | 'value' label | span (title target, output only) |  | DISPLAY ONLY — its 'title' is an Output of the autofill. MUST NOT enter steering_states (explicit code comment). |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-llabel` | 'loss' label | span (title target, output only) |  | Display only, same rule. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-health` | LatCH 1 health badge | span (output only) |  | Display only. '⚠ <health>' colored by _HEALTH_COLOR {ok #3a3, undertrained #c60, unstable #c00, unknown #888}; appends 'readout: unrecorded' when readout_source == 'not-recorded'. title = health_reason. |
| `inf-ctl-latch1-meter` | LatCH 1 dataset meter | div (output only) |  | Display only, BUT LOAD-BEARING: its 'children' is the Input that triggers the second half of preset load. Text: 'dataset <mean> ± <std><units>  ·  slider = mean ± kσ  ·  [lo, hi]', or the non-scalar / no-statistics variants. |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-head` | LatCH 2 head | dropdown | latch[1].head | Identical to slot 1; ids are f'inf-ctl-latch2-{head,kind,value,gain,start,end,loss,wsec}' + display-only -vlabel/-llabel/-health/-meter. |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-kind` | LatCH 2 target kind | dropdown | latch[1].kind |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-value` | LatCH 2 value | number input | latch[1].value |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-gain` | LatCH 2 gain | number input | latch[1].gain |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-start` | LatCH 2 start | number input | latch[1].start_pct |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-end` | LatCH 2 end | number input | latch[1].end_pct |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-loss` | LatCH 2 loss | dropdown | latch[1].loss_type |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-wsec` | LatCH 2 w_sec | number input | latch[1].w_sec |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch2-meter` | LatCH 2 meter | div (output only) |  | Also an Input to _preset_apply_slots. Siblings -vlabel/-llabel/-health also exist. |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-head` | LatCH 3 head | dropdown | latch[2].head | Same 8 value ids + 4 display ids. controls.LATCH_SLOTS = 3 is hardcoded and loops everywhere. |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-kind` | LatCH 3 target kind | dropdown | latch[2].kind |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-value` | LatCH 3 value | number input | latch[2].value |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-gain` | LatCH 3 gain | number input | latch[2].gain |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-start` | LatCH 3 start | number input | latch[2].start_pct |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-end` | LatCH 3 end | number input | latch[2].end_pct |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-loss` | LatCH 3 loss | dropdown | latch[2].loss_type |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-wsec` | LatCH 3 w_sec | number input | latch[2].w_sec |  |
| `inf-ctl-latch3-meter` | LatCH 3 meter | div (output only) |  | Also an Input to _preset_apply_slots. Siblings -vlabel/-llabel/-health also exist. |
| `inf-ctl-film-enable` | FiLM density adapter | checklist | film | value=[] default, single option value 'on'. When off, film = None in the payload. |
| `inf-ctl-film-value` | onsets/s | number input | film.value | default 4.0, step 0.1, width 90px. |
| `inf-ctl-film-gain` | FiLM gain | number input | film.gain | default 1.75, min 0 max 5 step 0.05. |
| `inf-ctl-dora-dd` | DoRA | dropdown | dora.name / dora.ckpt_path | clearable=False, width 160px, dora_default='none' ON THIS TAB. Options = 4 legacy registry names ['none','hof','newstack','evr1x'] + every loadable adapter from GET /models, with unmounted-drive entries kept but marked '— drive offline' and disabled, sorted last. A value starting with '/' is sent as ckpt_path with name=None; a bare name is sent as name. |
| `inf-ctl-dora-strength` | DoRA strength | number input | dora.strength | default 1.0, min 0 max 10 step 0.01. Sent even when name='none' (a top-level ckpt_path folds into this dict server-side via resolve_dora_req). Bracket weight-axis target and a bracket recall Output (allow_duplicate). |
| `inf-ctl-dora-imin` | DoRA σ-interval min | number input | dora.interval_min | default 0.0, min 0 max 1 step 0.05. Sigma-native gating (dit.py:466) — e.g. (0.25,1.0) disables the adapter for the final low-noise detail steps. NOTE: two bare inputs with no per-field label, unlike inf-cfg-interval which is a real range slider. |
| `inf-ctl-dora-imax` | DoRA σ-interval max | number input | dora.interval_max | default 1.0, min 0 max 1 step 0.05. |
| `inf-ctl-latch-rho` | ρ (LatCH adv) | number input | rho (top-level) | value=None, min 0, step 1, placeholder 'auto'. Guidance STEP SIZE. Empty = auto = slot-1 gain; the server then sets rho=mu=slot-1 gain and scales other slots relatively. Key omitted when None. Tooltip: move by factors of 2. |
| `inf-ctl-latch-mu` | μ (LatCH adv) | number input | mu (top-level) | value=None, placeholder 'auto'. Proximal/consistency term, auto-tied to ρ. Higher μ than ρ pulls toward the current latent (guidance bites less); lower lets guidance dominate and is where artefacts start. |
| `inf-ctl-latch-gamma` | γ (LatCH adv) | number input | gamma (top-level) | value=0.3, min 0 max 1 step 0.05. Noise added to the latent before each head evaluation (z_in = z0 + γ·ε, latch_guided.py:179). γ=0 makes the render bit-reproducible; any γ>0 makes head evaluation stochastic so a fixed seed no longer gives an identical file. |
| `inf-ctl-latch-niter` | n_iter (LatCH adv) | number input | n_iter (top-level) | value=4, min 1 max 16 step 1. Guidance iterations per sampling step; each takes a full mu_t gradient step on the re-evaluated z0, so it raises ADHERENCE as well as cost. 2 quick / 4 work / 8 when a target is ignored. |
| `inf-ctl-info-poll` | (shared 5 s option poller) | dcc.Interval |  | interval=5000, max_intervals=-1. Declared inside the steering panel but drives FIVE inference-tab callbacks too: inf-slot-dd options, inf-slot-status/inf-ab-arms, inf-preset-dd options, inf-root-dd options, inf-ckpt-dd options. Replaced the old Input(<id>,'id') one-shot fill, which left every picker permanently empty if :8056 was down at page load. Every consumer short-circuits on its own current State once filled. |
| `inf-mut-on` | enable (rebuilds model) | checklist | mutate.enabled | Inside <details open=False> 'Weight garden — DiT weight mutations (databending)'. value=[] default, option value 'on'. Whole mutate block is omitted from the payload when off. Enabling forces a model rebuild server-side. |
| `inf-mut-op` | op (shuffle = the proven-musical one) | dropdown | mutate.op | clearable=False, value='shuffle'. Options _MUT_OPS = ('shuffle','drift','blur','contrast','tilt','life'). |
| `inf-mut-amount` | amount (fraction mutated) | slider | mutate.amount | min 0.05 max 1.0 step 0.05 value 0.25, marks at 0.05/0.25/0.5/1. |
| `inf-mut-target` | target | dropdown | mutate.target | clearable=False, value='attn'. Options ('attn','mlp','norm','all'). |
| `inf-mut-seed` | mutation seed | number input | mutate.seed | value=1234. Separate from the render seed. |
| `inf-mut-decay` | decay direction | dropdown | mutate.decay_direction | clearable=False, value='late'. Options ('late','early'). |
| `inf-mut-decay-rate` | decay rate | slider | mutate.decay_rate | min 0 max 1 step 0.1 value 0.5, marks 0/0.5/1. |
| `inf-mut-quantile` | life quantile (alive \|w\| threshold; life op only) | slider | mutate.quantile | min 0.05 max 0.99 step 0.01 value 0.75, marks 0.05/0.5/0.75/0.99. Always sent, but the server's resolve_mutate reads it for op='life' only. |
| `inf-pres-on` | enable (ping-pong sampler, best-of-K renoise scored by LatCH head vs source envelope) | checklist | preserve.enabled | Inside <details open=False> 'Rhythm preserve — selection steering (a2a only)'. The whole preserve block is added ONLY on the a2a_track branch — turning it on with an empty init path silently does nothing. |
| `inf-pres-head` | scoring head | dropdown | preserve.head | clearable=False, value='onset_envelope'. Options ('onset_envelope','onset_envelope_drums','rms_energy_bass','rms_drums') — HARDCODED here, not fetched from /info latch_heads like the LatCH pickers. |
| `inf-pres-k` | K candidates | number input | preserve.k | value=4, min 2, max 16. |
| `inf-pres-until` | active until (fraction of steps) | slider | preserve.until | min 0.1 max 1.0 step 0.05 value 0.5, marks 0.1/0.5/1. |
| `brk-ax-steps` | steps (bracket axis) | text input | steps | Inside <details open=False> 'Bracket — param fan-out'. Comma list, placeholder 'e.g. 16,24,48', width 160px. Empty axis = the form's single value. |
| `brk-ax-cfg` | cfg (bracket axis) | text input | cfg_scale | placeholder 'e.g. 4,6,8.5'. |
| `brk-ax-nl` | nl (a2a only) | text input | noise_level | placeholder 'e.g. 0.35,0.5'. Refused unless op == a2a_track AND the noise ladder is empty. |
| `brk-ax-weight` | weight (bracket axis) | text input | (dora.strength \| latch[0].gain) | placeholder 'e.g. 0.5,1,1.5'. Special-cased through _apply_weight, which COPIES the nested dicts rather than mutating the shared base. |
| `brk-ax-weight-target` | weight target | dropdown |  | clearable=False, value='dora_strength'. Options 'DoRA strength' / 'LatCH-1 gain'. Validated at Start: latch1_gain needs a head in slot 1; dora_strength needs a ckpt_path or a dora name != 'none'. |
| `brk-count` | (live combo count) | span (output only) |  | '<n> combos (a×b×c×d)', plus ' · op a2a_track × batch <n>' when an init path is set, or ' — ⚠ nl axis needs an init audio path', or ' — that's a long night' above 32. Inputs include inf-init-path and inf-batch. |
| `brk-start` | Start bracket | button |  | Snapshots the whole form through form_states()+build_payload(), resolves a -1 seed ONCE, builds the Cartesian product (weight varies fastest), arms brk-tick. |
| `brk-stop` | Stop | button |  | Drains the queue; the in-flight POST still finishes and is kept as the last entry. |
| `brk-status` | (bracket status) | pre (output only) |  | '[done/total] last <n>s · ETA <n>s' then 'done — <n> combos in <n>s'. |
| `brk-queue` | (bracket queue) | dcc.Store |  | {'op','base','combos','total','weight_target','t0'}. Shrinks by one per tick. |
| `brk-results` | (bracket results) | dcc.Store |  | Per entry: index/label/combo/op/seed/error/urls/files/total_sec/params_echo. |
| `brk-tick` | (bracket ticker) | dcc.Interval |  | interval=400 ms, disabled=True until Start. ONE render per tick = sequential fan-out with per-item UI updates; the server serializes on GPU_LOCK anyway. |
| `brk-grid` | (bracket result grid) | div (output only) |  | Cards max 340px: bold label, red-bordered error <pre> (first 500 chars), audio players, a Recall button, and a <details>'params' with json.dumps(params_echo, indent=2). |
| `{"type": "brk-recall", "index": <int>}` | Recall → form | button (pattern-matching id) |  | Dash dict id, one per result card. Writes combo values BACK into inf-steps, inf-cfg, inf-init-noise, inf-ctl-dora-strength, inf-ctl-latch1-gain — all with allow_duplicate=True. Index-based lookup, never string matching. |
| `inf-render-btn` | Render | button |  | THE commit. prevent_initial_call=True. Blocks its worker thread synchronously (render_client.render timeout 3600 s). Failure text (ValueError or RenderError) goes to inf-status with no_update on the result/history outputs. |
| `inf-status` | (render status) | pre (output only) |  | whiteSpace pre-wrap. 'ok · op=<op> · seed=<s> · total=<n>s\nwarnings: <…>\nmeta: <…>'. |
| `inf-result` | (current render players) | div (output only) |  | _players(urls, files): one html.Audio(controls=True) per url with the filename underneath in 11px #666. src = render_client.audio_url(u) = BASE + rel. |
| `inf-history` | (render history) | dcc.Store |  | data=[] initial, capped at _HISTORY_CAP = 50, newest first. Entry: ts/op/label(prompt[:60])/urls/files/meta. CLIENT-SIDE ONLY — lost on page reload. |
| `inf-history-div` | History | div (output only) |  | Under an html.H4('History'). One bordered row per entry: '<ts> [<op>] <label>' plus the same players. |

**API calls**

- POST /generate — {prompt, negative_prompt, duration, batch_size, duration_padding_sec, steps, cfg_scale, seed, cfg_interval:[lo,hi], apg_scale, dist_shift?, ckpt_path?, mutate?{enabled,op,amount,target,seed,decay_rate,decay_direction,quantile}, latch:[{head,kind,value,gain,start_pct,end_pct,loss_type?,w_sec?}], film:{ckpt,value,gain}|null, dora:{name|ckpt_path,strength,interval_min,interval_max}|null, rho?, mu?, gamma?, n_iter?}
- POST /a2a_track — {audio_path, prompt, noise_level, noise_levels?, preserve?{enabled,head,k,until}, + the same common/steering block; NO duration/batch_size/negative_prompt/duration_padding_sec}
- POST /schedule — {steps, duration, dist_shift: float|"flux"|null, sigma_max: 1.0} -> {sigmas: [float] of length steps+1}. Memoized client-side per argument tuple, cache flushed above 256 entries. Drives inf-sigma-graph.
- GET /status (1 s timeout, every 2 s) -> {busy, job_id, log_tail:[...]} -> inf-server-badge
- GET /info (5 s timeout, memoized forever) -> {latch_heads:[{name,family,default_gain,target_kind_default,supports_kinds,supports_scalar_target,supports_loss_select,slider_min,slider_max,units,std_mean,std_std,sigma_k,out_channels,loss_type,health,health_reason,readout_source,value_default}], film_default:{ckpt}}
- GET /ckpts?rescan=&root_ids= -> {ok, root, ckpts:[{path,size,mtime,family,corpus,rank,epoch,verdict}], stale_root_ids:[...]}; a 404 body is passed through as 'ckpt root unavailable', not treated as server-down
- GET /roots -> {ok, roots:[{id,label,path,available,count}], stale_root_ids}
- GET /models?family=&corpus=&q=&loadable=&root_ids=&rescan= -> {ok,count,models:[{id,path,label,family,corpus,loadable,root_id,rank,alpha,adapter_type,epoch,step,load_cost_gb,recipe,purpose,verdict,kim_feedback,run_meta_path,provenance}]}
- GET /slots -> {slots:[{index,label,cost_gb}], free_gb, vram_floor_gb, max_slots}
- POST /slots — {slots:[{ckpt_path,label?}], activate?:int} -> {ok} or a refusal with reason/error/detail
- POST /ab — the /generate payload plus ab:{slots:[null|int,...]} -> {ok, arms:[{label,slot,strength,urls,error?}], seed, timings:{total_sec}}
- GET /presets -> {presets:[{name,prompt,...}]}
- GET /presets/<name> -> {schema,name,notes,created,payload,form?}
- POST /presets — {name, payload, notes:'op=<op>', form:{<form-id>: value}} (seed/batch_size stripped server-side)
- GET /audio/<job>/<file>.wav — via render_client.audio_url(rel) = BASE + rel, used as <audio src>
- render_client._OPS also declares a2a_mix, decode, longform, bend, but THIS TAB only ever issues generate and a2a_track

**Behaviors to preserve**

- SIGMA-SCHEDULE CHART, exactly how it works — the chart is a passive MIRROR, not a draggable surface. `inf-sigma-graph` has no clickData/relayoutData/selectedData/dragmode callback anywhere in the file; displayModeBar is off. The only draggable thing is the separate two-handle `inf-cfg-interval` RangeSlider directly above it. The chart redraws from an 6+9-Input callback `_sigma_chart`: inf-steps, inf-duration, inf-dist-shift, inf-dist-mode, inf-cfg-interval, inf-cfg, and every LatCH slot's head/start/end (3 slots x 3 = 9 more Inputs).
- CHART DATA PATH: `_sigma_figure` calls `render_client.schedule(steps, duration, ds)` -> POST /schedule {steps, duration, dist_shift: float|"flux"|null, sigma_max: 1.0} -> {"sigmas": [...]} of length steps+1, descending sigma_max->0. This is the REAL dist-shift-warped run schedule, not a client-side approximation. When the server is unreachable it falls back to a linear ramp `[1.0 - i/steps]` and stamps the title with 'LINEAR FALLBACK — server down, no dist-shift warp'.
- CHART MARKS: (1) blue 'sigma' line+markers (#1f77b4, marker size 4) descending over step index; (2) dashed cyan 'progress (1−σ)' line (#17becf); (3) a GREEN vrect at opacity 0.15 spanning min..max of the step indices whose sigma satisfies lo <= s <= hi, annotated top-left as 'CFG σ∈[lo,hi] (steps A–B)'; (4) one GOLD vrect at opacity 0.15 per active LatCH slot, spanning start*steps..end*steps, annotated bottom-left 'LatCH'; (5) the LatCH ∩ CFG overlap redrawn in ORANGE at opacity 0.25. Title line: 'σ schedule · steps=N · dur=Xs · dist_shift=<value|model> · cfg=N · [<source>]'.
- THE POINT OF THE SIGNATURE CONTROL: the user drags in sigma space because that is the DiT's native gate, but what they actually need to know is which STEPS get CFG — and that mapping is non-linear and moves with dist_shift and duration. The chart exists solely to answer that. A rewrite that turns the chart into a direct drag target would be an improvement, but must keep the sigma value authoritative and the step indices derived, never the reverse (the code comment is explicit: 'step indices are only a secondary annotation').
- EMPTY-BAND SILENCE: if no sigma in the returned schedule falls inside [lo,hi], `idx` is empty, `cfg_band` stays None, and NO green rect and no annotation are drawn at all — the user gets a chart that silently means 'CFG is off for every step'. There is no warning text.
- TWO-PHASE PRESET LOAD (a deliberate race fix, documented at lines 345-351). Setting a LatCH head fires `controls._autofill_defaults`, which writes that slot's gain/kind/value/loss/bounds from the head's own checkpoint defaults. If Load wrote the preset's per-slot numbers in the same response they would be immediately overwritten. So `_preset_load` writes only the EARLY ids and stashes the LATE ids (`kind,value,gain,start,end,loss,wsec` x 3 slots = 21 ids) into `inf-preset-pending`; a second callback `_preset_apply_slots` is triggered by Input(inf-ctl-latch{i}-meter, 'children') — an Output of the autofill — so Dash's dependency graph forces it to run AFTER. It clears the store to None on use so a later manual head change finds nothing to re-apply.
- PRESET IS A RECIPE, NOT A SNAPSHOT: `_VOLATILE_FORM_IDS = ('inf-seed','inf-batch')` are excluded from the saved form dict, and the server strips seed/batch_size from the payload too. Status text says '(seed not stored)' / '(seed/batch left as they are)'. A preset written by a CLI has no 'form' key and Load reports it as unusable from the viewer but still usable from the sweep CLI.
- PICKER RETRY DISCIPLINE: every option-filling callback keys off the shared 5 s `inf-ctl-info-poll` and short-circuits on its own current State once non-empty. This replaced Input(<id>,'id'), which Dash fires exactly once per page load — with :8056 down at load the pickers stayed empty forever with no error. The DoRA picker additionally rebuilds when the removable-drive availability signature `_ROOT_SIG[ns]` changes.
- REMOVABLE-DRIVE HONESTY: adapters on an unmounted root are kept in the DoRA list but labelled '— drive offline' and `disabled: True`, sorted after the usable ones. Root options get '— DRIVE NOT MOUNTED'. The ckpt status line appends ' · STALE (unmounted): <ids>'. The code calls the alternative — silently offering 692 unloadable adapters (measured 2026-08-26) — 'the same class of lie'.
- A/B RIG: several adapters held RESIDENT on one base; switching costs ~1.7 s per 8 s render vs a multi-GB reload for the ckpt picker. Bounded by VRAM — the server refuses a set that would cross the floor rather than OOMing, and the refusal reason is surfaced verbatim. 'base (no adapter)' is always the first arm because 'an adapter that sounds better against nothing is not a finding'. All arms share ONE seed so they differ only by the model.
- ARM-SELECTION PRESERVATION: on each 5 s refill, previously-checked arms are kept if still valid; if nothing survives, ALL arms are auto-selected. So the checklist self-heals rather than going empty.
- OP ROUTING IS INVISIBLE: the only cue that the tab is in a2a mode is the label 'init audio path (non-empty → a2a_track)'. Fields belonging to the other branch stay fully enabled and are silently dropped at payload time — negprompt/duration/batch/durpad on a2a; init-noise/ladder/preserve on t2a.
- BRACKET FAN-OUT: sequential, one render per 400 ms tick so the grid updates per item, with a running ETA from the mean of completed total_sec. A -1 form seed is resolved ONCE per run so combos differ only along the axes. A failed combo keeps its card in the grid with a red border. `meta.params_echo` from the server is the recall record and is shown raw in a collapsible.
- LATCH WINDOW NON-LINEARITY (from the tooltip, measured on a real 24-step schedule): per-step strength is scaled by (1-sigma)/sum(1-sigma), so rho/mu is a TOTAL budget weighted toward the clean end. First 20% of steps carry 0.2% of it, first 60% carry 9.3%, last 20% carry ~62%. Guidance advice: widen the window toward 1.0 BEFORE raising the gain.
- MODEL-INFO CARD is written to a 'three-audience standard' (eval-tables spec §14): what the model IS, the reproducible recipe, and the plain-language why — deliberately surfaced at the moment of picking, 'which is the only moment it changes a decision'.

**Rewrite hazards**

- POSITIONAL CONTRACTS EVERYWHERE. `_FORM_IDS` is a 30-tuple consumed by a single tuple-unpack in `build_payload`, immediately followed by `controls.steering_states('inf')`, a FROZEN 35-value ordered list consumed by `steering_payload` (which raises only on a LENGTH mismatch, never on a type mismatch). Reordering, inserting, or removing any field silently mis-binds every field after it — e.g. steps would arrive as cfg. `bracket.register` and `_preset_save` slice the same list by `len(_FORM_IDS)`. Any rewrite must replace this with named fields or reproduce the order exactly.
- `inf-ctl-latch{i}-vlabel`, `-llabel`, `-health`, `-meter` are display-only and MUST NEVER be added to steering_states — there is a capitalised comment saying so at controls.py:77, because the payload builder consumes the list positionally.
- SCHEDULE CACHE IS NOT CKPT-AWARE. `render_client.schedule` memoizes on (steps, duration, dist_shift, sigma_max) only. When dist_shift is None the returned schedule depends on the LOADED MODEL's trained sampling_dist_shift — so after switching `inf-ckpt-dd` the chart can keep showing the previous checkpoint's warp from cache. Cache is only ever cleared when it exceeds 256 entries.
- `render_client.info()` is memoized in a module global FOREVER after the first success. LatCH head lists, head defaults, slider bounds and the FiLM default ckpt are all frozen at the first successful /info. Restarting the render server with new heads requires restarting the Dash process.
- THE RENDER CALLBACK BLOCKS SYNCHRONOUSLY for up to 3600 s (render_client.render default timeout), and the bracket ticker blocks for the same on each tick. The module docstring says this occupies a worker thread; the 2 s `inf-interval` badge is the only liveness signal. A single-worker deployment freezes the whole UI. A rewrite needs real async/job polling — the server already has /status with busy + job_id.
- dist_shift 0.0 IS A VALID OVERRIDE, not 'blank'. `resolve_dist_shift_value` tests `dist_shift not in (None, "")`, and `build_payload` tests `if ds is not None` — so 0.0 passes through. But `inf-dist-shift` has NO min/max/step, so negative and absurd values also reach the server. Any rewrite that uses a falsy check here changes behavior.
- cfg_interval defaults are re-applied in two places with different code paths: `build_payload` does `lo, hi = (cfg_interval or [0.0, 1.0])` and `_sigma_figure` does the same independently. Chart and payload can only agree because both hardcode the same fallback.
- `inf-ab-arms` values are STRINGS ('base' or str(slot_index)) and are cast with `None if a == 'base' else int(a)`. A slot table that ever emits a non-integer index breaks this silently.
- DoRA 'none' MUST be sent to the server BY NAME. An absent dora block means 'use the op default' server-side (evr1x for /a2a_mix), which would silently override the user's base-model choice. So `dora = {'name': 'none', 'strength': ...}` is constructed deliberately for the 'none' case — dropping that as an optimisation changes renders.
- LatCH gains are RELATIVE, not absolute. The server normalises rho = mu = slot-1's gain and rescales every slot as slot_gain / slot-1 gain (resolve_latch:514-517). A gain copied from another tool is a different scale and will not reproduce. Consequence for the bracket: sweeping `latch1_gain` with blank rho/mu also sweeps the guidance step size, so it is NOT a clean single-variable axis.
- gamma > 0 breaks bit-reproducibility. `inf-ctl-latch-gamma` defaults to 0.3, so by default a fixed seed does NOT give an identical file whenever any LatCH head is active. Only gamma = 0 is reproducible.
- `inf-pres-*` (rhythm preserve) is a2a-only and `inf-negprompt`/`inf-duration`/`inf-batch`/`inf-durpad` are t2a-only, but nothing disables or greys them. Enabling rhythm preserve with an empty init path is a silent no-op.
- `inf-noise-ladder` and the bracket `nl` axis are two separate ladder mechanisms; the bracket refuses the combination explicitly ('two ladder mechanisms would multiply'). Nothing else guards against enabling both in a different code path.
- `inf-mut-quantile` is always sent inside `mutate` but is read server-side for op='life' only — the code comment says so. Changing it under any other op does nothing and the UI does not say so (only the span label does).
- `_FILM_CKPT_FALLBACK` hardcodes a Linux removable-drive path (/run/media/kim/Mantu or Mantu1, probed with os.path.isdir at import). This will not resolve on Windows or macOS and is used whenever /info is unreachable at payload-build time.
- The bracket recall button uses a Dash PATTERN-MATCHING dict id `{'type': 'brk-recall', 'index': N}` with Input(..., ALL) and `ctx.triggered_id['index']`. There is no equivalent in a non-Dash framework; it needs an explicit per-card handler.
- `inf-ctl-latch1-gain` and `inf-ctl-dora-strength` are Outputs of BOTH the controls autofill and the bracket recall, hence allow_duplicate=True throughout — a rewrite must decide the precedence explicitly, since Dash's is 'last writer wins per callback invocation'.
- `inf-history` lives only in a client-side dcc.Store and is capped at 50; a page reload loses the whole session's render history. The audio URLs it holds are server-relative and only valid while :8056 is up with the same job files on disk.
- LATCH_SLOTS = 3 is a module constant looped over in at least six places (layout, states, payload, autofill registration, chart Inputs, late-id list). Changing it changes the length of the frozen 35-value steering contract.
- `inf-ckpt-path` free text silently WINS over `inf-ckpt-dd`; the dropdown keeps showing the other value. The model-info card resolves the same precedence, so it is at least consistent, but the dropdown is visually stale.
- The ckpt journal's poller path short-circuits once `current` options are non-empty — the ONLY forced refresh is the Rescan button or a change to `inf-root-dd`. New checkpoints written while the page is open never appear on their own.

**Open questions**

- The render server source (~/Projects/SAO/eval/explorer_render_server.py) is not available locally, so /schedule, /slots, /ab, /presets, /roots, /models, /ckpts response shapes are inferred purely from render_client.py docstrings and the consuming callbacks. Field names beyond those actually read (e.g. everything inside `recipe`, or `verdict`'s value domain) are unknown.
- sigma_max is always sent as 1.0 by the client and is not exposed as a control. Is it ever meaningfully != 1, and should the rewrite surface it?
- Should the sigma chart become a DIRECT drag target in the rewrite (drag the green band edges on the chart itself)? The data flow supports it — sigma is authoritative, step indices derived — but the current code has no chart-input callback at all, so this is new behavior, not a port.
- `inf-dist-shift` accepts 0 and negatives with no bounds. Is 0 a legal constant alpha server-side, or should the rewrite clamp it (and to what)?
- controls.LATCH_SLOTS is hardcoded at 3 and the steering contract length (35) is derived from it. Is a variable slot count wanted in SA3 Studio, or is 3 a real server-side limit?
- `inf-pres-head` options are hardcoded ('onset_envelope','onset_envelope_drums','rms_energy_bass','rms_drums') while every other head picker is fed from /info. Is that a deliberate subset of scorable heads, or drift that should be fixed by fetching?
- The A/B slot count hint says 'up to 3–4 adapters' but the real bound is the server's max_slots + VRAM floor. What is the actual max_slots value?
- Which fields of `meta.params_echo` matter for recall — the bracket shows the whole blob raw, and only steps/cfg_scale/noise_level/_weight are ever read back into the form.
- The docstring cites file:line references into a codebase not present here (stable_audio_3/models/dit.py:479 and :466, interface/diffusion_cond.py:create_sigma_chart, latch_guided.py:117/138/176-186/179, explorer_render_server.py resolve_latch:514-517, ckpt_probe.FAMILIES). These were not verifiable in this read-only pass.
- Nothing in this tab touches the latent_server_sa3.py :7892 VAE-decode / LatCH /steer path — the timeline/compositor workflow will need a contract for that which this file does not provide.

---

### design_handoff_sa3_studio — README.md + "SA3 Studio v3.dc.html" (full template markup + <script type="text/x-dc"> logic class), cross-checked against latent_tool.txt, phosphor-border.js, and the live contract in plots/explorer_sa3/render_client.py

SA3 Studio v3 is a single-window, 1800x900, no-scroll (except right pane) desktop shell drawn as a React-ish `.dc.html` prototype: a 42px top bar, a scrolling centre holding a master mix strip + ruler + four fixed lanes, a 248px fixed bottom pane that shows exactly ONE of four tabs (CHROMA / PROMPT+SIGMA / MIX+SIGNAL PATH / TERMINAL), and a 284px collapsible right "CONTEXT" pane of six accordion modules (OVERLAP-INPAINT, FILES, GENERATION, <LANE N> CHAIN, ADVANCED SAMPLING, MASTER CHAIN). A second top-level view (STATISTICS) swaps the centre for four latent-analysis canvases lifted almost verbatim from the old Dash analysis/dataset tabs. Everything is drawn to 15 canvases with deterministic pseudo-random synthetic data — no audio, no model, no I/O, and notably NO TRANSPORT AT ALL (no play/stop, no audio element, no scrub playback; the playhead is only a click-to-locate marker). The genuinely load-bearing design content is: the oklch token set, the drag-to-scale numeric interaction (260px sweep = full range, shift = 1/100th, click-without-move focuses for typing), 82 `data-help` strings the README says to carry over verbatim, the sigma/schedule model, the harmonic-overlap chroma scoring, and `phosphor-border.js` (the one production-quality file). Its backing model is a compositor-in-latent-space: the SIGNAL PATH stage list is DECODE -> BUNGEE -> ENCODE -> LANE CHAINS -> A2A RE-NOISE -> INPAINT OVERLAPS -> MIX -> MASTER CHAIN -> DECODE, which is closer to the user's real workflow than the README prose claims, but it never says that repositioning a clip invalidates its latent, and the whole preview/alignment loop the user actually wants is simply absent.

**Controls (135)**

| id | label | kind | payload field | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `session` | SESSION | select |  | Top bar. 3 hardcoded options session_2026-09-06_0912 / _2026-09-07_2140 / _2026-09-08_0815. Needs a session store; no backend endpoint exists for this. |
| `model` | MODEL (checkpoint) | select |  | Options stable-audio-3-base / custom_v3 (LatCH medium) / custom_v2, all hardcoded. Real backend already has GET /models, /ckpts, /roots and POST /slots which the design ignores entirely. |
| `modelFolder` | (unlabelled) checkpoint folder path | text input | ckpt_path | Default '/home/kim/Projects/SAO/stable-audio-3/checkpoints/custom_v3'. Closest real analogue is POST /slots specs[].ckpt_path; the design has no slot table, no VRAM budget, no A/B. |
| `presets.master / setPresetMaster` | MASTER PRESET | select | name | Options club_mix_v1, ambient_bed, drum_swap_a, init. README: recalls every lane chain, clip layout, mix order + node values, master chain, sigma schedule and prompt. Real /presets stores only {name, payload, notes, form} — the whole-session shape is invented. |
| `(unbound) SAVE` | SAVE | button |  | DEAD CONTROL — no onClick binding at all in the markup. Would map to POST /presets. |
| `startRender / renderLabel / renderBtnStyle` | RENDER (becomes 'SAMPLING · N steps left') | button |  | Appears THREE times (top bar, MIX pane, GENERATION module) all bound to the same handler. Doubles as progress indicator and non-interactive while rendering. In the prototype it only drives the phosphor-border animation for fxCfg.dur seconds. |
| `setViewWorkspace` | WORKSPACE | button (tab) |  | Top-level view switch; hides the whole 248px footer stack when in stats. |
| `setViewStats` | STATISTICS | button (tab) |  |  |
| `toggleHelp` | HELP | button (toggle) |  | Turns on the hover-help layer; onRootMove does e.target.closest('[data-help]') and renders helpBoxStyle at cursor+14/+16. 82 data-help strings exist. |
| `toggleFxPanel` | FX | button (toggle) |  | Opens the RASTER FX tuning panel. README known-gap #5: temporary, should be removed. |
| `masterRef` | MASTER — MIX RESULT | canvas 1400x56 |  | Draws _contour('__master__') as a turquoise waveform with 2px red clip marks on top and bottom edges where amp >= 0.94*mid. Purely synthetic. |
| `env.points[0..3] / pt.onDown` | a2a noise envelope nodes | drag node (4) |  | Bitwig-style. Value = (90 - relY*100)/80 clamped 0..1. IMPORTANT: labelled as the MASTER envelope but the setter writes selClip.env — it is the SELECTED CLIP's a2a envelope, greyed to opacity 0.28 when that clip's a2a is off. |
| `env.segments[0..2] / seg.onDown` | envelope curve bend | drag segment (3) |  | 16px transparent hit stroke, ns-resize. curve = clamp(-1..1, startCurve + (startY-clientY)/60), rendered as a quadratic control point offset by curve*60. |
| `projectBpm / setProjectBpm / dragProjectBpm` | PROJECT BPM | number input + drag-to-scale |  | Range 60–200, step 0.1, cursor ew-resize. Default 124. Drives grid, secPerBeat, and every clip's stretch % label. |
| `snap / setSnap` | SNAP | select |  | 8 values: bar \| beat \| 8 \| 16 \| 32 \| lane ('downbeats (magnetic)') \| edge ('clip edges') \| off ('free'). 'lane' is magnetic within 5px converted to beats via laneWidthPx. |
| `autoMatch` | MATCH BPM | button |  | Sets projectBpm to mean of clips' bpmNative, rounded to 0.1. README calls this 'MATCH BPM' in one place and the section text says least-total-stretch. |
| `meetBpmLabel` | → N bpm | readout |  | Mean native BPM preview. |
| `autoMatchDownbeats` | MATCH DOWNBEATS | button |  | README calls it 'ALIGN DOWNBEATS' — copy mismatch. Computes circular mean of every clip's downbeat phase (phase derived from _hash(file), i.e. fake) and shifts each clip by the shortest path, wrapping at ±2 beats. |
| `zoomLabel` | 'N bars · bar M+' | readout + data-help |  | Carries the gesture help: middle-drag ↕ zoom / ↔ scroll in one gesture, shift+wheel scrolls, plain wheel left to the page. |
| `rulerRef / onRulerDown / onWheel` | RULER | canvas 1400x30 |  | Three stacked label rows per bar: bar number, seconds (1 dp), latent frame index at FRAME_HZ=10.767. Bar lines full height, beat lines from 55%. Click/drag = locate playhead (snapped). Middle-down enters zoom mode. |
| `playheadBarLabel / playheadTimeLabel` | bar N.MM / S.SSs · frame F | readout |  | In the 250px gutter left of the ruler. |
| `lane.onSelect` | lane header | click region (×4) |  | Sets activeLane and force-opens menus.chain. activeLane drives the LANE CHAIN module, the chroma heatmap source, the sigma graph's LatCH window lanes, and the detune scan. |
| `lane.chipStyle / lane.name / lane.countLabel` | colour chip, LANE N, 'k clips' / 'empty' | readout (×4) |  | Lane colours: L1 purple oklch(54% 0.10 300), L2 green oklch(56% 0.11 150), L3 turquoise oklch(55% 0.11 195), L4 grey oklch(50% 0.02 250). |
| `lane.onDrop / allowDrop` | 'drop to add clip at playhead' | drop target (×4) |  | 20px dashed slot INSIDE the header, not on the lane canvas. Creates {start: snap(playheadBeat), len: 16 (hardcoded), kind: LATENT if name ends .npy else AUDIO, bpmNative: 118 + laneIndex*3.4, loop:false, a2a:false, noise:0.2, env:null}. README says 'drop a clip at the pointer' — code drops at the playhead. |
| `lane.onTarget` | TARGET | button (×4) |  | Makes this lane the chroma reference; sets targetMode='lane'. Styled with --warm when active. |
| `lane.clipBpm / lane.onClipBpm / lane.dragClipBpm` | CLIP BPM | number input + drag (×4) |  | Range 60–200. Edits the SELECTED clip if it is in this lane, else laneClips[0] — a silent target ambiguity. |
| `lane.clipDetune / lane.onClipDetune / lane.dragClipDetune` | DETUNE ¢ | number input + drag (×4) |  | Range -100..+100, integer. Rotates the clip's 12-class chroma by cents/100 with energy split between neighbouring classes; drives the match score and the detune scan. |
| `lane.ref / lane.onLaneDown / onWheel` | lane waveform | canvas 1400x62 (×4) |  | Draws grid, per-clip waveform in lane colour, red 2px clip marks, 25%-darker ink where two clips overlap, downbeat markers with yellow coincidence glow (14px/6px/2px triple stroke), and the chroma-hover red line. Left-click on bare canvas = locate playhead + select lane; middle = zoom. |
| `clip.onDown` | clip block | drag (per clip) |  | MOVES the clip: horizontal = start (snapped), vertical = lane change via round(dy/laneHeight), clamped 0..3. README claims left-drag is 'scrub, looping' and that dragging near an edge trims — NEITHER EXISTS in the code. |
| `clip.onLoop / clip.loopStyle` | LOOP | button (per clip) |  | Hidden when the clip is narrower than 70px. Loop only doubles the waveform contour ((u*2)%1) — cosmetic. |
| `clip.scoreLabel / clip.scoreStyle` | 'χ 0.78' (prefixed '↻ ' when a2a on) | readout chip (per clip) |  | Harmonic-overlap match against the target; chip hue interpolates 75→260 by score. Explicitly protected from being the label that gets sliced when the clip is narrow. |
| `clip.bpmLabel / clip.bpmStyle` | '127.6→124 -2.8%' | readout chip (per clip) |  | Hidden below 150px clip width. |
| `ov.onSelect / ov.label` | 'INPAINT N.NN bar' | click region (per overlap) |  | Auto-derived: for each lane, consecutive sorted clips whose spans intersect. Key is `${a.id}-${b.id}`. Selecting it switches the bottom pane target to INPAINT and opens the OVERLAP module. |
| `playheadStyle` | playhead | readout |  | 2px purple, pointer-events none, drawn per lane. |
| `bottomTabs[chroma\|prompt\|mix\|terminal] / t.onClick` | CHROMA / PROMPT + SIGMA / MIX + SIGNAL PATH / TERMINAL | button tabs (4) |  | Exactly one visible at a time inside a fixed 248px footerStackStyle so the lanes never move. README says the earlier stacked layout was too cramped. |
| `bottomHint` | per-tab hint text | readout |  | 'hover the heatmap to read a frame' / 'settings follow the selection' / 'lane order feeds the mix nodes' / 'stdout of the running job'. |
| `chromaModeButtons (band:global\|band:bass\|band:mid\|band:high)` | GLOBAL / BASS oct1 / MID oct5 / HIGH oct9 | button group (4) |  | GLOBAL = 12 classes, all three regressors folded (UI's own summary). Bands render 128 raw bins each; captions 'octave 1 · width 1.0', 'octave 5 · width 1.5', 'octave 9 · width 1.0'. README's promised 'RAW vs 12-class toggle' does NOT exist (state.chromaMode:'folded' is dead). |

Colours oklch(58% 0.13 155) green, oklch(60% 0.12 90) yellow, oklch(58% 0.17 25) red. |

| `scanRef / onScanDown` | detune scan strip | canvas 500x30 + drag |  | Match vs detune across ±100¢ in 4¢ steps. Green dashed line = peak, red 2px = current detune, grey verticals at -50/0/+50. Click or drag sets detune to round(frac*200-100). README says it sits BELOW the heatmap; it is actually in the 250px sidebar to the LEFT. |
| `scanLabel` | 'now 0¢ · mean 0.78 ± 0.11 · peak +24¢' | readout |  |  |

| `chromaRef / onChromaHover / onChromaLeave` | chroma heatmap | canvas 1400 x chromaH |  | 9px target reference row on top, then 12 or 128 rows. Cell colour: lightness from bin value, HUE from the FRAME's match score (60 + (1-m)*200) — so the whole column is tinted by consonance. Pitch-class separator lines every 1/12. Hover marks the same frame red on the lane canvas. |
| `curveRef` | match curve over heatmap | canvas overlay |  | Y axis anchored to the unison/tritone anchors (not the data) so height means the same thing across clips; 54px right gutter holds the three labels; white 3.4px halo under a 1.7px dark stroke. |
| `targetTag / targetName` | GENERATE \| CLIP \| A2A \| INPAINT + file name | readout |  | The bottom pane follows the selection. For an overlap: 'a.file → b.file'. For nothing: 'new clip at the playhead'. |

| `clipNoise / setClipNoise / dragClipNoise` | NOISE | number input + drag |  | UI drag range 0–1, step 0.01. data-help states the real backend range is 0.1–100 for v/eps models and 0.01–1.0 for rectified flow — the drag range only covers the RF case. Safe value 0.2. |
| `prompt / setPrompt` | describe the sound… | textarea (flex:1) |  | Empty = unconditional. |
| `negPrompt / setNegPrompt` | negative prompt — what to steer away from | textarea (34px fixed) |  |  |

| `cfg / setCfg / dragCfg` | CFG | number input + drag |  | Range 0–15, step 0.1. Inert in POST mode (only via the cfgNote text — the field is NOT actually disabled in the markup). |

| `duration / setDuration / dragDuration` | LENGTH s | number input + drag | duration | Drag range 1–180. /schedule and /longform both take duration. Help notes caps: 2 min small, 6 min 20 s medium/large. |

| `randomizeSeed` | RND | button |  |  |

| `toggleMixRow` | ▾ / ▸ | button |  | DEAD CONTROL — mixSummaryStyle is hardcoded display:none, so the collapsed one-line summary never appears and the expanded row never hides. |
| `mixOrder / setMixOrder` | MIX ORDER | select |  | tree '(1+2) + (3+4)' \| cascade '((1+2)+3)+4' \| quad 'weighted 4-way (lerp only)'. Rationale in help: slerp is pairwise only. |
| `quadWeights[0..3] / q.onChange` | LANE 1..4 weight | range slider (4) |  | 0–1 step 0.01, only when mixOrder==='quad'. NOT normalised anywhere. |
| `mixNodes M1 / M2 / MX — node.onLerp, node.onSlerp` | LERP / SLERP | button pair (×3) | interp | Node labels differ by order: tree = '1+2','3+4','M1+M2'; cascade = '1+2','M1+3','M2+4'. Maps to the latent player's /mix?interp=slerp\|lerp. |
| `mixNodes node.onT` | interpolation position | range slider (×3) | t | 0–1 step 0.01, default 0.50. Maps to /mix?t=. |
| `pipeline (9 stages)` | SIGNAL PATH · live | readout list |  | 1 DECODE latent→audio, 2 BUNGEE stretch/pitch, 3 ENCODE audio→latent, 4 LANE CHAINS, 5 A2A RE-NOISE, 6 INPAINT OVERLAPS, 7 MIX pairwise/weighted, 8 MASTER CHAIN, 9 DECODE latent→audio. Each derives its on/off and note from actual state; dimmed to opacity 0.45 when off. This is the single most informative surface in the design. |
| `terminalCollapse / terminalNormal / terminalFull` | COLLAPSE / PANE / FULL SCREEN | button group (3) |  | PARTIALLY DEAD — terminalOpen is hardcoded `true`, so COLLAPSE only restyles the button. FULL sets position:absolute;inset:42px 0 0 0;z-index:40 but the terminal lives inside the 248px footer stack which is overflow:hidden. |
| `logLines` | terminal output | readout list (7) |  | Colour-coded by subsystem. Mock lines reveal the intended log vocabulary: 'player /status → ok same-l heads: adaln_zero, standardized, chroma_match', decode RTF, bungee stretch+pitch, overlap inpaint, chroma target, mix node states, master peak dBFS + clipped frames. |
| `terminalDotStyle` | running indicator | status dot |  | Hardcoded on, green. |
| `toggleSide / sideToggleLabel` | ▾ CONTEXT / ▸ | button |  | 284px open, 26px collapsed, 0.15s width transition. README says 296px — mismatch (the FX panel IS anchored at right:296px). |

| `toggleChromaXfade / chromaXfadeLabel` | CHROMA CROSSFADE | button (toggle) |  | Default ON. 'Crossfades the chroma of the two clips through the overlap using the harmonic-overlap matching, instead of a plain amplitude fade.' No backend exists for this. |
| `toggleLocalOverride / localOverrideLabel` | LOCAL STEPS / CFG | button (toggle) |  | Default off. When off the region uses the global steps/cfg. NOTE: the STEPS/CFG fields below are NOT disabled or hidden when the override is off. |
| `overlapSteps / setOverlapSteps / dragOverlapSteps` | STEPS (region) | number input + drag |  | Range 1–100 integer, default 28. |
| `overlapCfg / setOverlapCfg / dragOverlapCfg` | CFG (region) | number input + drag |  | Range 0–15, default 3.0. Help: lower than global blends better. |
| `(unbound) ▸ INPAINT OVERLAP` | ▸ INPAINT OVERLAP | button |  | DEAD CONTROL — no onClick. This is the only per-region commit action in the design and it is not wired. |
| `toggleMenuFiles / menuFilesDot` | FILES | accordion header |  | Path caption '~/PROJECTS/LATENTS_SA3/AUDIO'. Dot lit when clips exist. |
| `files[].onDragStart` | file browser rows | draggable list (8) |  | kick_break_140bpm.wav, pad_drone_Cmin.wav, vocal_chop_verse.wav, field_rain_loop.wav, bass_sub_hit.wav, arp_pluck_16th.wav, 000412.npy, 000871.npy. dataTransfer text/plain = filename. No tree, no search, no scan of a real directory. |
| `toggleMenuGen / menuGenDot` | GENERATION | accordion header |  | Dot lit when prompt or negPrompt is non-empty. |
| `renders[].onDragStart` | RENDERED THIS SESSION — drag to a lane | draggable list (4) |  | clip_014_take3.wav 0:45, clip_014_take2.wav 0:45, clip_012_a2a.wav 1:02, clip_011_out.wav 0:38. This is the only surfacing of render outputs; the README's top-bar 'CLIP select' does not exist. |
| `toggleMenuChain / activeLaneLabel / menuChainDot` | <LANE N> CHAIN | accordion header |  | Header text and 3px left border take the active lane's colour. Dot lit when any of latch(with a loaded head)/film/lora/bungee is on. |
| `toggleLatch / latchLabel + presets.latch / setPresetLatch` | LATCH GUIDANCE + module preset | button (toggle) + select |  | Preset options bpm_default, chroma_lock, centroid_ramp, off. |
| `latchSlots[0..1].onModel` | LatCH head checkpoint | select (×2) | head | none, bpm_madmom_v3.pt, spectral_centroid_v2.pt, rms_v1.pt, chroma_384_v1.pt, onset_strength_v1.pt. Help says the list comes from latch_weights/*.pt and slider bounds from the head's feature_stats / p1–p99 metadata. Real /steer takes head as a query param. |
| `latchSlots[0..1].onKind` | target kind | select (×2) |  | constant \| ramp_up \| ramp_down \| beat_grid \| chroma_major \| chroma_minor. Switching to beat_grid rewrites target to 120, else to 1. |
| `latchSlots[0..1].onTarget` | TARGET / BPM | range slider (×2) |  | Bounds swap by kind: beat_grid = 30–240 step 1 (labelled BPM); everything else 0–5 step 0.05. Help says the real bounds should come from the head's own p1–p99. |
| `latchSlots[0..1].onWeight` | WEIGHT | range slider (×2) | gain | 0–50 step 0.1, default 1.0. Help: 0 disables without unloading; past ~10 the head dominates the prompt. Nearest real analogue is /steer?gain=. |
| `latchSlots[0..1].onStart / onEnd` | START % / END % | range slider pair (×2) |  | 0–1 step 0.01, defaults 0.00–0.20. accent-color is the slot colour; these draw the hatched windows on the sigma graph. |
| `setLatchRho / latchH.rho` | ρ VARIANCE | range slider |  | 0–30 step 0.1, default 1.0. Help: high values wash out transients. |
| `setLatchMu / latchH.mu` | μ MEAN | range slider |  | 0–30 step 0.1, default 1.0. Help: this is the one that actually shifts the feature; raise it before ρ. |
| `setLatchGamma / latchH.gamma` | γ NOISE | range slider |  | 0–20 step 0.05, default 0.3. Help: 0 makes it deterministic and often over-smoothed. |
| `setLatchIter / latchH.nIter` | MEAN ITER | range slider |  | 1–80 step 1, default 4. Linearly slower. |
| `toggleLatchLog / latchLogLabel` | LOG GRADIENT NORMS | button (toggle) |  | Diagnostic; prints per-step gradient norms to the terminal. |
| `toggleFilm / filmLabel + presets.film / setPresetFilm + setFilmScale` | FILM / SCALE | toggle + select + range |  | Scale 0–2 step 0.05, default 1.2. Presets flat, bright, dark. No data-help on any of the three. |
| `toggleLora / loraLabel + presets.lora / setPresetLora + setLora + setLoraScale` | LORA / DORA | toggle + 2 selects + range | ckpt_path | loraOptions: — none —, vinyl_warmth (LoRA, r16), tape_sat (LoRA, r8), club_lowend (DoRA, r32), granular_pads (DoRA, r16). Scale 0–1 step 0.01, default 0.8. SINGLE SLOT — README known-gap #1 wants +/− to add more. Real backend already exposes a multi-slot resident adapter table via /slots. |
| `toggleBungee / bungeeLabel + setSemis / dragSemis` | BUNGEE STRETCH / PITCH · SEMITONES | toggle + number input + drag |  | Semitones drag range ±24, step 0.5. There is NO stretch-ratio control despite the label — stretch is implied entirely by clip BPM vs project BPM. |
| `toggleMenuAdv / menuAdvDot` | ADVANCED SAMPLING | accordion header |  | Dot lit (purple) when shape!=='logsnr' \|\| stepped \|\| cfgLo>0 \|\| cfgHi<1 \|\| cfgRescale>0. |
| `cfgLoField / setCfgLo / dragCfgLo` | CFG LO | number input + drag |  | Stored as progress 0–1 (default 0.10); displayed either as progress or as the step index where progress first crosses it. Drag range switches to 0–steps integer in STEP mode. Help stresses progress = 1 − sigma as the sampler reports it, NOT a step fraction. |
| `cfgHiField / setCfgHi / dragCfgHi` | CFG HI | number input + drag |  | Default 0.85. Same dual representation. |
| `toggleCfgUnit / cfgUnitLabel` | UNIT — STEP / PROG | button (toggle) |  | Purely a display switch; the value stays stored as progress so changing STEPS only changes the displayed step numbers. |
| `sampler / setSampler` | SAMPLER | select |  | 15 options: dpmpp-2m-sde, dpmpp-3m-sde, dpmpp-2m, k-heun, k-lms, k-dpmpp-2s-ancestral, k-dpm-2, k-dpm-adaptive, k-dpm-fast, v-ddim, v-ddim-cfgpp, euler, rk4, dpmpp, pingpong. Help says the list SHOULD be filtered by objective (rectified_flow → euler/rk4/dpmpp; rf_denoiser → pingpong; v/eps → k-diffusion set) but the prototype shows all 15 unconditionally. |
| `sigma.shape / setSigmaShape` | SHAPE | select |  | logsnr 'logSNR-uniform (SA3)', geometric, linear, log, exponential, cosine. Choosing logsnr swaps the λ MIN/λ MAX fields in and the σ MIN/σ MAX fields out (lamFieldStyle / sigFieldStyle). |
| `sigma.rho / setSigmaRho / dragSigmaRho` | σ CURVE | number input + drag |  | Drag range 0–10 in code; README's table says 1–15. Warps the time axis before every shape, including logsnr. |
| `sigma.lamMin / setLamMin / dragLamMin` | λ MIN | number input + drag |  | Range -12..0, SA3 ships -6.2. Only visible in logsnr shape. |
| `sigma.lamMax / setLamMax / dragLamMax` | λ MAX | number input + drag |  | Range 0..6, SA3 ships 2.0. |
| `sigma.min / setSigmaMin / dragSigmaMin` | σ MIN | number input + drag |  | Drag range 0–2 in code; README's table says 0.001–1; the data-help itself says 'range 0–2'. Default 0.01. Hidden in logsnr shape. Ignored by rectified-flow objectives. |
| `sigma.max / setSigmaMax / dragSigmaMax` | σ MAX | number input + drag | sigma_max | Drag range 0–1000 in code; README's table says 1–100; help says 'range 0–1000'. Default 100. The REAL /schedule endpoint defaults sigma_max=1.0 — a 100x semantic gap. |
| `toggleStepped / steppedLabel` | STEPPED | button (toggle) |  | Quantises the chosen shape into plateaus. Reveals TILT and PLATEAUS. |
| `sigma.tilt / setTilt / dragTilt` | TILT | number input + drag |  | 0–1 step 0.05, default 0.15. Long help: tilt 0 = dt 0 = no-op on ODE solvers, but a valid churn/restart step under ping-pong. |
| `sigma.plateaus / setSigmaPlateaus / dragPlateaus` | PLATEAUS | number input + drag |  | Drag range 2–24 integer, default 6, floor clamped to 2. Not in the README's range table at all. |
| `cfgRescale / setCfgRescale / dragCfgRescale` | RESCALE | number input + drag |  | 0–1, default 0. CFG rescale / scale_phi. Drawn as the dotted amber line on the sigma graph. Not in the README's range table. |
| `toggleMenuMaster / menuMasterDot` | MASTER CHAIN | accordion header |  | 'applied to the mixed latent, after the lane chains'. Dot lit when latchOn \|\| normOn. |
| `toggleMasterLatch / setMasterHead` | LATCH HEAD | toggle + select | head | Reuses latchModelOptions; default chroma_384_v1.pt. Maps to /steer?head=. |
| `setMasterGain / master.gain` | GAIN | range slider | gain | 0–120 integer, default 64. The real /steer client defaults gain=48.0. No data-help. |
| `toggleMasterNorm / masterNormLabel` | LATENT NORMALISE | button (toggle) |  | Default ON. Help: mixing two latents shrinks their norm, so leaving this off gives a quiet, dull decode. This is the only place the design acknowledges a real latent-mixing artefact. |
| `toggleFxLoop / fxLoopLabel` | LOOP ON / LOOP OFF | button (toggle) |  | RASTER FX — TEMP panel. Loops the sweep for tuning. |
| `fxPalette / setFxPalette` | PALETTE | select | palette | teal, amber, accent, sparse, c64 — all defined in phosphor-border.js PALETTES. |
| `fxFields[11] / fd.onInput` | SWEEP START Hz 0–2000/5, SWEEP END Hz 0–500/1, DURATION s 0.5–20/0.5, THICKNESS px 1–40/1, LINES / COLOUR 1–60/1, JITTER 0–1/0.01, PERSISTENCE s 0.002–0.12/0.002, CHROMA BLEED 0.2–12/0.1, SUPERSAMPLE 1–14/1, GAIN 0.2–5/0.05, SPOT SPREAD 0–1/0.01 | range sliders (11) |  | All but sweepStart/sweepEnd/dur are pushed live to fx.set(). Settled values are the ones in the README JSON block. |
| `fxDump` | config JSON dump | readonly textarea |  | JSON.stringify(fxCfg) for copying settled values out. |
| `fxRef` | raster border overlay | canvas 300x170 |  | position:absolute inset:0, image-rendering:pixelated, pointer-events:none, z-index:60, display toggled by `rendering`. alphaOut:true so unlit phosphor is transparent. |
| `statLaneButtons[0..4]` | LANE 1..4 + MASTER | button group (5) |  | Under an 'ANALYSE' label, with the caption 'features read from the sidecars (.TIMESERIES.npz, .json)'. Drives a per-lane RNG seed for all four mock charts. |
| `xcorrRef` | 256×256 DIM CROSS-CORRELATION | canvas 300x300 |  | Mock is only 32x32 cells. Real equivalent exists: analysis.dim_xcorr in the Dash explorer. |
| `pcaRef` | PCA — PC1 vs PC2 (frame pool) | canvas 300x300 |  | Real equivalent analysis.pca_frames exists but the Dash app never plotted it — this panel is a modest extension. |
| `featRef / statFeature / setStatFeature` | DIM ↔ FEATURE CORRELATION | canvas 300x180 + select |  | Options spectral_centroid, rms, onset_strength. Real Dash version populated the dropdown dynamically from the 1-D fields of the TIMESERIES npz. |
| `scatterRef / statAxisX / statAxisY` | scatter X vs Y | canvas 300x180 + 2 selects |  | Both dropdowns offer bpm, lufs, rel_pos — exactly the Dash SCALAR_FIELDS list. Defaults bpm vs lufs, same as _ds_fill. |
| `helpBoxStyle / helpText` | hover-help box | floating tooltip |  | position:fixed at cursor+14/+16, max-width 280px, dark oklch(24% 0.02 250) ground, 11px/1.45, z-index 100, pointer-events none. |

**API calls**

- DESIGN-IMPLIED (no code in the mockup — every one of these is a capability the drawn controls would need): POST /generate {prompt, negative_prompt, steps, cfg, cfg_lo, cfg_hi, cfg_rescale, seed, duration, sampler, sigma{shape, min, max, rho, lam_min, lam_max, stepped, plateaus, tilt}, model/ckpt}
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: POST /a2a_track per clip {clip audio or latent, init_noise_level (clipNoise), noise_envelope (4 points + 3 curve values), prompt block, sampler+sigma block}
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: POST /inpaint (NO SUCH OP IN THE VISIBLE CONTRACT) {mask regions [start_frame, end_frame] per overlap, crossfade curve, steps, cfg, chroma_xfade flag} — README says the 257xL binary-mask channel supports multiple regions per pass
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: POST /encode audio→latent per lane (SIGNAL PATH stage 3) — required by the measured non-translation-invariance of SAME latents, but absent from the render_client op set {generate, a2a_track, a2a_mix, decode, longform, bend}
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: latent mix — maps onto the latent player's GET /mix?crop_a=&crop_b=&t=&interp=slerp|lerp, but only pairwise; the design's 'weighted 4-way (lerp only)' node has no endpoint
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: LatCH guidance — maps onto GET /steer?crop=&head=&gain=, but the design needs far more: per-slot kind (constant|ramp_up|ramp_down|beat_grid|chroma_major|chroma_minor), target value, weight, step window start/end, plus shared rho/mu/gamma/n_iter and a gradient-norm log flag
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: POST /decode for the master result and for every latent clip on load (GET /decode?crop= exists on the latent player at :7892)
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: Bungee time-stretch + pitch-shift per lane {source_bpm, target_bpm, semitones} — no endpoint visible
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: chroma analysis per clip returning the three octave-band regressor outputs (128 bins each at octaves 1/5/9, widths 1.0/1.5/1.0) plus a 12-class fold — no endpoint visible
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: beat/downbeat detection and native-BPM estimation per clip (the mockup fakes both from an FNV hash of the filename) — no endpoint visible
- DESIGN-IMPLIED: file listing for the FILES pane and a session/render history for 'RENDERED THIS SESSION' — no endpoint visible
- REAL AND USED BY THE DESIGN'S SPIRIT: POST /schedule {steps, duration, dist_shift (float|'flux'|null), sigma_max} -> {sigmas: [steps+1 floats, descending sigma_max→0]}, memoized client-side
- REAL BUT UNSURFACED: POST /longform {prompt-ARC '0:promptA|45:promptB|...', audio_path? (windowed a2a arc loop) or duration (t2a LongFormRenderer)}, 7200 s timeout
- REAL BUT UNSURFACED: POST /bend {crop_id|latent_path, latent_dir?, seed, ops:[{op, amount, ...op keys}]} — latent data-bending then decode
- REAL BUT UNSURFACED: POST /a2a_mix
- REAL BUT UNSURFACED: GET /slots -> resident adapter table + VRAM budget; POST /slots {slots:[{ckpt_path, label?}], activate?} — refuses over the VRAM floor, over max_slots, or on a fullft path
- REAL BUT UNSURFACED: POST /ab {payload, ab.slots:[... , null]} — one payload across resident slots on ONE shared seed, null = bare-base control arm
- REAL BUT UNSURFACED: GET /ckpts {rescan, root, root_ids} -> {root, ckpts:[{path,size,mtime}]}; GET /roots -> {roots:[{id,label,path,available,count}], stale_root_ids}; GET /models {root_ids, family(adapter|fullft|control_adapter|latch_head|unknown), corpus, q, loadable, rescan} -> {count, models:[{id,path,label,family,corpus,loadable,root_id}]}
- REAL BUT ONLY PARTLY SURFACED: GET /presets -> [{...}] newest first; GET /presets/<name> -> {schema,name,notes,created,payload,form?}; POST /presets {name, payload, notes, form?} (seed and batch_size are stripped server-side so a preset cannot pin a sweep's seed axis)
- REAL: GET /status, GET /info (memoized), and BASE + resp['urls'][i] for /audio/<job>/x.wav; RenderError carries the server traceback
- CLIENT-SIDE ONLY (no network): phosphor-border.js — import {createPhosphorBorder, PALETTES}; createPhosphorBorder(canvas, {thickness, supersample, linesPerColour, jitter, persistence, chromaBleed, gain, spotSpread, sweepHz, alphaOut, palette}); fx.start() / fx.set({...}) / fx.stop() / fx.destroy(). Settled config: {sweepStart:95, sweepEnd:0, dur:5, thickness:4, linesPerColour:3, jitter:0.59, persistence:0.002, chromaBleed:1.5, supersample:8, gain:0.8, spotSpread:0.16, palette:'teal'}. PALETTES: teal, amber, accent, sparse, c64

**Behaviors to preserve**

- DRAG-TO-SCALE is the signature interaction and must be ported exactly: _numDrag(value, min, max, onValue, {int}) arms window-level mousemove/mouseup; raw = startVal + (dx/260)*range*gain where gain = 0.01 when shiftKey; decimals dec = clamp(0..4, 3 - floor(log10(|range|))) and +2 more while shift is held; result clamped to [min,max]. A mouseup with |dx| <= 1 calls el.focus() so a click still puts the caret in the field for typing. Only e.button===0 starts it.
- ONE BOTTOM TAB AT A TIME at a constant 248px so the lanes never move vertically. This was an explicit revision (README: 'the earlier stacked layout was too cramped to work in') and the leftover state keys chromaOpen/promptOpen/mixRowOpen/terminalOpen are vestiges of the stacked version.
- THE BOTTOM PANE FOLLOWS THE SELECTION: selecting a clip makes the target CLIP or A2A, selecting an overlap makes it INPAINT, selecting nothing makes it GENERATE, and the target bar restyles with that target's colour. The prototype only demonstrates the switching — see the NOTE FOR THE REAL APP hazard.
- MIDDLE-DRAG IS A COMPOUND GESTURE: vertical zooms (barsVisible = clamp(1..64, round(startBars * 1.012^dy))) and horizontal scrolls in the SAME drag. Shift+wheel scrolls one bar per notch. Plain wheel is deliberately left to the page. Middle-down is intercepted on the ruler and every lane before any other handling.
- MAGNETIC DOWNBEAT SNAP: with snap='lane', a dragged clip's OWN downbeats jump to any other clip's downbeats, but only inside a 5-pixel threshold converted to beats via laneWidthPx; dragging further pulls free again. This is the only snap mode that is pixel-relative rather than musical.
- DOWNBEAT COINCIDENCE GLOW: each lane's downbeats are compared to every other lane's; t = max(0, 1 - distance/(pxPerBeat/4))^0.7 and the marker interpolates from oklch(78% 0.08 250) blue-grey toward oklch(85% 0.17 95) yellow, drawn as a 14px halo + 6px core + 2px line. The window is one 32nd note at the project tempo (README says 'quarter-beat' in the on-screen caption).
- CONSONANCE COLOURING IS PER-FRAME AND COLUMN-WIDE: in the chroma heatmap, cell LIGHTNESS comes from the bin value but HUE comes from the whole frame's match score (60 + (1-m)*200), so a dissonant moment tints its entire column. Match uses a harmonic-overlap model, not pitch proximity: INTERVAL_W = [1.0, 0.10, 0.35, 0.62, 0.70, 0.82, 0.18, 0.90, 0.66, 0.72, 0.30, 0.22] by interval class, so a fifth (0.90) beats a semitone (0.10).
- THE MATCH-CURVE Y AXIS IS ANCHORED TO REFERENCE INTERVALS, NOT THE DATA: unison/fifth/tritone anchors are recomputed from the current target (target vs itself, vs itself rotated 7, vs itself rotated 6) so the same on-screen height means the same thing across clips and sessions. The legend ticks are positioned by those same anchor values.
- DETUNE ROTATES CHROMA FRACTIONALLY: _rotate splits energy between the two neighbouring pitch classes at 100 cents = one class, and the detune scan sweeps ±100¢ in 4¢ steps, sampling every 3rd frame, reporting BOTH mean and sd so HIGHEST optimises mean and STEADIEST optimises mean − sd.
- CFG INTERVAL IS STORED AS PROGRESS (1 − sigma/sigma_max) AND ONLY DISPLAYED AS STEPS. _stepAtProgress walks i=0..n and returns the first step whose progress crosses p. Changing STEPS therefore changes the displayed step numbers without changing the stored value — and the drag handler swaps its own range (0..steps integer vs 0..1 float) depending on the unit.
- MODEL STAGE IS A COMPOUND PRESET, NOT A FLAG: POST sets objective+sampler(pingpong)+steps(8)+shape(logsnr) in one setState; BASE sets objective+sampler(dpmpp-3m-sde)+steps(50)+shape(geometric). In POST the CFG fields are only annotated as inert, never actually disabled.
- STEPPED SCHEDULE QUANTISATION: quant(t) = min(1, (floor(t*p) + tilt*frac(t*p)) / (p-1)) applied to the time axis BEFORE the shape function, so it composes with every shape including logSNR. The sigma curve is then drawn as a stair (lineTo prevY before lineTo y).
- THE SIGMA GRAPH IS A COMPOSITE DIAGNOSTIC: tick DENSITY along the curve shows where steps are being spent; the turquoise band is the CFG-active region; per-LatCH-slot windows get their own stacked 7px lane in the slot colour with white diagonal hatching exactly where they overlap the CFG band; the dotted line is progress; a second dotted amber horizontal is cfgRescale.
- OVERLAPS ARE DERIVED, NEVER CREATED: _overlaps() sorts each lane's clips by start and emits a region for each consecutive intersecting pair, keyed `${a.id}-${b.id}`. Move a clip and the region's identity (and therefore its stored crossfade/steps/cfg) silently changes or disappears.
- THE A2A ENVELOPE IS PER-CLIP BUT DRAWN ON THE MASTER STRIP, and it visibly greys to opacity 0.28 with pointer-events:none when the selected clip's a2a is off, with the hint line switching to 'inactive; turn A2A on for the selected clip'.
- RESPONSIVE LABEL DROPPING BY MEASURED PIXELS: clipPx = (len/beatsVisible)*laneWidthPx; the BPM/stretch label hides below 150px and LOOP below 70px, and the code comments that the chroma score chip must never be the one sliced.
- CANVAS TEXT IS DRAWN THROUGH _textOn(), which scales the context by canvas.width/clientWidth and returns an X() mapper so text is not horizontally stretched on the 1400-wide backing canvases. Any rewrite that skips this gets distorted labels.
- RENDER PROGRESS IS THE C64 RASTER BORDER: sweepHz falls linearly from sweepStart(95) to sweepEnd(0) over dur seconds and stepsLeft = round(total*(1-u)), so the bars visibly slow to a standstill as the last steps land. phosphor-border.js simulates the FULL raster (because the colour register can change mid-line) then masks to a ring, with a one-pole low-pass along the beam and per-channel phosphor decay TAU_R 1.0 / TAU_G 1.6 / TAU_B 0.6 so a white flash trails green.
- HELP MODE IS A GLOBAL HOVER LAYER driven by one onMouseMove on the root that walks e.target.closest('[data-help]'); there are 82 data-help strings and the README says they are final copy to be carried over verbatim. Their house style is: what it is, how to use it, then 'Safe value: X' for the theoretical ones.
- THREE PRESET TIERS ARE IMPLIED BUT ONLY THE SELECT BOXES EXIST: master (top bar), prompt (PROMPT tab target bar), and per-module (LatCH, FiLM, LoRA). None of the five preset selects do anything except store their own string.

**Rewrite hazards**

- NO TRANSPORT AND NO AUDIO ANYWHERE. There is no play/pause/stop, no <audio>, no scrub playback, no output level meter — only a click-to-locate playhead. The user's authoritative workflow ('clips load onto a timeline; latents are decoded to audio on load; THE TIMELINE IS AUDIO; preview/scrub/mix is audio for checking onset/downbeat alignment before committing') has no surface in this design at all. A rewrite that faithfully reproduces the mockup ships a compositor you cannot listen to.
- THE README CONTRADICTS THE CODE ON LANE GESTURES. README's interaction table claims left-drag on a clip = 'scrub, looping' and drag near a clip edge = 'trim'. In the code, clip.onDown is mode:'clip' — it MOVES the clip in time and across lanes. There is no scrub handler and no trim handler anywhere in 2313 lines. Do not treat that table as a spec of implemented behaviour.
- THE README'S DRAG-RANGE TABLE DISAGREES WITH THE CODE IN THREE PLACES: σ min README 0.001–1 vs code 0–2 (help text says 0–2); σ max README 1–100 vs code 0–1000 (help text says 0–1000); rho README 1–15 vs code 0–10. The code and the data-help agree with each other, so the README table is the stale one. The table also omits four live drag ranges: clip detune ±100 (int), clip a2a noise 0–1, tilt 0–1, plateaus 2–24 (int), cfg rescale 0–1.
- SIGMA MODEL vs THE REAL BACKEND. render_client.schedule() posts {steps, duration, dist_shift (float | 'flux' | null), sigma_max (default 1.0)} and gets back a descending sigma_max→0 array of length steps+1 built from the model's own sampling_dist_shift. The design exposes σ MIN/σ MAX/ρ/λ MIN/λ MAX/SHAPE/STEPPED/TILT/PLATEAUS and NO dist_shift control at all, and defaults σ MAX to 100 where the server defaults 1.0. Wiring the design's fields straight through will produce nonsense schedules. The code comments already flag that only 'geometric' is expressible in the backend's three numbers and everything else must be sent as an explicit sigma array.
- FIVE CONTROLS ARE DEAD IN THE MOCKUP and would be silently dropped by a literal port: the master-preset SAVE button (no onClick), '▸ INPAINT OVERLAP' (no onClick — the only per-region commit action), toggleMixRow (mixSummaryStyle is hardcoded display:none), terminal COLLAPSE (terminalOpen hardcoded true), and terminal FULL SCREEN (sets inset:42px absolute but sits inside an overflow:hidden 248px footer).
