- THE 'NOTE FOR THE REAL APP' COMMENT IS A HARD REQUIREMENT, NOT A NICETY: prompt / negPrompt / steps / cfg / cfgLo / cfgHi / cfgRescale / seed / duration / sampler / sigma all live in ONE GLOBAL block, so selecting a different clip re-shows the same numbers. The comment insists these must move onto each clip (clip.render = {...}) and each overlap region, with a session default seeding new clips — otherwise a clip cannot say which prompt produced it, re-rendering one clip silently adopts another's settings, and master presets cannot restore a session faithfully. The per-clip a2a fields (a2a, noise, env) are the shape to follow.
- OVERLAP PARAMS ARE KEYED BY CLIP-PAIR ID (`${a.id}-${b.id}`), so moving either clip out of overlap and back, or inserting a third clip between them, orphans the stored crossfade curve / steps / cfg. There is no stable region identity.
- NOTHING IN THE DESIGN QUANTISES TO LATENT FRAMES. Clip start/len are floats in beats, drops hardcode len:16 beats, MATCH DOWNBEATS writes starts to 3 decimal places, and the ruler merely LABELS frame indices. Given the measured constraint that a sub-frame circular roll moves 212/256 SAME dims, every clip placement implies a re-encode at that exact offset — and the design never says so. The SIGNAL PATH stage list does include 'ENCODE audio → latent' for every used lane, which is the right shape, but nothing warns the user that a repositioned latent is invalid until re-encoded.
- THE MIX NODES ASSUME FOUR ALIGNED, EQUAL-LENGTH LATENTS. Lanes hold clips at arbitrary starts and lengths (and possibly several per lane), yet MIX is presented as a single lerp/slerp t per node over 'lane 1' vs 'lane 2'. There is no definition of what a lane's latent IS when it holds two clips with a gap, and the quad weights are never normalised.
- THE var(--warn, ...) FALLBACK IN flatWarnStyle REFERENCES A TOKEN THAT IS NEVER DEFINED. Either add --warn to the root token block or hardcode oklch(55% 0.14 40).
- RIGHT-PANE WIDTH IS INCONSISTENT: README says 296px, sidePaneStyle says 284px open / 26px collapsed, and fxPanelStyle anchors itself at right:296px — so the FX panel will not sit flush against the pane.
- ALL DATA IS DETERMINISTIC PSEUDO-RANDOM SYNTHESIS keyed on an FNV hash of the FILENAME: waveform contours, 12-class chroma (root = hash%12, always a [0,4,7,10] voicing), the 384/128-bin band expansions, downbeat PHASE (hash%997/997*4 — so 'downbeat alignment' in the mockup is aligning hashes), BPM values, and every statistics chart. None of it survives contact with real audio; in particular the whole MATCH DOWNBEATS feature is demonstrated against fake phases.
- THE DESIGN IGNORES BACKEND CAPABILITY THAT ALREADY EXISTS. render_client.py exposes /longform (prompt-ARC grammar '0:promptA|45:promptB|...', with or without audio_path), /bend (latent data-bending op list), /a2a_mix, /ab (one payload across resident slots on one shared seed, with null = bare-base control arm), /slots (resident adapter set + VRAM budget, 1.7 s A/B switching vs a multi-GB reload), /ckpts, /roots, /models (families: adapter | fullft | control_adapter | latch_head), /presets and /schedule. NONE of these have a surface in the design. Conversely the design's FILES pane, SESSION select and 'RENDERED THIS SESSION' list have no backend at all.
- THE STATISTICS VIEW IS THE ONLY PART LIFTED FROM SOMETHING REAL, and it is a downgrade: the Dash version populated the feature dropdown dynamically from the 1-D fields of each crop's .TIMESERIES.npz and pooled 400 sampled crops; the design hardcodes three feature names and three scalars and renders a 32x32 mock in a box captioned '256×256'.
- 82 data-help strings are declared final copy, but whole surfaces carry none: the entire STATISTICS view, the chroma band buttons, LERP/SLERP buttons, the quad weight sliders, FiLM scale, the LoRA select and scale, MASTER CHAIN head and GAIN, all terminal buttons, the RASTER FX panel, the piano keys, the RND button's neighbours, and the WORKSPACE/STATISTICS/FX top-bar buttons. A rewrite that promises help on every control has to write those.

**Open questions**

- What is a 'lane' as a backend object? The mix nodes take one latent per lane, but a lane can hold zero, one, or many clips at arbitrary starts with gaps and overlaps. Is a lane rendered to a single continuous latent of the project length (silence in the gaps) before mixing, or is MIX only defined over the region where all lanes have content?
- Where does re-encoding happen and at what offset? SIGNAL PATH stage 3 is 'ENCODE audio → latent' for every used lane, which is the right answer given that SAME latents are not translation-invariant — but the design never surfaces re-encode cost, staleness, or the fact that dragging a clip invalidates its latent. Should a moved clip show a 'needs re-encode' state?
- The design has no dist_shift control, yet /schedule's dist_shift (null = model default, 'flux' = length-dependent FluxDistributionShift, float = constant alpha) is the parameter that actually shapes the released model's schedule. Is the σ MIN/MAX/ρ/λ apparatus meant to REPLACE dist_shift, sit alongside it, or is it a leftover from the pre-SA3 k-diffusion backend?
- Is σ MAX really 0–1000 with a default of 100, or is that a v/eps-era range that should read 0–1 for rectified flow? The help text, the code and the README disagree, and /schedule defaults sigma_max=1.0.
- LatCH per-slot hyperparameters: README known-gap #2 says each slot should own its own ρ/μ/γ/mean-iter instead of the current shared set. Does the real guidance implementation actually support per-slot hyperparameters, and does it support two simultaneous heads with independent step windows at all — or is /steer's (crop, head, gain) the true extent?
- Where do LatCH target slider bounds come from at runtime? The help says the head's own feature_stats / p1–p99 metadata; is that metadata actually present in latch_weights/*.pt, and is there an endpoint to read it (GET /models family=latch_head returns entries but the field list is not visible from the client)?
- Should LoRA/DoRA be per-lane at all? The design puts a single LoRA in each lane's chain, but the real backend holds a RESIDENT adapter set globally via /slots with a VRAM budget and near-free switching between resident slots. Per-lane adapters may mean a reload per lane per render.
- Does the backend support multiple masked regions in one inpaint pass? The README asserts it does (binary mask channel concatenated to the 256-dim latent, 257xL, added at every block) and the overlap-region model depends on it, but the visible client has no inpaint op at all — /generate, /a2a_track, /a2a_mix, /decode, /longform, /bend. Which op carries the mask?
- Is 'CHROMA CROSSFADE' (defaulting to ON) a real capability or an aspiration? Crossfading two clips' chroma through an overlap using harmonic-overlap matching has no backend analogue anywhere in the visible contract.
- What produces the chroma shown in the CHROMA tab — the SAME chroma head's three octave-band regressors read off the latent, or an audio-domain chromagram computed locally? The design captions it as the head's output (octaves 1/5/9, widths 1.0/1.5/1.0, 128 bins each) but that requires a model forward pass per frame, which contradicts the interactive hover readout.
- The top bar has a SESSION select and the README additionally promises a CLIP select that does not exist in the markup. Is session/clip history a real persisted store, and does it replace or wrap the /presets recipes the server already keeps?
- Are the four lanes a hard limit? Every mix order, the quad weight row, LANE_META, the statistics buttons and the pipeline stage notes all hardcode four.
- README known-gap #4 wants a RAM/VRAM meter as a 10px bar with two 5px lanes in the top bar; /slots already returns a VRAM budget. Is that the intended data source?
- The design never shows /longform's prompt-ARC ('0:promptA|45:promptB|...') or /bend's op list, both of which are timeline-shaped features that would fit the arrangement view naturally. Were they deliberately scoped out, or simply invisible to the design agent?

---

### plots/explorer_sa3 — the whole Plotly Dash client: render_client.py + player_client.py (wire contract), app.py + callbacks.py (shell/ID wiring), scalar_cache.py, plus every module they pull in (controls.py, inference_tab.py, a2a_tab.py, bend_tab.py, bracket.py, viewer_tab.py, dataset_tab.py, analysis_tab.py, audio_panel.py, sidecar_index.py, latents.py, analysis.py). Read-only; nothing was run.

The Dash app is a thin, stateless HTTP client: it builds JSON payloads from form widgets, POSTs them synchronously to the FastAPI render server (default http://localhost:8056, overridable by SA3_RENDER_BASE env or [render_server] base_url in ../../latent_player_sa3.ini), and renders the returned relative /audio/... URLs into plain <audio controls> tags. Six tabs (Viewer, Dataset, Analysis, Inference, A2A, Latent lab) served from app.py on port 8051; layout is built once at boot from a filesystem scan of ~N sidecar JSONs in latent_dir, so the crop/track universe is fixed for the process lifetime. There are 63 Dash callbacks total (9 in callbacks.py, 2 in analysis_tab, 27 under inference_tab incl. 7 from controls(ns=inf) and 6 from bracket, 19 under a2a_tab incl. 7 from controls(ns=a2a), 6 in bend_tab); every one is server-side Python — there is not a single clientside_callback, no assets/ directory, and no JavaScript anywhere in explorer_sa3. Long renders block the callback thread for up to 3600 s (7200 s for longform and A/B), with a 2 s dcc.Interval badge as the only progress signal and no cancel path. Two contracts are positional and brittle: controls.steering_states(ns) returns exactly 35 States that steering_payload() unpacks by index (the module docstring and UI_BRIEF both still say 23 — they are wrong), and inference_tab._FORM_IDS is 30 ids unpacked by tuple assignment in build_payload(). The claimed "same-playhead audio behavior" does not exist in this app at all; it lives in the older 64-dim plots/explorer/ Web Audio player.

**Controls (185)**

| id | label | kind | payload field | notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sa3-track-dd` | Viewer: Track | dropdown |  | Options filled by the self-trigger idiom Input('sa3-track-dd','id') — fires exactly once per page load. Value = source_track string from the sidecar index. |
| `sa3-crop-dd` | Viewer: Crop (within track) | dropdown |  | Options+value written by _track_pick; auto-selects the first crop by rel_pos so the plots populate. Value = crop id (file stem). |
| `sa3-latent-graph` | Latent [256 x T] heatmap | graph |  | Heatmap of the .npy, x in seconds at 44100/4096 = 10.7666 Hz, dashed vline at content_frames (padding_mask sum). |
| `sa3-ts-dd` | Timeseries feature | dropdown |  | Options = keys of NNNNNN.TIMESERIES.npz; previous selection is preserved if still present. |
| `sa3-ts-graph` | Timeseries plot | graph |  | Line for 1-D fields, transposed heatmap for 2-D (hpcp_ts). |
| `sa3-audio-panel` | Viewer audio panel | div |  | LOAD-BEARING container: holds the two <audio> tags (decode_url + source_url). When player_client.status() is False it renders a launch-command hint instead. |
| `sa3-ds-progress` | Dataset: cache progress text | div |  | 'computing timeseries means… done/total crops' or '<n> features (timeseries-mean cache ready)'. |
| `sa3-ds-interval` | Dataset poll | interval |  | 1000 ms; its callback calls ScalarCache.ensure_started() (loads _ts_scalar_cache.npz or spawns ONE daemon thread) and sets its own disabled=True once state=='ready'. |
| `sa3-ds-x` | Dataset X axis | dropdown |  | Default 'bpm'. Options = bpm/lufs/rel_pos plus mean(<field>) for every 1-D TIMESERIES field once the cache is ready. |
| `sa3-ds-y` | Dataset Y axis | dropdown |  | Default 'lufs'; same option list as sa3-ds-x. |
| `sa3-ds-graph` | Dataset scatter | graph |  | Scattergl over all crops, hover text 'artist — title'. NOTE: there is NO click handler — the UI_BRIEF's 'point-click -> play that crop' is not implemented. |
| `sa3-analysis-go` | Analysis: Recompute | button |  | Drives three callbacks: xcorr figure, sa3-feat-dd fill, and the co-activation picker fill. |
| `sa3-analysis-n` | Analysis sample size | dropdown |  | 400 / 1000 / 0 where 0 means ALL crops. Only read by the xcorr callback; the feature-corr and co-act callbacks hardcode 400. |
| `sa3-xcorr-graph` | 256x256 latent-channel cross-correlation | graph |  |  |
| `sa3-feat-dd` | Feature to correlate against | dropdown |  | Filled from the 1-D fields of the FIRST sampled crop only. |
| `sa3-featcorr-graph` | latent channel <-> feature correlation bars | graph |  |  |
| `sa3-coact-crop` | Co-activation crop | dropdown |  | Filled with the first 4000 crop ids on Recompute. |
| `sa3-coact-feat` | Co-activation feature | dropdown |  | Options come from stats/latent_dim_feature_xcorr.csv (a file two levels up from the package), not from the sidecars. |
| `sa3-coact-graph` | dims x feature over time | graph |  | Two-row shared-x subplot; reads the .npy and .npz directly with np.load, bypassing latents.py. |
| `inf-server-badge` | Inference: render-server badge | div |  | Shows LAUNCH_HINT when /status is unreachable, else 'up · idle\|BUSY — <job_id>' + last 5 log_tail lines. |
| `inf-interval` | Inference badge poll | interval |  | 2000 ms; the ONLY progress signal during a blocking render. |
| `inf-preset-pending` | Preset hand-off store | store |  | LOAD-BEARING: carries the late (per-LatCH-slot) half of a loaded preset between the two halves of the two-phase load; cleared to None on use. |
| `inf-preset-dd` | Saved presets | dropdown |  | Options from GET /presets, label '<name>  ·  <prompt[:60]>'. |
| `inf-preset-load` | Load preset | button |  | Triggers phase 1 (44 early ids). |
| `inf-preset-name` | save as… | input |  | Empty -> status 'type a name in the box first'. |
| `inf-preset-save` | Save preset | button |  | Also an Input of the preset-options callback so the list refreshes after a save. |
| `inf-preset-status` | Preset status text | span |  | Written by two callbacks (the second uses allow_duplicate). |
| `inf-prompt` | Base prompt (persistent) | textarea | prompt | Joined with inf-variation as f'{base}, {variation}' when both non-empty; empty result raises ValueError('error: prompt is empty') before any HTTP. |
| `inf-variation` | Variation | textarea | prompt | Second half of the joined prompt. |
| `inf-negprompt` | Negative prompt | input | negative_prompt | Sent only on /generate; silently dropped on /a2a_track. |
| `inf-root-dd` | Checkpoint roots | dropdown-multi |  | Value = list of root ids -> /ckpts?root_ids=comma,joined. Options carry '— DRIVE NOT MOUNTED' and disabled when unavailable and count==0. |
| `inf-ckpt-dd` | Checkpoint | dropdown | ckpt_path | Value = absolute server-side path. Loses to inf-ckpt-path when that is non-empty. |
| `inf-ckpt-rescan` | Rescan | button |  | Forces /ckpts?rescan=1 and also re-fetches /roots. |
| `inf-ckpt-path` | or path (free text) | input | ckpt_path | Wins over the dropdown; both are .strip()ed. |
| `inf-ckpt-status` | '<n> ckpts · <scope> · STALE (unmounted): …' | span |  |  |
| `inf-model-info` | Model journal box | div |  | GET /models?q=<path>, then exact-path match; renders label/family/corpus/rank/alpha/adapter_type/epoch/step/load_cost_gb plus recipe (dict, one line per key), purpose, verdict, kim_feedback, a file:// link to run_meta.json and a provenance line. |
| `inf-slot-dd` | A/B slots: adapters to hold resident | dropdown-multi | slots[].ckpt_path | Options = /models?family=adapter&loadable=1. Only fills while empty. |
| `inf-slot-apply` | Make resident | button |  | POST /slots with [{ckpt_path: p}]; a RenderError is shown verbatim as 'refused: …' (VRAM floor / max_slots / fullft path). |
| `inf-slot-status` | '<n> resident · <free_gb> GB free · floor … · max … · <sum> GB in slots' | div |  |  |
| `inf-ab-arms` | compare (arms) | checklist | ab.slots | Option values are the STRING 'base' plus str(slot['index']); mapped to None\|int at payload time. Defaults to every arm when the previous selection no longer validates. |
| `inf-ab-go` | Render A/B | button |  | Refuses <2 arms and refuses any op != 'generate' ('A/B is the t2a path only — clear the init audio path'). |
| `inf-ab-status` | A/B status / seed / total_sec | span |  |  |
| `inf-ab-results` | A/B result cells | div |  | One flex cell per arm: label, 'slot <n> · strength <s>', a 250px <audio> of urls[0] only, or the arm's error in red. NOT written to any history store. |
| `inf-duration` | duration (s) | input-number | duration | 1-378, default 47; float()ed. Only sent on /generate. |
| `inf-steps` | steps | input-number | steps | 1-500, default 24. Also an Input of the sigma chart and an allow_duplicate Output of bracket recall. |
| `inf-cfg` | cfg | input-number | cfg_scale | 0-25 step 0.1, default 6.0. Also sigma-chart Input and bracket-recall Output. |
| `inf-seed` | seed | input-number | seed | -1 = server picks. VOLATILE: excluded from preset snapshots; bracket resolves -1 once per run. |
| `inf-batch` | batch | input-number | batch_size | 1-4. VOLATILE (not saved in presets). Only sent on /generate; bracket reads it just for the combo-count label. |
| `inf-apg` | apg | input-number | apg_scale | 0-1 step 0.1, default 1.0 (1.0 = orthogonal-only APG, 0.0 = vanilla CFG). |
| `inf-durpad` | dur pad (s) | input-number | duration_padding_sec | 0-30, default 6.0. /generate only. |
| `inf-dist-shift` | dist shift | input-number | dist_shift | Blank = checkpoint default. resolve_dist_shift_value(): a non-blank number always wins and is sent as a float; the key is OMITTED entirely when the resolution is None. |
| `inf-dist-mode` | dist-shift mode | dropdown | dist_shift | 'default' \| 'flux'. 'flux' sends the literal STRING 'flux' — only when inf-dist-shift is blank. |
| `inf-init-path` | init audio path | input | audio_path | THE OP ROUTER: non-empty (after strip) -> POST /a2a_track, else POST /generate. Also an Input of bracket's combo counter. |
| `inf-init-noise` | init noise | input-number | noise_level | 0.05-0.95, default 0.4. Also an allow_duplicate Output of bracket recall. |
| `inf-noise-ladder` | noise ladder | input | noise_levels | Comma list -> [float]; key omitted when empty. Bad token raises 'bad noise ladder: …'. Mutually exclusive with bracket's nl axis (bracket refuses to start). |
| `inf-cfg-interval` | CFG interval (sigma) | rangeslider | cfg_interval | 0..1 step 0.01, allowCross=False, default [0,1]. NATIVE SIGMA semantics: the DiT gates CFG on cfg_interval[0] <= sigma <= cfg_interval[1]; step indices are annotation only. Value is a 2-list. |
| `inf-sigma-graph` | sigma schedule chart | graph |  | Signature interaction. Redrawn by an 15-Input callback (steps, duration, dist-shift, dist-mode, cfg-interval, cfg + head/start/end of all 3 LatCH slots). Green vrect = the steps whose sigma falls in the CFG interval; gold = LatCH windows (fraction of steps); orange = their overlap. Title carries the data source: 'server /schedule' or 'LINEAR FALLBACK — server down, no dist-shift warp'. |
| `inf-mut-on` | Weight garden: enable (rebuilds model) | checklist | mutate.enabled | Value is a list; 'on' in list gates the whole mutate block. |
| `inf-mut-op` | mutation op | dropdown | mutate.op | shuffle \| drift \| blur \| contrast \| tilt \| life; default shuffle. |
| `inf-mut-amount` | amount (fraction mutated) | slider | mutate.amount | 0.05-1.0 step 0.05, default 0.25. |
| `inf-mut-target` | target | dropdown | mutate.target | attn \| mlp \| norm \| all; default attn. |
| `inf-mut-seed` | mutation seed | input-number | mutate.seed | default 1234. |
| `inf-mut-decay` | decay direction | dropdown | mutate.decay_direction | late \| early; default late. |
| `inf-mut-decay-rate` | decay rate | slider | mutate.decay_rate | 0-1 step 0.1, default 0.5. |
| `inf-mut-quantile` | life quantile | slider | mutate.quantile | 0.05-0.99, default 0.75; server reads it for op=life only but it is ALWAYS sent. |
| `inf-pres-on` | Rhythm preserve: enable | checklist | preserve.enabled | a2a_track only — silently ignored (never added to the payload) on the /generate branch. |
| `inf-pres-head` | scoring head | dropdown | preserve.head | onset_envelope \| onset_envelope_drums \| rms_energy_bass \| rms_drums. Hardcoded list, NOT fed from /info. |
| `inf-pres-k` | K candidates | input-number | preserve.k | 2-16, default 4. |
| `inf-pres-until` | active until (fraction of steps) | slider | preserve.until | 0.1-1.0, default 0.5. |
| `inf-render-btn` | Render | button |  | THE commit action. prevent_initial_call; binds all 65 form states + the history store; blocks the worker thread for up to 3600 s. |
| `inf-status` | Inference status text | pre |  | 'ok · op=… · seed=… · total=…s' + warnings + meta, or the raw ValueError/RenderError string (which can include the server traceback). |
| `inf-result` | Latest render players | div |  | One <audio controls> + filename caption per entry of resp['urls'], zip-padded against resp['files']. |
| `inf-history` | Inference history store | store |  | Browser-side dcc.Store, newest-first, capped at 50 (_HISTORY_CAP). Entry = {ts '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S', op, label=prompt[:60], urls, files, meta}. Lost on page reload. |
| `inf-history-div` | History list | div |  | Fully re-rendered from the store on every change — every <audio> node is recreated, which is why playback position cannot survive. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-head` | LatCH slot head (ns=inf\|a2a, i=1..3) | dropdown | latch[].head | steering_states index i*8+0. Options from /info latch_heads, label '<name> [<family>]'. None/'none'/'' -> the whole slot is dropped from the payload. Also an Input of the sigma chart (inf only) and the trigger of _autofill_defaults. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-kind` | LatCH target kind | dropdown | latch[].kind | index +1. constant \| ramp_up \| ramp_down \| beat_grid (overridden per head by supports_kinds); default 'constant'. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-value` | LatCH target value | input-number | latch[].value | index +2, default -30.0. min/max/step/disabled are rewritten per head from slider_min/slider_max/std_mean; disabled for non-scalar (multi-channel) heads. RAW units — the server standardises. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-gain` | LatCH gain | input-number | latch[].gain | index +3, default 512.0. RELATIVE, not absolute: the server sets rho=mu=slot-1 gain and scales other slots by slot_gain/slot1_gain. Slot 1's is an allow_duplicate Output of bracket recall. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-start` | LatCH window start | input-number | latch[].start_pct | index +4, 0-1 step 0.01, default 0.0. Fraction of STEPS. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-end` | LatCH window end | input-number | latch[].end_pct | index +5, default 0.6. Guidance is weighted toward the clean end, so 0-0.6 delivers <10% of the budget. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-loss` | LatCH loss shaping | dropdown | latch[].loss_type | index +6. '' = head default and the key is OMITTED. Options: '', mse, smooth_l1, l1, huber, bce_logits, cosine, scalar_pooled, chroma_rung1, chroma_rung2. Disabled for heads with supports_loss_select=false. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-wsec` | LatCH w_sec | input-number | latch[].w_sec | index +7. None -> key omitted. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-vlabel` | 'value' label (tooltip carrier) | span |  | DISPLAY ONLY — its title is an Output of _autofill_defaults. MUST NOT enter steering_states (positional contract). |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-llabel` | 'loss' label (tooltip carrier) | span |  | DISPLAY ONLY, same rule. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch{i}-health` | head health badge | span |  | DISPLAY ONLY. children/style/title from /info health, health_reason, readout_source ('not-recorded' appends a warning). |
| `inf-ctl-latch{i}-meter` | head dataset-stats meter | div |  | DISPLAY ONLY but LOAD-BEARING AS A TRIGGER: its 'children' is the Input that starts phase 2 of the preset load, which is what makes preset values land ON TOP of head autofill defaults instead of under them. |
| `{ns}-ctl-film-enable` | FiLM density adapter | checklist | film | steering index 24. Off -> film: null. On -> film {ckpt, value, gain} where ckpt comes from /info film_default.ckpt, falling back to a hardcoded Mantu path resolved at import. |
| `{ns}-ctl-film-value` | FiLM onsets/s | input-number | film.value | index 25, default 4.0. |
| `{ns}-ctl-film-gain` | FiLM gain | input-number | film.gain | index 26, 0-5 step 0.05, default 1.75. |
| `{ns}-ctl-dora-dd` | DoRA adapter | dropdown | dora.name / dora.ckpt_path | index 27. Default 'none' for ns=inf, 'evr1x' for ns=a2a. A value starting with '/' becomes {name: null, ckpt_path: <path>}; a bare name becomes {name: <name>, ckpt_path: null}. 'none' is sent EXPLICITLY as {name:'none'} because an absent dora block means 'op default' (evr1x on /a2a_mix) server-side. Options = 4 legacy registry names + every loadable adapter from /models, with offline-drive entries kept, marked and disabled, sorted last. |
| `{ns}-ctl-dora-strength` | DoRA strength | input-number | dora.strength | index 28, 0-10 step 0.01, default 1.0. Rides along even for name='none' because a top-level ckpt_path folds into the same dict server-side. inf's is an allow_duplicate Output of bracket recall. |
| `{ns}-ctl-dora-imin` | DoRA sigma-interval min | input-number | dora.interval_min | index 29, default 0.0. |
| `{ns}-ctl-dora-imax` | DoRA sigma-interval max | input-number | dora.interval_max | index 30, default 1.0. Sigma-native gating (dit.py:466). |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch-rho` | LatCH rho (guidance step size) | input-number | rho | index 31, TOP-LEVEL key (not inside latch). Blank = auto = slot-1 gain; key omitted when None. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch-mu` | LatCH mu (proximal term) | input-number | mu | index 32, top-level, omitted when blank. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch-gamma` | LatCH gamma (noise before head eval) | input-number | gamma | index 33, default 0.3 so it is ALWAYS sent. gamma>0 makes head evaluation stochastic, so a fixed seed is no longer bit-reproducible. |
| `{ns}-ctl-latch-niter` | LatCH n_iter | input-number | n_iter | index 34, 1-16 default 4, always sent, cast to int. |
| `{ns}-ctl-info-poll` | steering info poller | interval |  | LOAD-BEARING ACROSS MODULES: 5000 ms, max_intervals=-1. Lives in controls.py but is the Input for inf-slot-dd options, inf-slot-status, inf-preset-dd, inf-root-dd, inf-ckpt-dd and both steering pickers. It exists because Input(<id>,'id') fires once and left every picker permanently empty when :8056 was down at page load. Each consumer short-circuits on its own State once filled. |
| `brk-ax-steps` | Bracket axis: steps | input | steps | Comma list of ints; empty = axis inactive. |
| `brk-ax-cfg` | Bracket axis: cfg | input | cfg_scale | Comma list of floats. |
| `brk-ax-nl` | Bracket axis: nl | input | noise_level | a2a_track only; refuses to start if op is generate or if inf-noise-ladder is non-empty. |
| `brk-ax-weight` | Bracket axis: weight | input | dora.strength \| latch[0].gain | Varies FASTEST in the Cartesian product (axis order steps, cfg, nl, weight). |
| `brk-ax-weight-target` | weight axis target | dropdown |  | 'dora_strength' (default) \| 'latch1_gain'. Validated at Start: latch1_gain needs a head in slot 1; dora_strength needs a ckpt_path or a dora name != 'none'. |
| `brk-count` | live combo count | span |  | '<n> combos (a x b x c x d)', plus ' · op a2a_track x batch <n>' or a warning, plus "— that's a long night" above 32. |
| `brk-start` | Start bracket | button |  | Snapshots the whole inference form via form_states()/build_payload(), resolves seed -1 ONCE for the run, writes brk-queue and arms brk-tick. |
| `brk-stop` | Stop | button |  | Drains the queue and disables the ticker; the in-flight POST still finishes and is kept. |
| `brk-status` | '[done/total] last Ns · ETA Ns' / 'done — N combos in Ns' | pre |  | Written by three callbacks via allow_duplicate. |
| `brk-queue` | bracket queue | store |  | {op, base payload, combos:[...], total, weight_target, t0}. Set to None when drained. |
| `brk-results` | bracket results | store |  | Append-only list, NO cap. Entry = {index, label, combo, op, seed, error, urls, files, total_sec, params_echo}. |
| `brk-tick` | bracket ticker | interval |  | 400 ms, starts disabled. ONE render per tick = sequential fan-out with per-item UI updates; the interval only paces the loop, each tick blocks for the full render. |
| `brk-grid` | bracket result grid | div |  | Cards with label, red-bordered error <pre> (first 500 chars), players, a Recall button and a collapsible params_echo JSON dump. |
| `{'type':'brk-recall','index':N}` | Recall -> form | button (pattern-matching id) |  | The only pattern-matching (dict) id in the app. Matched by ALL; the handler reads ctx.triggered_id['index'] and writes steps/cfg/init-noise plus ONE of dora-strength / latch1-gain back into the form, all allow_duplicate. |
| `a2a-server-badge` | A2A render-server badge | span |  | Same /status poll, different markup and colours than the inference badge. |
| `a2a-interval` | A2A badge poll | interval |  | 2000 ms. |
| `a2a-wave-store` | loaded A/B waveform store | store | a_path / b_path | LOAD-BEARING: {'a': {path, dur, bpm, env:[float], env_hz}, 'b': {...}}. The envelope is a 40 Hz block-max computed CLIENT-SIDE in Python with soundfile, and BPM via librosa.beat.beat_track on a 60 s excerpt around the default anchor. The whole float list is shipped to the browser as JSON. |
| `a2a-a-crop-dd` | A source track | dropdown | a_path | Options = unique source_track paths from the sidecar index, label 'artist — title'. Filled by the self-trigger Input(id,'id') idiom. |
| `a2a-a-path` | A free audio path | input | a_path | Wins over the dropdown when non-empty. |
| `a2a-a-load-btn` | Load A | button |  | Shares ONE callback with Load B; the side is picked by ctx.triggered_id. Sets A's anchor to 0.62*dur and its max to dur. |
| `a2a-a-meta` | 'dur Ns · N BPM' | span |  | Also the error channel for an unreadable file ('load failed: …'). |
| `a2a-b-crop-dd` | B source track | dropdown | b_path | Same options list as A. |
| `a2a-b-path` | B free audio path | input | b_path |  |
| `a2a-b-load-btn` | Load B | button |  | Sets B's anchor to min(0.40*dur, dur-seg) and its max to dur-seg (so a full segment always fits). |
| `a2a-b-meta` | B duration/BPM | span |  |  |
| `a2a-a-anchor` | A ends @ (s) | input-number | a_end_sec | No initial value -> None until Load. Two-way bound to its slider through a callback that is BOTH Input and Output on the same prop. |
| `a2a-a-anchor-slider` | A anchor slider | slider | a_end_sec | max is written by the load callback. |
| `a2a-b-anchor` | B enters @ (s) | input-number | b_start_sec | Same two-way sync. |
| `a2a-b-anchor-slider` | B anchor slider | slider | b_start_sec |  |
| `a2a-seg-sec` | segment (s) | input-number | seg_sec | 20-180, default 75. Rescales a2a-trans-range's max and clamps its value. |
| `a2a-snap` | snap to bar grid (BPM from A) | checklist | snap_to_downbeat | Default ['on']. Client-side snap quantises the anchor to bar = 240/BPM_A (4 beats); the same flag is also sent to the server. |
| `a2a-overlay-graph` | A/B envelope overlay | graph |  | Signature interaction. A drawn upward, B mirrored downward, grey vrect + dotted vlines for the transition window, x-axis = composite time 0..seg. Pure GUI, no server call. Dash has no drag, so placement is numeric+slider only. |
| `a2a-trans-range` | Transition window | rangeslider | trans_start_sec / trans_end_sec | 0..seg step 0.5, default [26,49], allowCross=False. |
| `a2a-quantize-bars` | quantize whole bars | checklist | quantize_bars | Default ['on']. |
| `a2a-mode` | Noising mode | radio | mode | sinesweep (default) \| refine \| inpaint. Gates three other controls. |
| `a2a-noise` | noise level | input-number | noise_level | 0.05-0.95, default 0.42. |
| `a2a-seam-size` | seam-inpaint (frames) | dropdown | seam_inpaint | 0 \| 128 \| 256 \| 512; disabled unless mode=='sinesweep' but its value is STILL sent. |
| `a2a-seam-nl` | seam nl | input-number | seam_nl | default 0.35; disabled unless mode=='inpaint', value still sent. |
| `a2a-pure-basis` | pure-basis splice | checklist | pure_basis | Default ['on']; the option itself is marked disabled outside sinesweep, but the value is still sent. |
| `a2a-interp` | interp | dropdown | interp | slerp (default) \| lerp. |
| `a2a-construction` | construction | dropdown | construction | model (default) \| latent_xfade (no model pass) \| audio_xfade (v2 baseline). |
| `a2a-eps-seed` | eps seed (sinesweep clamp) | input-number | eps_seed | default 4242. |
| `a2a-seam-eps-seed` | seam eps seed | input-number | seam_eps_seed | default 2424. |
| `a2a-tempo-match` | tempo match | checklist | tempo_match | Default ['on']. |
| `a2a-tempo-mode` | tempo mode | radio | tempo_mode | ramp (default) \| follow. |
| `a2a-fine-align` | fine align (onset concurrence) | checklist | fine_align | Default ['on']. |
| `a2a-chroma` | chroma morph (harmonic steering) | checklist | chroma_morph | DEFAULT ON. |
| `a2a-chroma-gain` | chroma gain | input-number | chroma_gain | default 2048.0 (the chroma-head scale, vs 512 for the medium heads). |
| `a2a-guid-end` | guidance end pct | input-number | guidance_end_pct | 0-1, default 0.6. |
| `a2a-prompt-region` | region prompt (required) | textarea | prompt_region | Empty -> 'ERROR: region prompt is required' before any HTTP. |
| `a2a-whole-enable` | whole-track a2a pass | checklist | whole_track | Off -> whole_track: null (explicit null, not omitted). |
| `a2a-whole-prompt` | whole-track prompt | textarea | whole_track.prompt |  |
| `a2a-whole-noise` | whole-track nl | input-number | whole_track.noise_level | default 0.4. |
| `a2a-steps` | steps | input-number | steps | Shared by BOTH the a2a_mix render and the longform render. |
| `a2a-cfg` | cfg | input-number | cfg_scale | Shared by both renders. |
| `a2a-seed` | seed | input-number | seed | Shared by both renders; -1 = server picks. |
| `a2a-dist-mode` | dist shift | dropdown | dist_shift | 'default' \| 'flux'. There is NO numeric dist-shift and NO sigma chart / cfg_interval on this tab, contrary to UI_BRIEF. |
| `a2a-arc-schedule` | arc schedule | textarea | schedule | '0:promptA\|45:promptB\|…' steered_longform grammar; a bare string = a single prompt. Required. |
| `a2a-arc-init-path` | longform init audio path | input | audio_path | THE LONGFORM ROUTER: non-empty -> windowed a2a arc loop (adds noise_level); empty -> t2a LongFormRenderer (adds duration). |
| `a2a-arc-noise` | longform nl | input-number | noise_level | default 0.4; only sent on the a2a arc branch. |
| `a2a-arc-duration` | duration (s, t2a) | input-number | duration | 10-600, default 120; only sent on the t2a branch. |
| `a2a-arc-window` | window (s) | input-number | window_sec | 10-120, default 30. |
| `a2a-arc-overlap` | overlap (s) | input-number | overlap_sec | 1-30, default 5. |
| `a2a-arc-xfade` | xfade (s, t2a prompt joins) | input-number | xfade_sec | 0-20, default 4. |
| `a2a-longform-btn` | Longform render | button |  | POST /longform, 7200 s timeout. |
| `a2a-longform-status` | longform status JSON | pre |  | json.dumps of {seed, timings, warnings, meta minus params_echo}, or 'LONGFORM ERROR:\n<e.args[0]>'. |
| `a2a-longform-result` | longform players | div |  |  |
| `a2a-render-btn` | Render transition | button |  | POST /a2a_mix with 32 States + the 35 steering States. |
| `a2a-status` | a2a status JSON | pre |  | Same JSON shape as longform; errors as 'RENDER ERROR:\n<e.args[0]>'. |
| `a2a-result` | a2a players | div |  |  |
| `a2a-history` | a2a history store | store |  | Cap 50, newest-first. Entry = {ts '%H:%M:%S', label 'A_stem → B_stem [mode nl=x]', urls, files, meta, params (the FULL payload — the recall record)}. Written by two callbacks (longform via allow_duplicate). |
| `a2a-history-div` | a2a history list | div |  |  |
| `bend-server-badge` | Latent lab badge | div |  |  |
| `bend-interval` | Latent lab badge poll | interval |  | 2000 ms. |
| `bend-track-dd` | Track | dropdown |  | Self-trigger fill; mirrors the Viewer's browse. |
| `bend-crop-dd` | Crop (within track) | dropdown | crop_id | Auto-selects the first crop by rel_pos. |
| `bend-latent-path` | or latent path (.npy) | input | latent_path | Wins when non-empty; crop_id is then omitted entirely (the two keys are mutually exclusive). |
| `bend-src-panel` | source A/B panel | div |  | Reuses audio_panel.panel(cid, player_client.status()) -> the crop's unbent /decode plus its /source. |
| `bend-op{i}-op` | bend op (i=1..4) | dropdown | ops[].op | channel_swap \| channel_roll \| noise \| quantize \| segment_shuffle \| band_scale. 'splice' is deliberately NOT offered (needs a second latent). Empty row is skipped. |
| `bend-op{i}-amount` | bend amount | input-number | ops[].amount | default 0.25; omitted when None. |
| `bend-op{i}-extra` | extra keys (JSON) | input | ops[].* | Free JSON object merged into the op spec, e.g. {"bits": 6}, {"k": 32, "shift": 64}, {"channels": [1,5,9]}. Bad JSON or a non-object raises 'op row N: …' before any HTTP. |
| `bend-seed` | bend seed (-1 = random) | input-number | seed | default 1234; one seeded RNG drives all rows in order, so (ops, seed) reproduces exactly. |
| `bend-render-btn` | Bend + decode | button |  | POST /bend, 600 s timeout. Refuses when no op row is active or when neither crop nor path is set. |
| `bend-status` | bend status | pre |  | 'ok · seed=… · total=…s' + the ops JSON, or 'BEND ERROR:\n…'. |
| `bend-result` | bend players | div |  |  |
| `bend-history` | bend history store | store |  | Cap 50; entry = {ts, label '<src> [op1+op2 seed=N]', urls, files, meta, params}. |
| `bend-history-div` | bend history list | div |  |  |

**API calls**

- GET {RENDER_BASE}/status — timeout 1.0 s, returns None on any exception. Response used: {busy: bool, job_id: any, log_tail: [str]} (only last 5 log lines shown). Polled every 2 s by inf-interval, a2a-interval, bend-interval.
- GET {RENDER_BASE}/info — timeout 5.0 s, memoized FOREVER in module global _INFO_CACHE after first success (no TTL, no invalidation). Response used: {latch_heads: [{name, family, default_gain, target_kind_default, supports_kinds:[str], supports_scalar_target: bool, supports_loss_select: bool, value_default, slider_min, slider_max, std_mean, std_std, sigma_k, units, out_channels, loss_type, health: 'ok'|'undertrained'|'unstable'|'unknown', health_reason, readout_source}], film_default: {ckpt: str}}
- POST {RENDER_BASE}/{op} via render_client.render(op, payload, timeout=3600.0) — op gated by a client-side whitelist _OPS = {generate, a2a_track, a2a_mix, decode, longform, bend}; unknown op raises RenderError without a request. Non-200 OR a 200 body containing a truthy 'error' key raises RenderError('/{op} failed (HTTP {code}): {error}\n{traceback}'). Transport failure raises RenderError('/{op}: server unreachable ({e})'). Non-JSON body -> body={} and r.text[:2000] in the message.
- POST /generate — payload: prompt (str, required non-empty), negative_prompt (str, '' allowed), duration (float, default 47), batch_size (int 1-4), duration_padding_sec (float, default 6.0), steps (int), cfg_scale (float), seed (int, -1 = server picks), cfg_interval ([float,float] in SIGMA space, default [0,1]), apg_scale (float 0-1), dist_shift (float | 'flux' — KEY OMITTED when null), ckpt_path (str — omitted when empty), mutate ({enabled:true, op, amount, target, seed, decay_rate, decay_direction, quantile} — omitted when unchecked), plus the steering blocks latch/film/dora and top-level rho/mu/gamma/n_iter.
- POST /a2a_track — payload: audio_path (str), prompt, noise_level (float 0.05-0.95, default 0.4), noise_levels ([float], optional ladder), preserve ({enabled:true, head, k, until}, optional), plus the same common+steering keys as /generate. NOTE: duration, batch_size, negative_prompt and duration_padding_sec are NOT sent on this op.
- POST /a2a_mix — payload: a_path, b_path, a_end_sec (float|null), b_start_sec (float|null), seg_sec (float), snap_to_downbeat (bool), tempo_match (bool), tempo_mode ('ramp'|'follow'), fine_align (bool), trans_start_sec, trans_end_sec (float), quantize_bars (bool), mode ('sinesweep'|'refine'|'inpaint'), noise_level (float), seam_inpaint (int in {0,128,256,512}), seam_nl (float), pure_basis (bool), interp ('slerp'|'lerp'), construction ('model'|'latent_xfade'|'audio_xfade'), eps_seed (int, default 4242), seam_eps_seed (int, default 2424), chroma_morph (bool), chroma_gain (float, default 2048), guidance_end_pct (float, default 0.6), prompt_region (str, required), whole_track ({noise_level, prompt} | null), steps, cfg_scale, seed, dist_shift ('flux' ONLY, added only when a2a-dist-mode=='flux'), plus steering blocks.
- POST /longform via render_client.longform(payload, timeout=7200.0) — payload: schedule (str arc grammar '0:promptA|45:promptB|…'), steps, cfg_scale, seed, window_sec, overlap_sec, xfade_sec, then EITHER audio_path + noise_level (windowed a2a arc) OR duration (t2a LongFormRenderer), plus dist_shift ('flux' only) and steering blocks.
- POST /bend via render_client.bend(payload, timeout=600.0) — payload: ops ([{op, amount, ...op-specific extra keys merged from a free JSON field}]), seed (int, -1 = random), latent_dir (str, absolute server-side path taken from the client's ini [server] latent_dir), and exactly one of crop_id (str) or latent_path (str .npy, wins when non-empty).
- POST /ab via render_client.ab(payload, timeout=7200.0) — BYPASSES the _OPS whitelist and render(). Payload is a full /generate payload plus ab: {slots: [null | int]} (null = bare base = control arm). Success requires HTTP<400 AND body.ok truthy; otherwise RenderError(reason|error|detail|'HTTP {code}'). Response used: {ok, seed, timings:{total_sec}, arms:[{label, slot, strength, urls:[rel], error}]}.
- GET /slots — timeout 10.0 s, None on failure. Response used: {slots:[{index:int, label:str, cost_gb:float}], free_gb, vram_floor_gb, max_slots}.
- POST /slots via set_slots(specs, activate=None, timeout=300.0) — body {slots: [{ckpt_path: str, label?: str}], activate?: int}. Requires HTTP<400 AND body.ok; else RenderError(reason|error|detail).
- GET /ckpts — timeout 60.0 s. Query params: rescan (0|1), root (str, optional single-folder override), root_ids (comma-joined str, optional). A 404 body is returned as-is (it means an unmounted removable scan root, not a dead server). Response used: {ok?: bool, root: str, ckpts:[{path, size, mtime, family, corpus, rank, epoch, verdict}], stale_root_ids:[str], error?}.
- GET /roots — timeout 10.0 s. Response used: {ok, roots:[{id, label, path, available: bool, count: int}], stale_root_ids:[str]}.
- GET /models — timeout 60.0 s. Query params: rescan (0|1), root_ids (comma str), family ('adapter'|'fullft'|'control_adapter'|'latch_head'|'unknown'), corpus, q (free-text/path), loadable (0|1). Response used: {ok, count, models:[{id, path, label, family, corpus, loadable, root_id, rank, alpha, adapter_type, epoch, step, load_cost_gb, recipe: dict, purpose, verdict, kim_feedback, run_meta_path, provenance: dict}]}.
- GET /presets — timeout 5.0 s, returns resp.json()['presets'] or None. Each entry used for {name, prompt}.
- GET /presets/{name} — timeout 5.0 s. Response: {schema, name, notes, created, payload, form?}. 'form' is the viewer snapshot ({dash_id: value}); presets written by a CLI have none.
- POST /presets — timeout 10.0 s, body {name, payload, notes, form?}. Server strips seed/batch_size. Failure returns None (silent).
- POST /schedule — timeout 10.0 s, body {steps: int, duration: float, dist_shift: float|'flux'|null, sigma_max: float (always 1.0 from the UI)}. Response {sigmas: [float] length steps+1, descending}. Memoized per (steps, duration, dist_shift, sigma_max) in _SCHED_CACHE, cleared wholesale past 256 entries. None -> caller draws a linear ramp and labels the chart 'LINEAR FALLBACK'.
- GET {RENDER_BASE}{rel} — render_client.audio_url(rel) is plain string concat of BASE + a relative url from resp['urls'] (e.g. /audio/<job>/x.wav); served as the src of an <audio> element, so the browser does the GET, not Python.
- GET {PLAYER_BASE}/status — player_client.status(timeout=0.5) via urllib; returns bool. PLAYER_BASE = SA3_PLAYER_BASE env, else http://localhost:{SA3_PLAYER_PORT or 8056} — i.e. the render server by default, NOT the 7892 in the ini/README.
- GET {PLAYER_BASE}/decode?crop=<id> — WAV, used as an <audio src>.
- GET {PLAYER_BASE}/source?crop=<id> — WAV of the original slice; needs the removable Mantu drive mounted server-side.
- GET {PLAYER_BASE}/mix?crop_a=&crop_b=&t=%.3f&interp=slerp|lerp — builder exists in player_client but NO call site in explorer_sa3 (dead).
- GET {PLAYER_BASE}/steer?crop=&head=&gain= — builder exists but NO call site in explorer_sa3 (dead).
- POST /decode — listed in the _OPS whitelist but never invoked by any UI call site (dead op).

**Behaviors to preserve**

- OP ROUTING IS IMPLICIT, NOT A CONTROL. On the Inference tab a non-empty inf-init-path (after strip) silently switches the destination from POST /generate to POST /a2a_track, and four keys (duration, batch_size, negative_prompt, duration_padding_sec) stop being sent while three others (noise_level, noise_levels, preserve) start. Same trick on the longform panel: a2a-arc-init-path non-empty switches /longform between the windowed-a2a and the t2a branch. A rewrite should probably make this an explicit mode switch, but the wire behaviour must match.
- THE 35-VALUE POSITIONAL STEERING CONTRACT. controls.steering_states(ns) returns exactly 35 dash States in a fixed order (3 slots x 8 fields = 24, then film 3, dora 4, advanced rho/mu/gamma/n_iter 4) and steering_payload() slices them by index (vals[i*8:(i+1)*8], vals[24:27], vals[27:31], vals[31:35]) with a hard length check. Every consumer (inference render, A/B, preset save, bracket start, a2a render, a2a longform) splats the same list. Display-only ids (-vlabel, -llabel, -health, -meter) are deliberately excluded and the code carries an explicit 'NEVER add these' comment.
- THE 30-ID POSITIONAL FORM CONTRACT. inference_tab._FORM_IDS is a 30-tuple unpacked by tuple assignment in build_payload(); form_states() = those 30 + the 35 steering states = 65 States, and four separate callbacks (Render, A/B, preset save, bracket start) splat it. build_payload() is deliberately shared so a preset, a bracket combo and a single render cannot drift.
- TWO-PHASE PRESET LOAD (a deliberate race fix). Setting a LatCH head fires controls._autofill_defaults, which overwrites that slot's gain/kind/value from the head checkpoint. So loading is split: phase 1 writes the 44 'early' ids and stashes the 21 'late' per-slot values in the inf-preset-pending Store; phase 2 is triggered BY inf-ctl-latch{1,2,3}-meter 'children' (an Output of the autofill callback), so Dash's dependency graph guarantees it runs after, and it clears the store so a later manual head change finds nothing to re-apply.
- THE 5-SECOND RETRY POLLER IDIOM. Six pickers (dora options, 3x latch head options, slot options, root options, ckpt options, preset options, slot status) are driven by dcc.Interval {ns}-ctl-info-poll rather than the one-shot Input(<id>,'id') idiom, because with :8056 down at page load the one-shot fired once, returned [], and left the picker permanently empty with no error. Each consumer short-circuits on its own current State, so the steady-state cost is one cheap GET. The DoRA picker instead compares a root-availability signature so it also refreshes when a removable drive is plugged in.
- THE OLD ONE-SHOT IDIOM SURVIVES in exactly four places that read purely local data: sa3-track-dd, a2a-a-crop-dd, a2a-b-crop-dd and bend-track-dd all use Input(<same id>, 'id') as a fire-once-on-load trigger.
- BRACKET IS A CLIENT-DRIVEN SEQUENTIAL QUEUE. Start snapshots the form into a base payload plus a Cartesian product (weight axis varies fastest), resolves a seed of -1 ONCE for the whole run so combos differ only along the axes, and arms a 400 ms dcc.Interval. Each tick pops one combo, POSTs it synchronously, appends the result (errors included, so a failed combo stays visible in the grid) and reports a running ETA from the mean total_sec. Stop just drains the queue — the in-flight POST completes and is kept.
- THE WEIGHT AXIS COPIES NESTED DICTS. bracket._apply_weight rebuilds payload['latch'] as [dict(s) for s in ...] before touching slot 0's gain, and rebuilds payload['dora'] with {**old, 'strength': w} — the shared base payload's inner dicts must never be mutated or every later combo inherits the change.
- DoRA 'none' MUST BE SENT EXPLICITLY. An absent dora block means 'use the op default' server-side, which is evr1x for /a2a_mix — so the client sends {name:'none', strength, interval_min, interval_max} rather than omitting the key. Similarly a dropdown value starting with '/' is sent as ckpt_path with name=null, and a bare name as name with ckpt_path=null.
- dist_shift RESOLUTION HAS THREE STATES. resolve_dist_shift_value(): a non-blank number wins and is sent as a float (constant-alpha override); else mode 'flux' sends the literal string 'flux'; else None and the KEY IS OMITTED so the checkpoint's own sampling_dist_shift applies. The same resolved value is passed to POST /schedule so the sigma chart shows the real warped run schedule.
- cfg_interval IS IN SIGMA SPACE, NOT STEP SPACE. The RangeSlider is 0..1 in sigma; the DiT gates CFG per step on cfg_interval[0] <= sigma <= cfg_interval[1]. The chart derives step indices from the fetched sigma array purely as an annotation. A rewrite that treats the interval as a step fraction will silently change every render.
- LatCH WINDOWS ARE FRACTIONS OF STEPS AND ARE NOT LINEAR IN DELIVERED GUIDANCE. Per-step strength is scaled by (1-sigma)/sum(1-sigma), so the default 0.0-0.6 window delivers under a tenth of the rho/mu budget and the last 20% of steps carry ~62%. The tooltip text encodes this and is arguably part of the product.
- LatCH GAINS ARE RELATIVE. The server sets rho = mu = slot-1 gain and rescales every other slot as slot_gain/slot1_gain, so a gain copied from another tool is a different scale. Leaving rho/mu blank keeps that auto tie; bracketing latch1_gain therefore sweeps rho and mu too.
- A2A ANCHOR TWO-WAY SYNC. Each anchor has a numeric input and a slider bound by ONE callback that is both Input and Output on the same 'value' props; ctx.triggered_id picks the master, the value is optionally snapped to a bar (bar = 240/BPM_A, i.e. 4 beats) and written back to both. Dash breaks the loop because the second pass returns an unchanged value. The load callback also writes those same props with allow_duplicate.
- A2A BPM AND ENVELOPES ARE COMPUTED IN THE CLIENT PROCESS. _load_side reads the file with soundfile, builds a 40 Hz block-max envelope (hop = round(sr/40)) and estimates BPM with librosa.beat.beat_track over a 60 s excerpt centred on the default anchor (0.62*dur for A, 0.40*dur for B). The whole rounded float envelope is JSON-serialised into a browser Store. The server decodes anything via ffmpeg; the GUI only needs what soundfile can read, and an unreadable file surfaces as text in the side's meta span, never as a modal.
- MODE GATING SETS disabled BUT STILL SENDS THE VALUE. a2a-seam-size, a2a-seam-nl and a2a-pure-basis are enabled/disabled by a2a-mode, yet all three are read as State and always included in the /a2a_mix payload.
- CHECKLISTS ARE LISTS, COERCED WITH bool(). Every on/off control is a dcc.Checklist whose value is [] or ['on']; the payload builders use bool(x) or "'on' in x". A rewrite using real booleans must preserve which of the two idioms each site uses (bool([]) is False, so they agree, but 'on' in None would raise).
- HISTORY IS BROWSER-SIDE AND EPHEMERAL. Each of the three render tabs keeps its own dcc.Store list, newest-first, capped at 50; bracket's results store has no cap. Nothing is written to disk by the client, so a page reload loses every list. The server-side recall record is meta.params_echo (echoed merged request), which bracket surfaces per card and a2a/bend stash as entry['params'].
- RESULTS ARE ALWAYS RE-RENDERED WHOLESALE. Every history list is a pure function of its Store, so any new render recreates every <audio> DOM node in the list. Combined with the absence of any JS, that is why no playback state can survive.
- /info IS MEMOIZED FOR THE PROCESS LIFETIME. _INFO_CACHE is set on the first successful GET and never invalidated, so head metadata, slider bounds and the FiLM default ckpt are frozen at first contact; restarting the render server with different heads requires restarting the Dash app.
- /schedule IS MEMOIZED PER ARGUMENT TUPLE (steps, duration, dist_shift, sigma_max) and the whole cache is cleared once it passes 256 entries.
- THE SCALAR CACHE IS A LAZY BACKGROUND THREAD. On the first Dataset-tab poll, ScalarCache.ensure_started() either loads latent_dir/_ts_scalar_cache.npz (only if its stored _ids list matches the scanned index EXACTLY, order included) or spawns exactly one daemon thread that means every 1-D TIMESERIES field per crop, updating a progress counter every 25 crops, saving float32 columns and falling back to in-memory-only if the directory is unwritable. Missing or corrupt sidecars become NaN and those crops silently drop out of the scatter.

**Rewrite hazards**

- THE 'SAME-PLAYHEAD AUDIO BEHAVIOR' DOES NOT EXIST IN THIS APP. UI_BRIEF.md line 60/89/157 states it as a hard constraint ('result players share playhead position when switching clips; re-click stops'), but explorer_sa3 contains zero clientside callbacks, no assets/ directory (verified: no such path), and no JS. Every player is a bare html.Audio(src=..., controls=True) recreated on each store update, so switching clips resets to 0. The behaviour that inspired the claim lives in the OLDER 64-dim viewer at plots/explorer/: app.py registers two app.clientside_callback()s driving plots/explorer/assets/player.js (529 lines) — a Web Audio engine with a {action: play|stop|fade|prefetch, url, loop_start, loop_end, from_hover, continue_auto, vae_fade} command Store, AbortController-cancelled fetches (concurrent /decode calls were exhausting VRAM), crop auto-advance read from response headers, prefetch, and a VAE-blend crossfade via a /crossfade?mode=ab URL. Its 'same' behaviour is `if (_lastUrl === url && _sourceNode) return;` plus stopCurrent() — i.e. re-triggering the playing clip is a no-op, not position-preserving. A rewrite must treat this as a feature to DESIGN, not to port.
- THE DOCUMENTED STEERING CONTRACT SIZE IS WRONG EVERYWHERE. controls.py's module docstring says 'EXACTLY 23 States' and UI_BRIEF repeats '23 states'; the code returns and requires 35 (steering_payload raises ValueError on any other length). Trusting the prose costs a silent index shift across six call sites.
- POSITIONAL SPLATTING IS THE MAIN FOOT-GUN. Inserting a control into _FORM_IDS or steering_states without updating build_payload/steering_payload's slice indices misassigns every later field with no error (both are same-typed numerics in long runs). Any rewrite should move to named payload construction, but must then re-verify each key name against this map.
- PLAYER BASE URL DEFAULTS TO 8056, NOT 7892. player_client.BASE = SA3_PLAYER_BASE or http://localhost:{SA3_PLAYER_PORT or '8056'} — the render server, which holds SAME-L resident as MODEL.model.pretransform. README.md and latent_player_sa3.ini's [server] port = 7892 describe the pre-2026-08-25 standalone player and are stale; the ini's port key is never read by the client. Copying the ini into a rewrite will point decode/source at nothing.
- THE INI IS READ FROM A PATH RELATIVE TO THE PACKAGE. Both app.py and render_client.py resolve Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / 'latent_player_sa3.ini' (repo root, two levels above plots/explorer_sa3). Precedence for the render base: SA3_RENDER_BASE env > [render_server] base_url > http://localhost:8056. app.py reads [server] latent_dir and will KeyError at boot if the ini is missing.
- /ab AND /slots BYPASS THE render() WRAPPER and use a different success rule: HTTP<400 AND a truthy body.ok, with the message taken from reason|error|detail. render() instead treats any 200 with a truthy 'error' key as a failure and appends the server traceback. Two different error contracts on one server.
- BLOCKING SYNCHRONOUS RENDERS WITH NO CANCEL. render() has a 3600 s default timeout (7200 s for /longform and /ab, 600 s for /bend) and runs inside the Dash callback thread. There is no cancel endpoint, no job polling, no dcc.Loading on the render buttons — only a 2 s /status badge showing busy + job_id. Bracket's Stop cannot abort the in-flight POST. A rewrite that keeps the sync model inherits an unkillable UI.
- ERROR TEXT IS RAW SERVER OUTPUT. RenderError messages embed body['traceback'] verbatim and land in a <pre> (bracket truncates to 500 chars, nothing else does). That is deliberate and useful, but it means the client renders untrusted server strings; keep it as text, never as markup.
- a2a's _players USES zip(urls, files) WITHOUT PADDING, unlike inference/bend/bracket which pad files with None. If the server ever returns urls without a matching files list, the A2A tab and its history silently show NO players at all.
- CROSS-MODULE ID DEPENDENCIES. bracket.py binds inf-init-path and inf-batch as Inputs and writes inf-steps / inf-cfg / inf-init-noise / inf-ctl-dora-strength / inf-ctl-latch1-gain as allow_duplicate Outputs; inference_tab binds inf-ctl-info-poll (owned by controls.py) in four callbacks; inference_tab's preset phase 2 binds inf-ctl-latch{i}-meter 'children'. Renaming any of these breaks a different file than the one being edited.
- allow_duplicate CHAINS ON SHARED PROPS. inf-preset-status, brk-status, brk-queue, brk-tick.disabled, a2a-history, a2a-*-anchor(.value) and the five bracket-recall targets each have two or more writers. Dash tolerates this with allow_duplicate; a rewrite in a framework without that escape hatch needs an explicit last-writer-wins or a reducer.
- DISPLAY-ONLY IDS THAT ARE STILL LOAD-BEARING: inf-ctl-latch{i}-meter (trigger for preset phase 2), {ns}-ctl-info-poll (fills six pickers), inf-preset-pending, brk-queue/brk-results/brk-tick, a2a-wave-store, sa3-audio-panel, sa3-ds-interval (self-disabling). Dropping any of them because it 'renders nothing the user reads' breaks a flow.
- THE ONLY PATTERN-MATCHING ID is {'type': 'brk-recall', 'index': N}, matched with ALL and resolved via ctx.triggered_id['index']. Index-based on purpose — never string-matched against labels.
- A CLIENT-SIDE _OPS WHITELIST GATES render(): generate, a2a_track, a2a_mix, decode, longform, bend. 'decode' is in the set but has NO call site, and player_client.mix_url / steer_url are likewise dead code. Do not assume a rewrite must implement them; do confirm with the server author whether /decode-as-POST still exists.
- A HARDCODED LINUX FALLBACK PATH LEAKS INTO PAYLOADS. When /info is unreachable at payload-build time, the FiLM ckpt falls back to <Mantu-or-Mantu1>/sa3_control_runs/onset_Fusion_lr1e-4_randomcrop/riffer_final.pt, resolved by probing the local filesystem at IMPORT time — on a non-Linux client that probe always fails and the payload carries a path that may not exist server-side.
- bend SENDS latent_dir AS AN ABSOLUTE CLIENT-SIDE STRING (str(latent_dir) from the ini) for the server to resolve. Client and server must share a filesystem view; the same is true of every free-text path field (inf-ckpt-path, inf-init-path, a2a-*-path, a2a-arc-init-path, bend-latent-path) — they are all SERVER-side paths typed into the client.
- PRESETS ARE RECIPES, NOT SNAPSHOTS. The server strips seed and batch_size; the client additionally excludes inf-seed and inf-batch from the 'form' viewer snapshot. A preset written by a CLI has no 'form' key and the loader refuses it with an explanatory message rather than half-applying it.
- THE SCALAR CACHE FILE IS ORDER-SENSITIVE. _ts_scalar_cache.npz is discarded whenever the stored _ids list differs from the freshly scanned index in content OR order, which happens on any add/remove in latent_dir — an expensive silent recompute, not an error.
- PORT AND TAB-COUNT DRIFT IN THE DOCS. app.py runs on 8051; UI_BRIEF says 8050. UI_BRIEF also describes a Viewer 2D embedding scatter (the code draws a 256xT heatmap), a Dataset click-to-play (no click handler exists), a sampler-type control and a randomize-seed button (neither exists), a checkpoint strength slider on the Inference tab (strength lives only in the DoRA row), a hovering progress bar (only a 2 s text badge), and sigma-interval + dist-shift controls on the A2A tab (only a2a-dist-mode exists there). Treat the code as authoritative.
- BOOT COST IS O(number of sidecars). app.main() runs sidecar_index.scan_index(latent_dir) synchronously before the server starts, json-parsing every NNNNNN.json that has a sibling .npy and skipping unreadable ones without a warning. The Viewer/A2A/Latent-lab dropdown universes are then fixed for the process lifetime — a new crop needs an app restart.

**Open questions**

- Exact /info JSON schema — only the ~18 keys the client reads are recoverable here (latch_heads[*] fields + film_default.ckpt). Anything else the server emits is invisible from this side.
- Whether the render responses have a stable envelope. The client only ever reads urls, files, seed, timings.total_sec, warnings, meta (and meta.params_echo). Whether 'warnings' is a list or a string, and whether 'files' is guaranteed the same length as 'urls', cannot be determined from call sites — the padding differences between inference/bend (padded) and a2a (zip-truncated) suggest the author was not sure either.
- Whether POST /decode still exists server-side. It is whitelisted in _OPS but never called; the GET /decode?crop= form is what the audio panel actually uses.
- Whether GET /mix and GET /steer are still live on :8056. player_client builds those URLs (with t formatted as %.3f and a slerp|lerp interp) but nothing in explorer_sa3 calls them; the ONNX player at :7893 returns 501 for /steer.
- Is /audio served with Range support? Every result player is a plain <audio src> against BASE + rel, so seeking in a long longform render depends entirely on the server honouring byte ranges.
- What does the server do with a null a_end_sec / b_start_sec on /a2a_mix? The A2A anchors start with no value, so a Render before a successful Load (impossible today only because Load is also what populates a2a-wave-store) would send JSON nulls.
- Exact semantics of the /slots 'activate' field — set_slots accepts it but no UI call site ever passes it.
- Whether the second server (latent_server_sa3.py :7892 / latent_server_onnx.py :7893) is still part of the intended architecture, or fully absorbed into :8056. The client defaults say absorbed; README says separate.
- What 'same-playhead' should actually mean in the new app — position-preserving clip switching, the old Web Audio engine's re-click-is-a-no-op, or a true shared transport across the timeline. The project brief's compositor model (audio timeline, RENDER commits) points at a shared transport, which neither existing implementation provides.
- Whether the A2A tab is supposed to gain the sigma chart / cfg_interval controls (UI_BRIEF claims it already has them). If yes, /a2a_mix presumably accepts cfg_interval and apg_scale too — the client never sends them on that op.

---

## Part 2 - Build plans (4)

### Angle: PARITY-FIRST: nothing that works today gets lost, and the arrangement workspace is built on top of a finished tool rather than instead of one.

The case, concretely. The Dash app is not a prototype — it is six tabs of accumulated, measured knowledge that exists nowhere else. `controls.py`'s `_WINDOW_HELP` encodes a measured fact (first 20% of steps carry 0.2% of the LatCH budget, last 20% carry ~62%) that no one will re-derive. `inference_tab.py`'s removable-drive honesty (offline adapters kept, labelled "— drive offline", `disabled: True`, sorted last, because silently offering 692 unloadable adapters is "the same class of lie") is a design decision with a date on it. The A/B rig's "base (no adapter)" always-first-arm rule exists because "an adapter that sounds better against nothing is not a finding". `bend_tab.py`'s `_TIP_OPS` is the only documentation of the bend op grammar. The design handoff contains none of this — it never saw the Dash app — so a design-led rewrite deletes all of it by omission.

The count is stark. Cross-referencing the handoff against the live app: of ~30 working capabilities, the design has a home for roughly 8. It has NO surface for the checkpoint journal (`GET /roots` `/ckpts` `/models`, the model-info card written to the "three-audience standard"), the A/B resident-slot rig (`GET|POST /slots`, `POST /ab`), the weight garden (`inf-mut-*` → `mutate{}`), rhythm-preserve (`inf-pres-*`), the noise ladder (`inf-noise-ladder`), the bracket fan-out (`bracket.py`, 345 lines, with pattern-matching recall), `/longform`'s prompt-ARC, `/bend` at all, `dist_shift`, `apg_scale`, or the Analysis co-activation view (`analysis_tab.register_coact`). Its MODEL select is three hardcoded strings where the live app has a journal over multiple removable roots.

Where this angle is genuinely weak, stated plainly:

1. Parity work is slow and invisible. M0–M3 ship nothing the user can't already do. There is a real risk that the workspace — the actual reason for the project — is still unstarted six weeks in, and that Kim keeps opening the Dash app because it works. Mitigation: M4 (the audio engine) is deliberately sequenced as a standalone widget testable during M3, so the risky, unproven premise gets probed early even though the workspace UI comes later.

2. Some of what I am calling "parity" is Dash scar tissue. The 35-value positional steering contract, the two-phase preset load raced against `_autofill_defaults` via `Input("inf-ctl-latch{i}-meter","children")`, the 5 s `{ns}-ctl-info-poll` that exists only because `Input(<id>,'id')` fires once — none of these are features. Porting them faithfully would be malpractice. The plan below ports the *payload* and the *behaviour*, and explicitly deletes the mechanism.

3. Two live surfaces are dominated by the new design and should NOT be ported pixel-for-pixel: the A2A overlay canvas (`overlay_figure`, A up / B mirrored, no drag because "Dash has no native drag") and the client-side librosa BPM/envelope path in `a2a_tab._load_side`. I port the a2a_mix *form* (32 payload keys, exactly) so no render becomes unreproducible, and let the timeline replace the placement UI later.

4. Parity-first pushes toward keeping the current synchronous render model, which is the app's worst liability (`render_client.render` timeout 3600 s, `longform` 7200 s, no cancel). I accept that trade for M1–M3 — see architecture: in a browser a 3600 s `fetch` does not block the UI the way it blocks a Dash worker thread, so this is cheaper to defer than it looks — but cancel is a real gap and I list it as backend work.

**Architecture**

RECOMMENDED: TypeScript + React 18 + Vite SPA, rendered by Electron (Chromium fixed at one version), talking HTTP to the unchanged FastAPI render server on :8056. Charts: plotly.js-dist-min for every parity chart. Timeline/chroma/sigma: raw Canvas 2D, as the handoff already does. Audio: Web Audio API (AudioContext + AudioBufferSourceNode scheduling, AudioWorklet for lane gain/envelopes). Build: one codebase, two targets — `vite build` produces a bundle that runs (a) inside Electron and (b) served from the FastAPI server at `/ui` in a plain browser tab, which is the fallback if Electron packaging ever gets in the way.

Process topology (unchanged from today, deliberately):
- render server :8056 — Python/FastAPI, GPU, holds SAME-L resident. Untouched except the additive endpoints listed under backend_changes.
- latent/player endpoints — note `player_client.BASE` already defaults to `http://localhost:8056`, not the 7892 in `latent_player_sa3.ini`; `GET /decode?crop=` and `/source?crop=` were folded onto the render server on 2026-08-25. The README and ini are stale. The new app should read one base URL, resolved as `SA3_RENDER_BASE` env > config > `http://localhost:8056`, and drop the second base entirely.
- Electron main process — window, native menus, and a *tiny* privileged IPC surface (session file read/write, app config). No Node in the renderer. Nothing else.

WHY THIS STACK, against each alternative:

vs. PySide6/Qt — rejected, and it is not close for a parity-first plan. Qt throws away three assets that are already written and already work. (a) Every parity chart in the Dash app is a Plotly figure; `plotly.js` is the *same library* Dash renders, so `analysis.py`'s `dim_xcorr` heatmap, the co-activation `make_subplots(shared_xaxes=True, row_heights=[0.22,0.78])` with its linked zoom, the Scattergl over ~5400 crops, and `_sigma_figure`'s vrect/annotation stack all transfer as figure specs rather than as re-implementations. In Qt that is a pyqtgraph rewrite of six non-trivial views, and pyqtgraph has no equivalent of Plotly's shared-x zoom linking out of the box. (b) The design handoff is 2313 lines of `.dc.html` — React-ish markup, canvas drawing, 82 `data-help` strings the README says to carry over verbatim, plus `phosphor-border.js`, which the map identifies as the one production-quality file in the handoff. In Qt, all of it is reference art. (c) The timeline. A multi-lane, sample-accurate audio transport with per-clip gain envelopes is a solved, declarative problem in Web Audio (`source.start(when, offset)` against `AudioContext.currentTime`) and a from-scratch DSP + ring-buffer + callback-thread project in Qt (QAudioSink gives you a byte sink, not a scheduler; realistically you end up in `sounddevice` + numpy mixing on a Python thread, in the same process as the UI). The one honest Qt win — same language as the backend, native file dialogs — is worth less than it looks, because every path field in the app (`inf-ckpt-path`, `inf-init-path`, `a2a-a-path`, `bend-latent-path`, `bend`'s `latent_dir`) is a SERVER-side path typed into the client. A native OS picker returns the wrong string the moment the client and the render server are not the same machine. That needs a server-side browse endpoint regardless of toolkit.

vs. plain web in a browser — this is the fallback and I keep it working, but it is not the primary. It cannot own a session on disk without inventing a server-side session store (extending a contract we do not control), it has no OS drag-and-drop from a file manager into a lane, and it gives up window/menu control on a tool meant to be a single 1800x900 no-scroll workspace. Keeping the SPA browser-runnable costs almost nothing (one IPC shim with a browser fallback) and buys a debug path plus an escape hatch.

vs. Tauri — the genuinely close call, and the tempting one: ~10 MB vs ~200 MB, faster cold start, and the backend is already remote so there is no Rust sidecar to write. I still recommend Electron, for one reason that is specific to this app: Tauri uses the OS webview, which is WebKitGTK on Arch (Kim's primary target) and WebView2/Chromium on Windows. The load-bearing feature of the whole project — judging onset and downbeat alignment by ear before committing a render — depends on AudioWorklet, precise `AudioContext.currentTime` scheduling, and `OffscreenCanvas` for the lane waveforms, and those are exactly where WebKitGTK and Chromium diverge in behaviour and in bug surface. "The alignment preview sounds subtly wrong on Linux but fine on Windows" is the worst possible bug for this tool and the hardest to attribute. Electron pins one engine on all three platforms, and gives identical DevTools when profiling audio glitches. The mitigation for the size cost: keep the renderer engine-agnostic (no Node globals, all privileged calls behind a preload IPC interface with a browser fallback), so switching to Tauri at v1.0 is a shell swap, not a rewrite. Re-evaluate once the audio engine is proven.

STATE AND THE CONTRACT LAYER (the highest-value single decision):
One TypeScript module, `renderClient.ts`, mirrors `render_client.py` one-to-one, and one pure function per op builds the payload — `buildGeneratePayload`, `buildA2aTrackPayload`, `buildA2aMixPayload`, `buildLongformPayload`, `buildBendPayload`, `buildAbPayload`. Forms are typed objects, never positional tuples: `controls.steering_states()`'s 35-value frozen list and `inference_tab._FORM_IDS`'s 30-tuple both die here, replaced by `SteeringForm` and `RenderForm` interfaces. Because the current code slices by absolute index (`vals[24:27]`, `vals[27:31]`, `vals[31:35]`) with a hard length assert, the slicing and the assert must be deleted together — half a migration mis-maps fields silently.

Every omit-vs-null rule from the live client becomes an explicit, tested branch, because these are semantic, not cosmetic:
- `dist_shift`: key OMITTED when it resolves to None (checkpoint's own `sampling_dist_shift`); the literal string `"flux"`; or a float. `0.0` is a valid override — `resolve_dist_shift_value` tests `not in (None, "")` and `build_payload` tests `is not None`, so a falsy check here changes renders.
- `dora`: `{"name": "none", ...}` must be sent EXPLICITLY. An absent block means "op default" server-side, which is `evr1x` for `/a2a_mix`. A value starting with `/` is `ckpt_path` with `name: null`; a bare name is `name` with `ckpt_path: null`.
- `whole_track`: explicit `null` when off, not omitted.
- `loss_type` omitted when `""`; `w_sec` omitted when None; `rho`/`mu` omitted when blank; `gamma`/`n_iter` ALWAYS sent (defaults 0.3 / 4).
- a2a's disabled-but-still-sent fields: `seam_inpaint`, `seam_nl`, `pure_basis` ship in every payload regardless of `mode`. Omitting them "because they're greyed out" does not reproduce current renders.
Golden-file tests: capture the exact JSON `build_payload` produces for ~15 form configurations, assert the TS builders emit byte-identical objects. This is the only thing standing between a rewrite and a corpus of renders that can no longer be reproduced.

ASYNC AND BLOCKING: keep the synchronous POST for M1–M3. In a browser a long `fetch` occupies zero UI thread; the Dash freeze was a worker-thread artefact, not an inherent one. Model each render as a client-side job object (`pending | running | done | error`) fed by the existing 2 s `GET /status` poll (`busy`, `job_id`, `log_tail`), so the UI is already job-shaped when the server grows real job endpoints. Two things must change client-side on day one regardless: `render_client.info()` is memoized in a module global FOREVER (restarting the server with new LatCH heads requires restarting the client), and `_SCHED_CACHE` keys on `(steps, duration, dist_shift, sigma_max)` with no checkpoint in the key, so switching `inf-ckpt-dd` can leave the sigma chart showing the previous checkpoint's warp. Both get TTLs and a checkpoint-aware key.

**Feature parity**

| capability | in design? | disposition |
|---|---|---|
| t2a render — POST /generate with prompt/negative_prompt/duration/batch_size/duration_padding_sec/steps/cfg_scale/seed/apg_scale (inference_tab.build_payload, empty inf-init-path branch) | partial | PORT. The design's GENERATION module + prompt textareas cover prompt/negprompt/steps/cfg/seed/duration and nothing else — no batch_size, no duration_padding_sec (6 s paper default, trimmed on decode), no apg_scale. Add all three back as first-class fields; apg_scale 1.0 = orthogonal-only APG (Sadat 2024) and is not a cosmetic knob. |
| a2a render — POST /a2a_track, routed by a non-empty inf-init-path, which silently drops duration/batch_size/negative_prompt/duration_padding_sec and adds noise_level/noise_levels/preserve | partial | PORT the wire behaviour exactly, REDESIGN the routing. Today the only cue is the label 'init audio path (non-empty → a2a_track)'; fields belonging to the other branch stay fully enabled and are dropped at payload time. Make the op an explicit segmented control that disables the irrelevant fields, but keep the identical key set per branch. |
| Sigma-schedule chart + CFG interval in SIGMA space (inf-sigma-graph + inf-cfg-interval, fed by POST /schedule) | partial | PORT, and treat this as the signature control. The DiT gates per step on cfg_interval[0] <= sigma <= cfg_interval[1]; step indices are derived annotation only. The design's SIGMA canvas draws a superficially similar picture from a completely different model (σ MIN/MAX/ρ/λ shapes, sigma_max default 100 where /schedule defaults 1.0). Keep the live semantics: sigma authoritative, steps derived, real dist-shift-warped schedule from the server, and the explicit 'LINEAR FALLBACK — server down' title when /schedule is unreachable. Also fix the live silent failure: when no sigma falls inside [lo,hi] the green band is simply not drawn and the user gets no warning that CFG is off for every step. |
| dist_shift override + dist mode (inf-dist-shift numeric, inf-dist-mode default\|flux) | no | PORT. This is the parameter that actually shapes the released model's schedule and the design has no control for it at all. Three states must survive: key omitted (ckpt default), literal 'flux', or a float constant-alpha. Add the bounds the live field lacks (it has no min/max/step, so negatives reach the server) — but confirm with the server author before clamping, since 0.0 is currently a legal override. |
| Checkpoint journal — GET /roots, GET /ckpts?rescan=&root_ids=, GET /models?q=, removable-drive awareness, Rescan button, and the model-info card (recipe/purpose/verdict/kim_feedback/run_meta link) | no | PORT, unchanged in substance. The design offers three hardcoded MODEL strings. The live behaviour is a real feature set: root scoping, '— DRIVE NOT MOUNTED' labels, ' · STALE (unmounted): <ids>' in the status line, a 404 body treated as 'ckpt root unavailable' rather than server-down, and a dict-valued recipe expanded one line per key (the code carries an explicit anti-pattern note about str(dict) putting a 1000-char repr on one line). Fix one live bug while porting: the ckpt picker short-circuits once its options are non-empty, so checkpoints written while the page is open never appear without Rescan. |
| A/B resident-adapter rig — inf-slot-dd, POST /slots, inf-ab-arms, POST /ab | no | PORT. ~1.7 s switching between resident slots vs a multi-GB reload; server refuses over the VRAM floor rather than OOMing and the refusal reason is surfaced verbatim. Keep two invariants: 'base (no adapter)' is always the first arm, and all arms share ONE seed. Replace the string arm values ('base' \| str(index), cast with None if a=='base' else int(a)) with a typed discriminated union. Note /ab and /slots bypass render() and use a different success rule (HTTP<400 AND body.ok, message from reason\|error\|detail) — the TS client must model both error contracts. |
| Steering panel — 3 LatCH slots x 8 fields, FiLM, DoRA, and the advanced rho/mu/gamma/n_iter row, with per-head autofill of 17 properties from GET /info | partial | PORT the contract, REBUILD the mechanism. The design has 2 slots with a WEIGHT slider 0–50; the live default gains are 512 (medium heads) and 2048 (chroma_other), and gains are RELATIVE — the server sets rho = mu = slot-1 gain and rescales every slot as slot_gain/slot1_gain. A UI presenting gain as an absolute 0–50 weight is wrong by three orders of magnitude and changes every render.
